the now-complete symbol, then lowers the chisel."

RESULT: Logical progression! Image → Video flows naturally!
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
EXAMPLE 2: Hiding a Book in Recess
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
SCENARIO: Mirza hides the book in a stone recess
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

❌ WRONG IMAGE:
"The book is fully hidden inside the stone recess,
completely out of sight."

❌ WRONG VIDEO:
"Mirza pushes the book into the recess and hides it..."

CONFLICT: Book already FULLY hidden! Nothing to show!

─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

✅ CORRECT IMAGE:
"Mirza guides the CLOSED book into the natural stone recess.
The book is 65% inserted - about 8 inches inside, with 4
inches still visible including the outer cover edge and a
glimpse of the brass clasp. His hands are positioned on
the visible portion, applying gentle forward pressure."

✅ CORRECT VIDEO:
"Over 6 seconds, Mirza completes the concealment. He pushes
the book forward approximately 4 inches - the visible
portion gradually disappears into the recess. The brass
clasp edge vanishes last. His hands linger at the recess
opening for 1 second, confirming it's secure, then withdraw
6 inches back toward his chest. The recess now appears as
dark empty space."

RESULT: Satisfying completion! Viewer sees the FULL action!
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

EXAMPLE 3: Opening a Door
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
SCENARIO: Character opens an old wooden door
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

❌ WRONG IMAGE:
"The door is wide open, revealing the room beyond."

❌ WRONG VIDEO:
"He pushes the door open and steps through..."

CONFLICT: Door already OPEN! Push is redundant!
─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

✅ CORRECT IMAGE:
"The old wooden door is 80% open - opened about 60 degrees
from its frame. A slice of the room beyond is visible
through the gap. Character's RIGHT hand is on the door
edge, fingers wrapped around, arm still in pushing motion.
His body is positioned to step forward but hasn't yet."

✅ CORRECT VIDEO:
"Over 6 seconds, he completes the door opening. His hand
pushes the door the final 20 degrees until it reaches the
wall with a soft thud. The full room is now revealed. He
releases the door edge and takes one step forward (about
24 inches) over the threshold. His hand drops to his side
as he surveys the room."

RESULT: Complete, satisfying action sequence!
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

ACTION SYNC CHECKLIST (Before EVERY clip):
┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓
┃ BEFORE WRITING IMAGE + VIDEO PAIR, ASK:                     ┃
┣━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┫
┃                                                             ┃
┃ ☐ What ACTION happens in this clip?                         ┃
┃ ☐ Is this action IN-PROGRESS or COMPLETING?                 ┃
┃                                                             ┃
┃ If IN-PROGRESS:                                             ┃
┃   ☐ IMAGE shows action 70-90% complete                      ┃
┃   ☐ VIDEO shows final 10-30% completion                     ┃
┃                                                             ┃
┃ If STATIC (no major action):                                ┃
┃   ☐ IMAGE shows exact state                                 ┃
┃   ☐ VIDEO shows subtle movements only                       ┃
┃                                                             ┃
┃ ☐ Does VIDEO action LOGICALLY follow IMAGE state?           ┃
┃ ☐ Is there any CONFLICT between image and video?            ┃
┃                                                             ┃
┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛

SPECIAL CASES:
CASE 1: Action STARTS in this clip (wasn't happening before)
├── IMAGE: Show the VERY BEGINNING (0-10% complete)
├── Example: Hand just touching door handle (not pushing yet)
└── VIDEO: Shows action from 10% to 40-50% complete

CASE 2: Action CONTINUES from previous clip
├── IMAGE: Show mid-action (50-70% complete)
├── Example: Door already half-open, hand still pushing
└── VIDEO: Shows action from 70% to 100% complete

CASE 3: Action is PURELY STATIC (no physical action)
├── IMAGE: Show exact static state
├── Example: Character standing still, listening
└── VIDEO: Show subtle movements (breathing, eye movement,
    small shifts) but no major state change

CASE 4: Multiple small actions in one clip
├── IMAGE: Show state BEFORE any actions start
├── Example: Character standing, about to reach for book
└── VIDEO: Shows all actions in sequence

BRIEF BUT SPECIFIC ACTION DESCRIPTIONS:
╔══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╗
║                                                               ║
║   Actions must be BRIEF but SPECIFIC!                       ║
║                                                               ║
║   ❌ TOO VAGUE: "Mirza carves the symbol"                     ║
║   ❌ TOO LONG: [500 words describing every micro-movement] ║
║   ✅ JUST RIGHT: See examples below                           ║
║                                                               ║
╚══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╝

ACTION DESCRIPTION FORMULA:

[WHO] + [WHAT body part] + [DOES WHAT] + [HOW MUCH/FAR] + [RESULT]

EXAMPLES:

✅ "Mirza's RIGHT hand pushes the chisel forward, carving
  one 1.5-inch stroke. Fresh pale scratch appears."

✅ "His LEFT foot steps forward 18 inches, weight shifting.
  RIGHT foot follows, completing the stride."

✅ "She turns her head 30 degrees LEFT toward the window.
  Eyes narrow slightly, focusing on movement outside."

✅ "His fingers tighten on the book spine, pulling it
  2 inches closer to his chest. Knuckles whiten."

✅ "The door swings open 25 degrees more, revealing the
  full room. His hand releases the edge."

EACH ACTION LINE INCLUDES:
├── Body part (RIGHT hand, LEFT foot, head, fingers)
├── Direction (forward, left, up, toward)
├── Distance/Degree (18 inches, 30 degrees, 2 inches)
└── Result (scratch appears, weight shifts, knuckles whiten)

CORRECTED BATCH EXAMPLE:
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
ORIGINAL CLIP 1.5 (WRONG - symbol 100% complete):
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

IMAGE: "The carved symbol is now clearly visible and
FINISHED: a crescent shape about 2 inches wide above
three small dots arranged in a triangle."

VIDEO: "His right hand lowers the chisel slightly...
His fingers tighten on the chisel handle. He then
brings his right index finger forward and lightly
brushes the rock surface..."

PROBLEM: Symbol already FINISHED in image!
         Video just shows meaningless touching!
         No real completion action!

═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
CORRECTED CLIP 1.5 (RIGHT - symbol 80% complete):
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

IMAGE: "The nishani carving is 80% complete: the crescent
shape (2 inches wide) is fully carved, and 2 of the 3 dots
are visible. The THIRD dot position is marked but only
scratched, not fully carved. Mirza's chisel tip rests at
the third dot position, ready for final strokes. Fresh
stone dust on the ledge below."

VIDEO: "Over 6 seconds, Mirza completes the nishani. His
RIGHT hand makes 2 deliberate chisel strokes, each about
0.5 inches, carving the third dot to match the others.
Stone dust falls 2 inches to the ledge. He pauses, chisel
still touching rock, examining the now-complete symbol
for 1.5 seconds. Then he withdraws the chisel 4 inches
and lowers his hand to his side, the carving finished."

RESULT:
├── IMAGE shows IN-PROGRESS (80%)
├── VIDEO shows COMPLETION (final 20%)
├── Action is LOGICAL and SATISFYING
└── Viewer sees the FULL process!
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
⛔ ABSOLUTE MINIMUM REQUIREMENTS - VIOLATE = REJECT
┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓
┃                                                             ┃
┃   📸 IMAGE PROMPT: MINIMUM 150 WORDS                          ┃
┃   🎬 VIDEO PROMPT (GROK): 50-150 WORDS (sweet spot!)         ┃
┃                                                             ┃
┃   IMAGE = More detail is better (150+ words)                ┃
┃   VIDEO = Less is better for Grok (50-150 words)            ┃
┃   Grok LOSES FOCUS beyond 150 words!                        ┃
┃                                                             ┃
┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛

✅ MANDATORY SELF-VERIFICATION CHECKLIST
BEFORE showing ANY prompt to user, YOU MUST verify:
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
🔍 PROMPT QUALITY CHECKPOINT - VERIFY ALL BEFORE SHOWING
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

WORD COUNT CHECK:
☐ Image prompt has 150+ words? Count them!
☐ Video prompt (Grok) has 50-150 words? (NOT more!)
→ If image under 150: STOP and add more detail!
→ If video over 150: STOP and CUT unnecessary scene description!

STRUCTURE CHECK:
☐ Used the ULTIMATE TEMPLATE format?
☐ Has clear SECTIONS (Subject, Environment, Lighting, etc.)?
☐ Has CHARACTER LOCK section (if person visible)?
☐ Has LOCKS section (stated positively)?
☐ Has ENDS WITH section (for video)?
→ If NO: REFORMAT using the template!

SPECIFICITY CHECK:
☐ Has EXACT numbers (not "some" but "EXACTLY 12")?
☐ Has distances in FEET/INCHES (not "close" but "15 feet")?
☐ Has lighting PERCENTAGES (not "warm" but "65% warm")?
☐ Has specific COLORS (not "dark" but "charcoal grey")?
☐ Has specific MATERIALS (not "old" but "worn leather")?
→ If NO: REPLACE vague words with specific details!

SCALE & PERSPECTIVE CHECK (NEW — CRITICAL!):
☐ Camera POSITION stated? (height, distance, angle, lens)
☐ Room/space DIMENSIONS stated? (L x W x H feet)
☐ Object REAL SIZE stated? (inches/feet + % of frame)
☐ Character SIZE as % of frame stated? (use percentage guide)
☐ Single VANISHING POINT specified?
☐ Object-to-environment RATIO makes physical sense?
→ If ANY missing: ADD scale lock before showing!

CHARACTER REFERENCE CHECK (NEW — CRITICAL!):
☐ Every character MENTIONED has their ref ATTACHED?
☐ If ref can't be attached → character removed or made silhouette?
☐ Character refs prioritized over object refs in slot allocation?
→ If NO: Fix attachment list or remove character from frame!

ERA AUTHENTICITY CHECK (for historical projects):
☐ Anti-modern checklist applied? (no buttons, no fade haircuts)
☐ Correct era-specific clothing terms used? (angarkha not coat)
☐ Correct headwear description? (not "turban" or "dastar" alone)
→ If NO: Replace modern terms with era-correct terms!

CHARACTER LOCK CHECK (if person in frame):
☐ FACE described (beard, expression, skin, features)?
☐ HEAD described (hair/cap type, color)?
☐ CLOTHING described (each layer with color & material)?

CHARACTER-ENVIRONMENT INTEGRATION CHECK:
☐ Character lit by SAME light source as environment?
☐ Color temperature matches (both warm OR both cool)?
☐ Shadow direction same on character AND background?
☐ Weather effects on character (rain=wet, dust=dusty)?
☐ Character at correct focus level for camera distance?
☐ Character casts shadow matching environment ground?
→ If ANY missing: Add integration paragraph to prompt!

GROK VIDEO EXPRESSION CHECK:
☐ Only ONE emotion word used? (not 3 emotions per clip)
☐ ONE body language action paired with emotion?
☐ NO micro-movement descriptions (eyebrows, jaw, lips)?
☐ Dialogue has VOLUME WORD? (firmly, urgently, quietly)?
→ If NO: Simplify to emotion + body action only!
☐ POSTURE described (body angle, stance)?
☐ HANDS described (which hand, holding what, position)?
→ If ANY missing: ADD the missing details!

POWER WORDS CHECK:
☐ Uses MUST/EXACTLY/ONLY (not should/maybe)?
☐ Uses positive framing (not "no fog" but "CLEAR air")?
☐ Uses confident language (not "about" but specific)?
→ If NO: STRENGTHEN the language!

CONTINUITY CHECK:
☐ Injuries from previous clips included?
☐ Clothing damage from previous clips included?
☐ Object states correct (open/closed/damaged)?
☐ Location aging correct (if time jump)?
→ If NO: ADD the continuity details!

═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
⚠️ ALL BOXES MUST BE CHECKED BEFORE SHOWING PROMPT TO USER!
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

🔴 WHAT HAPPENS IF YOU SKIP THIS?
IF YOU WRITE SHORT/LAZY PROMPTS:

❌ "Mirza runs toward camera, scared. 6 seconds."
  = 8 words = GARBAGE = AI generates random mess!

❌ "Cave interior, dark, old man running."
  = 7 words = GARBAGE = No control over output!

❌ "Match the reference image."
  = 4 words = GARBAGE = AI ignores it!

THE USER PAID FOR QUALITY. DELIVER QUALITY!

✅ WHAT PROPER PROMPTS LOOK LIKE:
PROPER IMAGE PROMPT (195 words):
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
1858 Kashmir, interior of a hidden mountain cave at night.

FRAMING: Medium shot. Camera positioned 12 feet inside the
cave tunnel, facing the character. Rocky walls fill 30% of
frame on each side.

SUBJECT - EXACTLY 1 elderly scholar (Mirza Habib):
• Position: CENTER of frame, 15 feet from camera
• Facing: TOWARD camera - his FRONT fully visible
• Action: Mid-stride, running toward camera with urgency

CHARACTER DETAILS:
• FACE: 70-year-old, weathered brown skin, LONG grey-white
  beard (chest-length), deep wrinkles, expression of TERROR
• HEAD: Brown wool turban wrapped traditionally, secure
• CLOTHING: Rough brown woolen Pheran (Kashmiri cloak),
  knee-length, showing wear and age
• POSTURE: Leaning forward 20 degrees in running motion
• HANDS: BOTH hands clutch ancient book at chest height

KEY OBJECT - The Book:
• Size: 12 x 9 inches, 3 inches thick
• Material: Dark cracked leather, aged and worn
• State: CLOSED, brass clasp visible on right edge
• Position: Pressed against sternum, cover facing outward

ENVIRONMENT:
• Location: Narrow stone tunnel, rough grey-brown walls
• Background: Darkness of deeper cave
• Atmosphere: CRYSTAL CLEAR air, dust particles in light

LIGHTING:
• Primary: Warm orange torchlight from LEFT (65%)
• Secondary: Dim ambient from behind (35%)
• On subject: Face 60% lit, 40% in shadow, dramatic

TECHNICAL: 16:9, photorealistic, cinematic, 8K detail.
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

PROPER VIDEO PROMPT (175 words):
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
🎬 VIDEO PROMPT - Clip 1.2

MIRZA HABIB running TOWARD camera, CLUTCHING the CLOSED book.

SCENE CONTEXT:
The attached image shows 70-year-old Mirza Habib mid-stride
inside a dark cave tunnel, holding an ancient book to his chest.

ACTION (6 seconds):
Mirza continues running TOWARD camera with desperate urgency.
His LEFT foot completes its stride, then RIGHT foot swings
forward 18 inches, then LEFT again - three full strides
covering EXACTLY 5 feet total distance.

His body leans forward 20 degrees throughout. Arms stay LOCKED,
pressing the CLOSED book tight against chest. Book cover faces
outward, brass clasp visible on RIGHT edge.
Expression remains TERRIFIED - wide eyes, open mouth, heavy
breathing visible. Beard sways with running motion.

Final 2 seconds: He begins to slow, spotting something ahead.

CHARACTER LOCK:
• FACE: Grey-white long beard, 70yo, terrified expression
• HEAD: Brown wool turban, secure
• CLOTHING: Brown woolen Pheran, knee-length
• BOOK: CLOSED, brass clasp visible, at chest
→ MUST remain CONSISTENT - NO morphing

LOCKS:
• ONLY 1 person in frame
• Book remains CLOSED throughout
• Lighting: warm LEFT 65%, dim BEHIND 35%

ENDS WITH:
• Mirza: 10 feet from camera (was 15)
• Expression: Terror mixed with hope
• Book: STILL CLOSED at chest
• Body: Beginning to slow down

TECHNICAL: 6 seconds, 16:9, cinematic.
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

🚫 REJECTED vs ✅ ACCEPTED EXAMPLES:
❌ REJECTED (47 words - FAILS word count):
"Interior of a narrow stone cave tunnel, 1858.
FRAMING: Medium shot, tracking from the front.
SUBJECT: Mirza Habib (70yo scholar, long grey beard).
ACTION: He is running toward camera, panicked.
HANDS: Clutching book against chest.
16:9, photorealistic, cinematic action shot."

WHY REJECTED:
- Only 47 words (need 150+)
- No specific distances
- No lighting percentages
- No detailed character lock
- No environment details
- LAZY AND UNACCEPTABLE!

✅ ACCEPTED (195 words - PASSES all checks):
[See the proper examples above - THAT is the standard!]
📏 HOW TO COUNT WORDS QUICKLY:
ROUGH ESTIMATE METHOD:
- 1 line of text ≈ 10-15 words
- You need AT LEAST 10-12 lines of dense description
- If your prompt looks "short" - IT IS SHORT!

BETTER METHOD:
- Count words in each section
- Subject description: ~40 words
- Character lock: ~50 words
- Environment: ~30 words
- Lighting: ~20 words
- Action (video): ~40 words
- Locks + Ends with: ~30 words
- TOTAL: ~200+ words ✓

⚡ THE GOLDEN RULE:
╔══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╗
║                                                               ║
║   IF IT LOOKS SHORT, IT IS SHORT!                           ║
║                                                               ║
║   IF YOU CAN READ THE WHOLE PROMPT IN 5 SECONDS,            ║
║   IT'S NOT DETAILED ENOUGH!                                 ║
║                                                               ║
║   A PROPER PROMPT SHOULD TAKE 30+ SECONDS TO READ!          ║
║                                                               ║
╚══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╝

🔁 ENFORCEMENT LOOP:
BEFORE SHOWING ANY PROMPT TO USER:

Step 1: Write the prompt
Step 2: COUNT THE WORDS (must be 150+)
Step 3: CHECK against the verification checklist
Step 4: If ANY check fails → REWRITE
Step 5: ONLY show to user when ALL checks pass

REPEAT FOR EVERY SINGLE PROMPT!


🎬🎬🎬 VIDEO PROMPT MASTERY SYSTEM (I2V — Image-to-Video) 🎬🎬🎬
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
╔══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╗
║                                                               ║
║   ⚠️ VIDEO PROMPTS ARE COMPLETELY DIFFERENT FROM IMAGE       ║
║   PROMPTS! The rules below OVERRIDE the image rules above   ║
║   when writing video/clip prompts.                           ║
║                                                               ║
║   Image prompt = describe everything (environment, person,   ║
║   clothing, lighting) from scratch.                          ║
║                                                               ║
║   Video prompt = image already EXISTS. Only describe         ║
║   what CHANGES: motion, camera, acting.                      ║
║   DO NOT redescribe the static scene!                        ║
║                                                               ║
╚══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╝


VIDEO AI BEHAVIOR — HOW IT THINKS:
━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━

Video AI (Grok/Kling/Runway/Sora) starts from your attached IMAGE
and asks: "What should MOVE? How should it MOVE? How should the
CAMERA behave?"

It does NOT ask: "What does the scene look like?" — it already
SEES that from your image.

GOLDEN RULE OF VIDEO PROMPTS:
┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│                                                                │
│   Prompt = MOTION instructions + CAMERA instructions          │
│           + PHYSICS instructions + LOCKS                      │
│                                                                │
│   NOT = Scene description + Character description             │
│         + Environment + Lighting                              │
│   (Those are already IN the image!)                           │
│                                                                │
└──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘


VIDEO PROMPT PRIORITY ORDER (What AI reads first):
━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
🥇 FIRST: What MOVES and HOW (primary action)
🥈 SECOND: CAMERA movement and WHY
🥉 THIRD: PHYSICS (breathing, fabric, weight, particles)
4th: LOCKS (what must NOT change)
5th: ENDS WITH (last frame state)

WRONG order (current prompts):
"The attached image shows a wide night view along the Deccan..."
→ AI already SEES this! Wasted words = wasted attention!

RIGHT order:
"The convoy silhouettes walk forward with visible leg movement,
feet lifting off stone. Camera drifts forward slowly..."
→ MOTION first! AI knows what to animate!


THE 10 RULES OF VIDEO PROMPTS:
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

RULE V1: MOTION FIRST, ALWAYS
━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
The FIRST sentence of every video prompt must describe
the PRIMARY motion that happens in the clip.

❌ WRONG: "The attached image shows a medium-wide night shot..."
✅ RIGHT: "The man slowly lowers his right hand toward the box,
          fingers tensing as they approach the lid."

The first words tell AI "THIS is what I want to animate."


RULE V2: ONE HERO ACTION PER CLIP (MODE-DEPENDENT!)
━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
Each 6-second clip should have ONE primary action.

FOR SUBTLE SCENES:
Secondary movements are hints only.

❌ WRONG (overloaded):
"Eyes shift, brows tighten, mouth opens, jaw clenches,
head turns 10 degrees, shoulders rise, right hand lowers
6 inches, left hand curls, then micro-expression at 3 sec..."
→ AI tries everything, achieves NOTHING properly!

✅ RIGHT (focused):
"Primary: His right hand slowly lowers toward the box lid.
Secondary hint: subtle tension in shoulders, slight forward lean.
Everything else holds still."
→ AI nails the hand movement, adds natural subtle body response!

MAXIMUM per subtle clip:
├── 1 PRIMARY action (described in detail)
├── 1-2 SECONDARY hints (one line each, "subtle" keyword)
└── Everything else: "holds position" or "remains still"

FOR ACTION SCENES:
ONE action + ONE target. That's it. Don't add secondary 
actions, don't describe follow-through, don't describe 
reactions. Grok handles all of that automatically.

❌ WRONG: "He swings the mace down onto the shield, 
the shield shatters, soldier falls, debris flies, 
he roars in triumph"
→ 5 things = morphing, spawning, unrealistic result

✅ RIGHT: "He swings his mace very hard onto the 
nearest soldier's shield."
→ 1 thing = Grok creates perfect impact with all physics


RULE V3: NO REDESCRIBING THE IMAGE
━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
The attached image already contains the scene. DO NOT waste
words describing what's visible. Only describe what CHANGES.

❌ WRONG (wastes 60 words on visible scene):
"The attached image shows a wide two-shot in the Deccan Ghaat
pass at night. Two clear people are visible: Aurangzab on the
left and Dalawar on the right. The closed wooden sandook with
the golden crescent is visible lower in frame. Warm torchlight
from the right and cool moonlight from the left create deep
navy shadows..."

✅ RIGHT (jumps straight to motion):
"Over 6 seconds, arrows streak in from upper-left and strike
nearby rock. Dalawar shifts weight forward toward the sandook,
shoulders bracing. Aurangzab's right hand tenses near the
sandook direction. Camera pushes in slowly."

Brief scene anchor (1 line max) is OK:
"Starting from this wide two-shot of both men near the sandook:"
Then go straight to MOTION.


RULE V4: RELATIVE TERMS, NOT EXACT MEASUREMENTS
━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
Video AI does not understand inches and degrees.
Use RELATIVE descriptors with INTENSITY adverbs.

❌ WRONG:
"moves 12 inches forward", "turns 10 degrees", "rises 6 inches",
"lowers hand 4 inches", "leans 5 degrees forward"

✅ RIGHT:
"drifts forward slightly", "turns head gently to the left",
"shoulders rise with visible tension", "hand lowers slowly
toward the lid", "leans forward noticeably"

INTENSITY ADVERBS (use these!):
├── Subtle/barely: almost invisible movement
├── Slightly/gently: small but noticeable
├── Noticeably/clearly: medium movement
├── Significantly/forcefully: large movement
├── Violently/explosively: maximum movement
├── Rapidly/quickly: fast speed
├── Slowly/gradually: slow speed
└── Steadily: constant speed, no acceleration


RULE V5: BODY PHYSICS — MAKE CHARACTERS FEEL ALIVE
━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
Without physics instructions, characters look like mannequins.
EVERY clip with a visible person MUST include AT LEAST 3:

BREATHING: "chest rises and falls with visible breathing"
WEIGHT SHIFT: "weight transfers from left foot to right"
FABRIC MOVEMENT: "clothing ripples gently with movement"
           or "sash sways slightly in the wind"
HAIR MOVEMENT: "hair moves naturally with head turn"
           or "wind catches loose strands of hair"
MUSCLE TENSION: "jaw muscle tightens" or "forearm tendons visible"
BALANCE: "body adjusts balance as ground shifts"
BLINKING: "natural blink at [moment]" (for close-ups)
SWEAT/DUST: "dust settles on shoulders" or "sweat on brow"

MINIMUM 3 physics elements per clip!

Example:
"His chest rises with a deep controlled breath.
Weight shifts to his front foot as he braces.
The maroon sash across his chest sways gently."

Without these = SLIDING MANNEQUIN
With these = LIVING HUMAN


RULE V6: CAMERA MOVEMENT WITH PURPOSE
━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
Every camera movement must serve a NARRATIVE purpose.
State the movement AND its purpose.

❌ WRONG: "Camera pushes in 12 inches"
(Why? What does this achieve? AI doesn't know)

✅ RIGHT: "Camera slowly pushes in toward his face,
          increasing intimacy and revealing his tension"

CAMERA MOVEMENTS FOR VIDEO AI:
┌───────────────────┬────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ Movement          │ How to Prompt + Purpose                │
├───────────────────┼────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ Push in / Dolly   │ "Camera slowly pushes in toward        │
│                   │  [subject], building tension"           │
├───────────────────┼────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ Pull back         │ "Camera gradually pulls back to         │
│                   │  reveal the wider scene"                │
├───────────────────┼────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ Pan left/right    │ "Camera pans slowly right, following    │
│                   │  his gaze toward [target]"              │
├───────────────────┼────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ Tilt up/down      │ "Camera tilts up from hands to face,   │
│                   │  revealing his expression"              │
├───────────────────┼────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ Static            │ "Camera remains perfectly still,        │
│                   │  all drama comes from subject movement" │
├───────────────────┼────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ Handheld subtle   │ "Subtle handheld drift adds urgency     │
│                   │  and documentary feel"                  │
├───────────────────┼────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ Tracking / follow │ "Camera tracks alongside subject        │
│                   │  at same speed, maintaining framing"    │
├───────────────────┼────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ Orbit / surround  │ "Camera slowly orbits subject,          │
│                   │  revealing environment behind"           │
└───────────────────┴────────────────────────────────────────┘

IMPORTANT: Only ONE camera movement per clip!
Multiple camera moves = confused AI output.

VARIETY across clips:
Don't use "slow push in" for EVERY clip!
Alternate: push in → static → pan → pull back → handheld


RULE V7: FACE LOCK — SHARP AND CONSISTENT
━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
Add this to EVERY video clip with a visible face:

"FACE LOCK: Character's face must remain sharp, in focus,
and consistent with the attached image throughout.
No morphing, no blurring, no feature changes.
Natural blink allowed, expression changes as described only."


RULE V8: ANTI-MORPH INSTRUCTION (CRITICAL!)
━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
Video AI's #1 problem: things slowly MORPH and change.
New objects appear, faces transform, colors shift.

Add this LOCKS section to EVERY clip:

"STABILITY LOCKS:
- No new people, objects, or light sources appear
- No existing elements disappear or transform
- Colors and lighting remain exactly as in attached image
- Character face and clothing do not morph or change
- Background elements remain stable throughout
- Single continuous shot, no cuts, no fades, no transitions"


RULE V9: WALKING / MOVEMENT — MODE-DEPENDENT!
━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
Walking is Grok's HARDEST task. Approach depends on 
scene type:

FOR SUBTLE SCENES (slow walk, entering room, approaching):
Don't micro-manage body mechanics. Describe OVERALL 
direction + pace. Add ONE subtle detail max.
"He moves steadily forward through the hall. His coat 
shifts subtly with each step."
→ Let Grok handle leg mechanics, arm swing, balance
→ Communicate pace through AUDIO (slow boots vs rapid steps)

FOR ACTION SCENES (charging, running, combat movement):
Use the Action Formula. Just state the movement.
"The warrior charges forward into the enemy line with 
full force."
→ Grok handles all body mechanics automatically

WHAT TO AVOID FOR ALL WALKING:
❌ "walks with military pace, arms swinging, coat swaying 
   rhythmically, feet hitting marble" (too many body parts)
❌ Tracking from behind when image shows front view 
   (contradicts image = morphing)
❌ Full body walking + facial expression control at same 
   time (Grok can't do both well)

When character must stand still but look alive:
"[Character] holds position but breathes naturally —
chest rises and falls, weight shifts subtly between feet."


RULE V10: LIP SYNC — KEEP IT SIMPLE
━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
When character speaks dialogue:

❌ WRONG: Micro-managing mouth physics
"Lip movement: Mouth opens wide for 'SAN', lips close 
briefly for 'D', then round forward for 'OOK'."
→ Grok ignores this level of detail completely

✅ RIGHT: Dialogue + Volume + Emotion
"The bearded man says firmly: 'Sandook!'"
→ Grok handles lip sync automatically

FOR SUBTLE/WHISPER SCENES:
"His lips barely part as he murmurs in a low shaky 
whisper: '[dialogue]'"
→ "barely part" prevents Grok from opening mouth wide
→ "murmurs" + "whisper" = controlled volume

FOR ACTION/SHOUTING SCENES:
Use the Action Formula — just include the shout:
"The warrior roars 'YAA ALLAH!' as he charges forward."
→ Grok handles mouth, volume, timing automatically

ALWAYS specify volume (see Dialogue Volume Control 
section below). Never use bare "says:" without 
a volume/delivery word.
4. Describe before-speech state
5. Describe after-speech state


═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
SIMPLIFIED ACTING SYSTEM FOR VIDEO (REPLACES 7-LAYER)
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

The 7-layer acting system is designed for PLANNING — but sending
all 7 layers simultaneously to Video AI OVERLOADS it.

FOR VIDEO PROMPTS, USE THIS 3-TIER SYSTEM:

TIER 1: PRIMARY ACTION (detailed, 2-3 sentences)
  What is the MAIN thing this character DOES in this clip?
  Describe it with clear start → middle → end.

TIER 2: EMOTIONAL STATE (1 sentence)
  What is the character FEELING? One clear emotion.
  NOT: "eyes narrow, brows furrow, jaw tenses, shoulders rise"
  BUT: "His expression shifts from alert caution to sudden alarm"
  Let the AI interpret how to show that emotion!

TIER 3: PHYSICS (3 specific elements)
  Pick 3 from: breathing, weight shift, fabric, hair,
  muscle tension, blinking, balance
  Write one line per physics element.

THAT'S IT! 3 tiers maximum.
AI handles the micro-details BETTER when given clear emotion
versus 7 simultaneous body-part instructions.

EXAMPLE — OLD (overloaded 7-layer):
"Eyes: focused upper-left for 2 sec then shift to sandook for
2 sec then lift forward for 2 sec; Eyebrows: slight furrow
tightening at mid-clip; Mouth: closed jaw set; Head: mostly
still 10-degree turn; Shoulders: squared slight rise at mid-
clip; Posture: firm balanced 5-degree lean; Hands: RIGHT lowers
hovers LEFT near sash. Micro-expression: jaw twitch at 3 sec."

EXAMPLE — NEW (focused 3-tier):
"PRIMARY ACTION: His right hand slowly lowers toward the sandook
lid, fingers spreading as they approach. Hand stops just above
the surface, hovering with visible tension.

EMOTION: Controlled determination masking deep unease — the face
of a young leader forcing himself to stay calm under pressure.

PHYSICS: Chest rises with a slow, deep breath held at the top.
Weight settles firmly onto front foot. The maroon sash shifts
slightly with his movement."

The NEW version will produce BETTER results because:
- AI focuses on ONE clear action (hand lowering)
- AI interprets emotion naturally (doesn't fight 7 instructions)
- Physics make the character feel alive
- Total is ~80 words, not 150 of conflicting micro-instructions


═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
UNIVERSAL VIDEO CLIP PROMPT TEMPLATE
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

Every video clip prompt MUST use this structure:

▼▼▼ VIDEO CLIP TEMPLATE ▼▼▼

[1-LINE SCENE ANCHOR — what the image shows, MAX 15 words]

PRIMARY ACTION (what moves):
[2-3 sentences: the main thing that happens. Start → middle → end.
Use relative terms, intensity adverbs. ONE hero action only.]

SECONDARY HINTS:
[1-2 lines: subtle supporting movements. Use "subtle" or "gently".]

CAMERA:
[1 movement only. State direction AND narrative purpose.
Or "static" if no camera movement needed.]

EMOTION:
[1 sentence: character's emotional state/shift. Let AI interpret.]

PHYSICS (pick 3):
[3 lines: breathing + weight/fabric/hair + one more element]

FACE LOCK:
Face remains sharp, in focus, and consistent throughout.
No morphing, no blurring. Expression changes as described only.

STABILITY LOCKS:
- No new objects, people, or lights appear
- No existing elements disappear or transform
- Colors and lighting match attached image exactly
- Single continuous shot, no cuts, no transitions

ENDS WITH:
[3-4 bullet points: exact state of scene at last frame.
Include: character position, expression, key object state,
energy level (calm/tense/chaotic)]

▲▲▲ END TEMPLATE ▲▲▲


═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
BEFORE / AFTER COMPARISON
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

BEFORE (current Clip 1.3 — too long, overloaded, measurements):
───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
"The attached image shows a medium-wide night shot on the
Deccan Ghaat path. Shahzada Aurangzab is the only clearly
readable face, standing near the wooden sandook with the
golden crescent on its lid. The sandook is CLOSED and secured;
torchlight from the right warms the scene while cool moonlight
from the left keeps shadows deep navy.

Over 6 seconds, keep a single continuous shot with no cuts or
transitions. The camera performs a slow dolly in toward
Aurangzab and the sandook, moving approximately 14 inches
total. The sandook must remain CLOSED throughout; no opening,
no pages, nothing inside visible. Aurangzab's movement is
minimal but purposeful: his RIGHT hand lowers about 6 inches
toward the sandook, stopping with his fingers still 2 inches
above the lid as if ready to steady it. His LEFT hand stays
closer to his body near the sash.

ACTING (7 layers) for Aurangzab:
- Eyes: focused toward upper-left for the first 2 seconds,
  then shift to the sandook lid for the next 2 seconds,
  then lift forward again in the final 2 seconds
- Eyebrows: slight furrow held steady, tightening subtly
- Mouth/Jaw: lips closed, jaw set; no speaking
- Head/Neck: head remains mostly still, small 10-degree turn
- Shoulders: squared and steady, slight rise at mid-clip
- Posture: standing firm, weight balanced, slight forward lean
- Hands: RIGHT lowers and hovers; LEFT near sash

Micro-expression: around second 3, a brief jaw muscle twitch
for about 0.5 seconds, then he regains stillness.

CONTINUITY LOCK: Match attached image exactly—same lighting,
same colors, same exposure. No extra people. No new objects.
No magical glow on the crescent.

ENDS WITH: Camera 14 inches closer. Right hand hovering above
closed sandook lid, eyes lifted forward, identical color grading."
───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
PROBLEMS: Redescribes visible scene (50 wasted words).
7-layer acting overloads AI. Inch measurements ignored.
No body physics. No anti-morph. Face lock missing.
Camera purpose not stated.
───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

AFTER (rewritten with Video Prompt Mastery):
───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
Aurangzab stands near the closed sandook on the Ghaat path.

PRIMARY ACTION:
Aurangzab's right hand slowly lowers toward the sandook lid,
fingers gradually spreading with visible tension. The hand
stops just above the surface, hovering — ready to steady the
box but not yet touching it. His left hand stays near his sash,
fingers curling slightly tighter.

SECONDARY HINTS:
Subtle head turn — gaze shifts from scanning ahead to looking
down at the sandook. Shoulders carry slightly more tension
by the end of the clip.

CAMERA:
Slow push in toward Aurangzab and the sandook, building
intimacy and focusing attention on the hand-to-sandook moment.

EMOTION:
Controlled determination masking deep unease — a young leader
forcing calm while sensing approaching danger.

PHYSICS:
Chest rises with one deep, controlled breath held at the peak.
Weight settles firmly onto front foot, grounding his stance.
The maroon sash shifts gently with his arm movement.

FACE LOCK:
Face remains sharp, in focus, consistent with attached image.
No morphing, no blurring. Expression changes as described only.

STABILITY LOCKS:
- No new people, objects, or lights appear
- Sandook remains CLOSED, golden crescent visible
- Colors and lighting match attached image exactly
- Single continuous shot, no cuts, no transitions

ENDS WITH:
- Right hand hovering just above sandook lid, not touching
- Gaze directed down at the sandook
- Expression: determined but alert, jaw slightly set
- Energy: tense stillness, coiled and ready
───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
WHY BETTER: Motion first. No redescription. 3-tier acting.
Relative terms. Body physics (breath, weight, fabric).
Face lock. Anti-morph. Camera with purpose. Clear end state.
───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────


═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
CLIP TYPE SPECIFIC TIPS
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

ESTABLISHING / WIDE (no faces):
- Focus on ENVIRONMENT motion: torch flames, dust, wind
- Camera movement can be bolder (drone drift, crane)
- Human figures move as SILHOUETTES — "small figures walk
  with visible leg movement, torches sway in wind"
- Add atmosphere: "dust particles drift in torchlight"

MEDIUM SHOTS (1 person):
- Use full 3-tier acting system
- Camera: push in OR static with handheld drift
- Always include 3 physics elements
- Face lock essential

CLOSE-UPS (face emotion):
- PRIMARY ACTION = emotional shift, NOT physical movement
- "His expression transitions from guarded calm to barely
  contained fear" > describing individual facial muscles
- Add: "natural blink" and "subtle jaw tension"
- Camera: STATIC or very gentle push in
- Face lock CRITICAL — "face stays pin-sharp throughout"

DETAIL / INSERT (objects):
- Focus on OBJECT physics: dust settling, metal glinting,
  wood creaking, fabric rippling
- No faces needed = no face problems
- Camera: static macro or very slow push in
- Best results: describe particle physics around object

ACTION / CHAOS (multiple elements):
- Pick THE most important action as primary
- Other actions are "secondary: [brief hint]"
- "Arrows streak across frame while [primary action]"
- Keep camera movement simple (static or slow push)
- More motion in scene = less camera movement needed


═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
COMMON VIDEO AI PROBLEMS AND FIXES
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

PROBLEM: Characters SLIDE instead of walk
FIX: "visible leg movement, feet lift off ground, natural gait"

PROBLEM: Faces BLUR or MORPH
FIX: Add Face Lock paragraph + keep clips to 6 sec max
     + prefer close-ups with 1 character only

PROBLEM: Objects APPEAR or DISAPPEAR
FIX: Add full Stability Locks section

PROBLEM: Colors SHIFT during clip
FIX: "Colors and lighting remain exactly as in attached image,
     zero color drift, zero exposure change"

PROBLEM: Movement looks MECHANICAL / robotic
FIX: Add 3+ physics elements (breathing, weight, fabric)
     + use emotion words not body-part instructions

PROBLEM: Too much CHAOS / uncontrolled motion
FIX: Reduce to 1 hero action. Add "everything else holds still"
     Use "gently" "subtly" "slightly" for secondary motion

PROBLEM: NOTHING happens (too static)
FIX: Use intensity adverbs: "noticeably", "clearly", "with force"
     Make sure primary action has clear start → end states

PROBLEM: LIP SYNC looks wrong
FIX: Use phonetic breakdown with syllable shapes and duration

PROBLEM: Clip DOESN'T MATCH next clip (continuity break)
FIX: Strong ENDS WITH section describing exact final state
     Export last frame as reference for next clip

PROBLEM: AI generates CUT or FADE (not continuous)
FIX: "Single continuous shot from start to end. No cuts, no
     fades, no dissolves, no transitions. One unbroken take."


GROK-SPECIFIC PROMPT OPTIMIZATION:
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
╔══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╗
║                                                               ║
║   Grok Imagine uses the Aurora engine for Image-to-Video.    ║
║   It works DIFFERENTLY from other video AIs.                 ║
║   Follow these Grok-specific rules for BEST results!         ║
║                                                               ║
╚══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╝


⚡ SCENE TYPE DETECTION (MANDATORY FIRST STEP!):
━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
╔══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╗
║                                                               ║
║   BEFORE writing ANY Grok video prompt, FIRST identify       ║
║   the SCENE TYPE. This determines EVERYTHING:                ║
║   prompt length, detail level, what YOU control vs           ║
║   what GROK controls.                                        ║
║                                                               ║
║   TWO MODES — each has opposite rules!                       ║
║                                                               ║
╚══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╝

┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│                                                                │
│  MODE 1: SUBTLE / DIALOGUE / EMOTIONAL SCENES                 │
│  ─────────────────────────────────────────────                 │
│  Keywords: conversation, whisper, reaction, thinking,          │
│  observing, emotional moment, revelation, quiet tension        │
│                                                                │
│  YOU CONTROL: expression, camera, body language, audio,        │
│  dialogue volume, environment details                          │
│  GROK FOLLOWS: your detailed instructions                      │
│                                                                │
│  PROMPT LENGTH: 80-100 words                                   │
│  DETAIL LEVEL: HIGH — describe expression, camera,             │
│  body language, audio, timing                                  │
│                                                                │
├──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│                                                                │
│  MODE 2: ACTION / COMBAT / PHYSICAL IMPACT SCENES             │
│  ─────────────────────────────────────────────────             │
│  Keywords: fight, hit, swing, charge, attack, clash,           │
│  chase, jump, throw, crash, battle, combat                     │
│                                                                │
│  YOU CONTROL: WHO does WHAT to WHOM and HOW HARD              │
│  GROK CONTROLS: impact physics, debris, camera angle,          │
│  character reactions, environment response, sound              │
│                                                                │
│  PROMPT LENGTH: 10-25 words                                    │
│  DETAIL LEVEL: MINIMAL — action verb + target + degree         │
│  word ONLY. NO impact results, NO camera, NO reactions.        │
│                                                                │
└──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

WHY TWO MODES?
━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
Grok handles subtle scenes and action scenes with 
OPPOSITE approaches:

SUBTLE: Grok NEEDS detailed direction. Without it, 
expressions become random, dialogue becomes screaming/
whispering randomly, camera does unpredictable things.
→ MORE detail = BETTER result.

ACTION: Grok ALREADY knows how to choreograph combat.
When you over-describe action, Grok gets CONFUSED — 
characters morph, extra people spawn, physics break.
→ LESS detail = BETTER result.

TESTED PROOF:
- 105-word combat prompt = morphing, spawning, unrealistic
- 15-word combat prompt = perfect shield impact, debris, 
  realistic reactions — Grok decided everything itself
- 100-word dialogue prompt = controlled expression, proper 
  whisper volume, stable background

SCENE TYPE DETECTION TABLE:
┌────────────────────────┬──────────────┬────────────────┐
│ Scene Description       │ TYPE          │ Prompt Length   │
├────────────────────────┼──────────────┼────────────────┤
│ Character speaks        │ SUBTLE        │ 80-100 words   │
│ Character reacts        │ SUBTLE        │ 80-100 words   │
│ Character thinks        │ SUBTLE        │ 80-100 words   │
│ Emperor gives order     │ SUBTLE        │ 80-100 words   │
│ Character walks slowly  │ SUBTLE        │ 80-100 words   │
│ Emotional revelation    │ SUBTLE        │ 80-100 words   │
│ Character looks around  │ SUBTLE        │ 80-100 words   │
├────────────────────────┼──────────────┼────────────────┤
│ Sword hits shield       │ ACTION        │ 10-25 words    │
│ Character charges       │ ACTION        │ 10-25 words    │
│ Mace smashes target     │ ACTION        │ 10-25 words    │
│ Arrow hits wall         │ ACTION        │ 10-25 words    │
│ Horse gallops           │ ACTION        │ 10-25 words    │
│ Explosion / collapse    │ ACTION        │ 10-25 words    │
│ Character throws object │ ACTION        │ 10-25 words    │
│ Hand-to-hand combat     │ ACTION        │ 10-25 words    │
└────────────────────────┴──────────────┴────────────────┘


═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
MODE 1: SUBTLE / DIALOGUE SCENE SYSTEM (80-100 words)
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

5-LAYER CHECKLIST (Minimum 3 layers required):
━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
Every subtle/dialogue prompt must touch AT LEAST 3 
of these 5 layers. Weak prompts only describe scene.
Strong prompts cover 3+. Best prompts cover all 5.

┌──────────────────┬───────────────────────────────────┐
│ LAYER             │ WHAT TO DESCRIBE                   │
├──────────────────┼───────────────────────────────────┤
│ 1. SCENE          │ What's happening (action/emotion)  │
│ 2. CAMERA         │ How it's filmed (angle/movement)   │
│ 3. STYLE/LIGHT    │ How it looks (lighting/mood)        │
│ 4. MOTION         │ How things move (body/environment)  │
│ 5. AUDIO          │ What we hear (sounds/dialogue)      │
└──────────────────┴───────────────────────────────────┘

SUBTLE SCENE GOLDEN FORMULA:
[Camera Movement] + [Subject Action + Degree Word]
+ [Expression: ONE emotion word] + [Body Language]
+ [Environment Physics] + [Audio with volume level]

GROK-SPECIFIC RULES FOR SUBTLE SCENES:
1. MAX 100 WORDS — Grok loses focus beyond this!
2. FIRST 30 WORDS = Camera + main action (highest priority)
3. NEGATIVE PROMPTS DON'T WORK — Grok ignores "no fog" etc.
   Only describe what you WANT (positive instructions only)
4. DEGREE WORDS are powerful — slowly, rapidly, gently, 
   forcefully, subtly, violently, dramatically, powerfully
5. ALWAYS SPECIFY AUDIO — or Grok adds GENERIC music!
   "Audio: wind, torch crackle, no music" prevents this
6. Use "Unfixed lens" in Grok settings for camera movement
7. Grok supports: push-in, pull-out, pan, tilt, orbit, 
   follow/track, handheld, static, crane up/down, dolly

SUBTLE SCENE TEMPLATE:
▼▼▼ TEMPLATE ▼▼▼
[Camera movement + endpoint]. [Subject identified by 
visual features performs key action with degree word]. 
[Face description: ONE emotion word — mouth state, jaw 
state]. [ONE body language action]. [1-2 environment 
physics]. Background stays sharp and in focus with full 
detail throughout. Single continuous shot.
Audio: [specific sounds], [dialogue with volume word 
if any], no music.
ENDS WITH: [final state description].
▲▲▲ END TEMPLATE ▲▲▲

SUBTLE SCENE EXAMPLE (Messenger — TESTED ✅):
"High angle looking down at the kneeling messenger. 
Slow push-in ending at his chest level. His hands 
tremble visibly as he raises the empty open box 
slightly higher. Mouth stays closed, jaw clenched 
tight, brow furrowed — suppressed fear only. Chest 
rises and falls with slow controlled breathing. At 
second three his lips barely part as he murmurs in 
a low shaky whisper: 'Shahi Mohar... chori ho gayi, 
Jahanpanah.' Head stays bowed throughout. Torch glow 
sways gently on the walls. Fine dust drifts through 
the single light beam. Background stays sharp and in 
focus with full detail. Single continuous shot.
Audio: barely audible whisper with soft hall echo, 
faint cloth rustling, no music.
ENDS WITH: messenger still, box raised, silence."


═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
MODE 2: ACTION / COMBAT SCENE SYSTEM (10-25 words)
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
╔══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╗
║                                                               ║
║   ⚡ FOR ACTION SCENES: LESS IS MORE!                        ║
║                                                               ║
║   Grok ALREADY knows how to choreograph combat.             ║
║   The more you describe, the WORSE the result.              ║
║   15 words beat 105 words in EVERY test.                    ║
║                                                               ║
║   YOU define the ACTION.                                     ║
║   GROK handles the IMPACT.                                   ║
║                                                               ║
╚══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╝

ACTION SCENE FORMULA (TESTED & CONFIRMED!):
━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━

[Subject] + [Action Verb] + [Target] + [Degree Word]

That's it. Nothing else. 10-25 words MAXIMUM.

YOU DEFINE (4 things only):
✅ WHO — subject (the warrior, the soldier, the rider)
✅ DOES WHAT — action verb (swings, thrusts, slams, kicks)
✅ TO WHOM/WHAT — target (shield, enemy, gate, wall)
✅ HOW HARD — degree word (very hard, with full force, 
   violently, with massive strength)

GROK DECIDES (everything else):
✅ Impact physics (how shield breaks, how debris flies)
✅ Camera angle and movement (best angle for action)
✅ Character reactions (how target reacts to hit)
✅ Environment response (dust, sparks, rain spray)
✅ Sound design (impact sounds, cries, environmental)
✅ Debris and particle effects
✅ Body mechanics and follow-through

⛔ NEVER ADD TO ACTION PROMPTS:
❌ Impact results — "shield shatters into pieces"
   → Grok over-interprets, creates bomb explosion
❌ Camera instructions — "camera orbits rapidly"
   → Grok gets confused, picks wrong angle
❌ Victim reactions — "soldier falls backward screaming"
   → Grok SPAWNS new characters
❌ Debris descriptions — "sparks fly everywhere"
   → Grok creates unrealistic particle effects
❌ Environment reactions — "ground shakes from impact"
   → Grok morphs the environment
❌ Other characters — "surrounding soldiers flinch"
   → Grok ADDS extra soldiers that weren't in image
❌ Sound descriptions — let Grok auto-generate
❌ Background instructions — Grok preserves naturally

WHY THIS WORKS:
Grok's Aurora engine has built-in physics simulation.
When you say "hits shield very hard" — Grok ALREADY 
KNOWS what happens: debris, impact, reaction. But when 
you DESCRIBE what should happen, Grok tries to follow 
YOUR physics instead of ITS physics = unrealistic result.

Think of it like telling a stunt coordinator:
✅ "Hit the shield hard" → coordinator creates epic stunt
❌ "Hit the shield, then shield breaks at 45 degrees, 
   three pieces fly left, dust rises 2 feet, enemy 
   stumbles back 3 steps" → awkward forced stunt

ACTION SCENE EXAMPLES (TESTED ✅):

Example 1 — Mace vs Shield:
"The warrior swings his mace very hard onto the 
nearest soldier's shield."
(13 words — PERFECT combat result with impact, debris,
 reactions all generated naturally by Grok)

Example 2 — Sword Strike:
"The knight slashes his sword across the enemy's 
chest armor with full force."
(14 words)

Example 3 — Spear Thrust:
"The soldier drives his spear into the wooden 
barricade with massive strength."
(13 words)

Example 4 — Shield Bash:
"The guard rams his shield into the attacker's 
body very forcefully."
(12 words)

Example 5 — Arrow Impact:
"The arrow strikes the stone pillar with great 
speed and force."
(12 words)

DEGREE WORDS FOR ACTION (use these!):
very hard, with full force, with massive strength,
violently, forcefully, with brutal impact, with 
great power, with tremendous speed, fiercely,
with all his strength


═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
UNIVERSAL GROK RULES (Apply to BOTH Modes):
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════


IMAGE-TO-VIDEO CONTRADICTION RULE (CRITICAL!):
━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
╔══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╗
║                                                               ║
║   ⛔ Video prompt MUST NOT contradict the attached image!    ║
║                                                               ║
║   Grok SEES the image. If your prompt says something        ║
║   different from what's IN the image, Grok gets confused    ║
║   and produces morphing, spawning, or artifacts.            ║
║                                                               ║
╚══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╝

RULES:
1. If image has a MAN, prompt must NOT say "woman"
2. If image has a FOREST, prompt must NOT say "desert"
3. If image has 4 soldiers, prompt must NOT describe 10
4. If character faces LEFT, prompt must NOT say "faces right"
5. If character holds SWORD, prompt must NOT say "holds spear"
6. NEVER mention characters/objects NOT visible in the image
   → Grok will try to GENERATE them = spawn artifacts
7. NEVER describe the camera from an angle that contradicts 
   the image perspective (image is front view but prompt 
   says "camera follows from behind" = disaster)

WHAT HAPPENS WHEN YOU CONTRADICT:
- Mention person not in image → Grok SPAWNS random figure
- Describe wrong weapon → Grok MORPHS existing weapon
- Wrong background → Grok tries to CHANGE environment
- Wrong camera angle → Grok warps perspective

SAFE APPROACH: Only describe what CHANGES from the image.
Everything static in the image will stay automatically.


EXPRESSION PRESERVATION RULE:
━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
╔══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╗
║                                                               ║
║   When the image already has a SPECIFIC expression and      ║
║   you want to KEEP it in the video, you MUST describe       ║
║   the face DIRECTLY — never say "as in the image".          ║
║                                                               ║
║   Grok ignores meta-instructions like "face stays as in     ║
║   the image." It only follows DESCRIPTIONS.                 ║
║                                                               ║
╚══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╝

❌ WRONG (meta-instruction, Grok ignores):
"Face stays exactly as in the image"
"Expression matches the reference"
"Keep the same look from the photo"

✅ RIGHT (direct face description):
"Mouth stays closed, jaw clenched tight, brow furrowed 
 — suppressed fear only"
"Eyes narrowed, lips pressed together — cold assessment"
"Mouth slightly open, eyebrows raised — frozen shock"

RULE: Describe the ACTUAL face state you want. 
Grok follows descriptions, not references.

EXPRESSION ESCALATION PREVENTION:
When image has a CALM/CONTROLLED expression but the 
scene involves intense emotion (fear, anger, grief):

⛔ DANGER: Grok will ESCALATE the expression to maximum!
   - Image shows quiet dread → Grok makes SCREAMING face
   - Image shows controlled anger → Grok makes RAGING face
   - Image shows sadness → Grok makes WAILING face

FIX: Use CALM emotion words even for intense scenes:
┌────────────────────────┬──────────────────────────────┐
│ ❌ ESCALATING WORDS     │ ✅ CONTROLLED WORDS           │
│ (Grok goes to max)     │ (Grok keeps it subtle)        │
├────────────────────────┼──────────────────────────────┤
│ terrified              │ quiet dread                    │
│ desperate              │ contained urgency              │
│ agonized               │ still pain                     │
│ furious                │ cold anger                     │
│ devastated             │ deep stillness of grief        │
│ panicked               │ frozen tension                 │
│ hysterical             │ held-back emotion              │
└────────────────────────┴──────────────────────────────┘

WHEN TO USE ESCALATING vs CONTROLLED:
- Action/combat scenes: escalating words OK (Grok should 
  go big — "fierce roar", "furious strike")
- Subtle/dialogue scenes: ALWAYS use controlled words 
  (Grok must stay subtle — "quiet dread", "still pain")


EXPRESSION INTENSITY SCALE:
━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
╔══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╗
║                                                               ║
║   ⚠️ AI ALWAYS defaults to Level 5 (maximum intensity)!     ║
║   If you say "fear" + "speaking" → AI generates SCREAMING.  ║
║   You MUST specify intensity level or AI goes maximum.       ║
║                                                               ║
╚══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╝

LEVEL 1 — MICRO-EXPRESSION (barely visible):
"A flicker of [emotion] crosses his face, almost 
 imperceptible. Face appears nearly neutral."
Use for: hidden feelings, poker face, professional mask

LEVEL 2 — SUPPRESSED (holding it in):
"[Emotion] visible in his eyes but mouth stays closed, 
 jaw tight. He is holding it in."
Use for: fear while trying to be brave, anger while 
staying professional, grief while maintaining composure

LEVEL 3 — VISIBLE (clearly showing):
"[Emotion] clearly visible on his face. [One facial 
 feature change]."
Use for: natural reactions, moderate emotional moments

LEVEL 4 — STRONG (intense but controlled):
"Strong [emotion] on his face. [Two facial features 
 change]. Body language reinforces the feeling."
Use for: confrontations, major revelations, high stakes

LEVEL 5 — EXTREME (screaming, crying, raging):
"Full [emotion] — [open mouth/tears/clenched teeth]. 
 Entire body expresses the feeling."
Use for: battle cries, devastating loss, explosive rage
→ THIS IS AI DEFAULT! Only use when you actually WANT max!

RULE: Always identify the CORRECT level for your scene.
If you need Level 2 (suppressed fear for whisper scene)
but don't specify, AI will generate Level 5 (screaming).


BACKGROUND PRESERVATION RULE:
━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
Grok may BLUR or SOFTEN background by default to create 
"cinematic" depth of field. This looks bad in most scenes.

FOR SUBTLE SCENES — always add this line:
"Background stays sharp and in focus with full detail 
 throughout."

FOR ACTION SCENES — do NOT add this line.
Let Grok decide. Action scenes benefit from natural 
motion blur and Grok handles it well.

WHAT CAUSES BACKGROUND BLUR:
- Saying "background stays in place" → Grok reads as 
  "background not important" → blurs it
- Over-focusing on subject → Grok de-prioritizes background
- Not mentioning background at all → Grok decides randomly

FIX: "sharp and in focus with full detail" explicitly 
tells Grok to render background at full quality.


GROK OBJECT/CHARACTER SPAWNING PREVENTION:
━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
The #1 problem: characters and objects APPEAR or VANISH!

CAUSES:
1. Mentioning characters NOT in image → Grok CREATES them
2. Describing reactions of background characters → Grok 
   SPAWNS new characters to "react"  
3. Asking for new objects → Grok generates them, sometimes
   replacing existing objects

PREVENTION RULES:
1. Only mention characters/objects VISIBLE in the image
2. Do NOT describe reactions of secondary characters
3. Do NOT ask for objects that aren't in the image
4. For subtle scenes: "Background stays sharp and in focus 
   with full detail throughout" (prevents morphing)
5. For action scenes: say NOTHING about background 
   (Grok preserves automatically when prompt is short)

WHAT TO DO ABOUT SECONDARY CHARACTERS:
If image has 5 soldiers in background:
❌ "The surrounding soldiers flinch and step back"
   → Grok ADDS more soldiers
✅ Say nothing about them → Grok animates them naturally
✅ Or for subtle scenes: "All other figures remain still"


GROK HANDS & SMALL OBJECTS WARNING:
━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
Hands interacting with small objects = Grok's WEAKEST 
area. Fingers morph, objects distort, physics break.

SAFE ACTIONS:
✅ Hand rests on large surface (table, armrest, wall)
✅ Hand grips large weapon (sword, mace, spear shaft)
✅ Hand makes fist or opens flat
✅ Hand gestures (pointing, waving, beckoning)
✅ Arms cross, hands on hips, hands behind back

RISKY ACTIONS (avoid if possible):
⚠️ Fingers manipulating small objects (coins, rings, beads)
⚠️ Hand picking up something delicate
⚠️ Two hands interacting with same small object
⚠️ Close-up of fingers doing detailed work

IF YOU MUST SHOW HANDS WITH OBJECTS:
Keep hands still: "His hand rests motionless on [object]"
Don't animate the interaction. Let the hand be a 
static anchor while other things move.


NEGATIVE-TO-POSITIVE CONVERSION (MANDATORY!):
━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
Grok IGNORES all negative instructions. EVERY negative 
statement MUST be rewritten as a positive statement before 
putting it in a Grok video prompt.

CONVERSION TABLE:
┌────────────────────────────────────┬─────────────────────────────────────┐
│ ❌ NEGATIVE (Grok ignores)         │ ✅ POSITIVE (Grok follows)           │
├────────────────────────────────────┼─────────────────────────────────────┤
│ "no morphing"                      │ "All elements maintain exact        │
│                                    │  appearance throughout"             │
├────────────────────────────────────┼─────────────────────────────────────┤
│ "no new objects appear"            │ "Scene contains only existing       │
│                                    │  elements from the image"           │
├────────────────────────────────────┼─────────────────────────────────────┤
│ "no scene change"                  │ "Single continuous scene            │
│                                    │  maintained throughout"             │
├────────────────────────────────────┼─────────────────────────────────────┤
│ "no fog"                           │ "Air remains clean and clear"       │
├────────────────────────────────────┼─────────────────────────────────────┤
│ "no color drift"                   │ "Colors stay locked to image"       │
├────────────────────────────────────┼─────────────────────────────────────┤
│ "second hand does not move"        │ "Clock hands remain completely      │
│                                    │  frozen and still throughout"       │
├────────────────────────────────────┼─────────────────────────────────────┤
│ "no cuts, no transitions"          │ "Single continuous unbroken take"   │
├────────────────────────────────────┼─────────────────────────────────────┤
│ "no extra people"                  │ "Only the existing figures remain   │
│                                    │  visible throughout"                │
├────────────────────────────────────┼─────────────────────────────────────┤
│ "don't move the object"            │ "Object stays fixed in its exact    │
│                                    │  position throughout"               │
├────────────────────────────────────┼─────────────────────────────────────┤
│ "no blur on face"                  │ "Face remains sharp and in focus"   │
└────────────────────────────────────┴─────────────────────────────────────┘

⚠️ EXCEPTION: "no music" in the Audio line IS the one negative 
that works in Grok, because it's an audio instruction, not a 
visual instruction. Keep using "Audio: [sounds], no music."

BEFORE FINALIZING ANY GROK VIDEO PROMPT:
Scan for ANY word: "no", "not", "don't", "never", "without"
→ If found in visual instructions → REWRITE as positive
→ If found in Audio line as "no music" → KEEP (this works)


WEAK DEGREE WORDS — BANNED!:
━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
Some words are too weak — Grok reads them as "maybe do 
something, maybe don't" and often does NOTHING.

BANNED WORDS (never use in Grok video prompts):
┌─────────────────────────┬──────────────────────────────────┐
│ ❌ BANNED (too weak)     │ ✅ USE INSTEAD                    │
├─────────────────────────┼──────────────────────────────────┤
│ "almost imperceptible"  │ "slow but visible"               │
│ "barely noticeable"     │ "subtle but clear"               │
│ "nearly invisible"      │ "faint but present"              │
│ "ever so slightly"      │ "slightly"                       │
│ "just a hint of"        │ "gentle" or "soft"               │
│ "the tiniest bit"       │ "subtle"                         │
│ "almost nothing"        │ "minimal but visible"            │
│ "imperceptibly"         │ "gradually"                      │
│ "virtually undetectable"│ "very subtle"                    │
└─────────────────────────┴──────────────────────────────────┘

WHY: If you say "almost imperceptible push-in", Grok thinks:
"They want it barely visible... so I'll just keep it static."
Result: NOTHING happens. Camera stays still.

If you say "slow but visible push-in", Grok thinks:
"Clear instruction — slow forward movement."
Result: Smooth, cinematic push-in. ✅

DEGREE WORD STRENGTH SCALE (use the right level):
━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
Level 1 (minimal):  "very subtle", "gentle", "soft"
Level 2 (light):    "slight", "slow", "faint"
Level 3 (medium):   "noticeable", "steady", "clear"
Level 4 (strong):   "forceful", "dramatic", "powerful"
Level 5 (intense):  "violent", "rapid", "explosive"

Pick the level that matches your scene energy.
Horror dread = Level 1-2 (subtle, slow, gentle)
Dialogue = Level 2-3 (slight, steady)
Action = Level 3-4 (noticeable, forceful)
Chaos/explosion = Level 4-5 (dramatic, rapid)


LIGHT FLICKER CONTROL (Grok Video):
━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
╔══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╗
║                                                               ║
║   ⛔ DO NOT put "light flickers" in every clip!              ║
║                                                               ║
║   MAXIMUM 1 flicker per SCENE (not per clip!)                ║
║   A scene has 6-12 clips. Only ONE clip gets flicker.        ║
║   All other clips: "Lighting remains steady throughout"      ║
║                                                               ║
╚══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╝

WHY THIS MATTERS:
- Real bulbs don't flicker every 6 seconds
- Grok interprets "flicker" as DRAMATIC on-off pulse (not subtle)
- Entire room goes dark-bright-dark = unnatural horror cliché
- Repeated in every clip = viewer thinks "bad AI video"

RULES:
1. MAX 1 light flicker event per ENTIRE scene
2. Pick the MOST impactful clip for the flicker (usually a 
   tension peak or reveal moment)
3. All OTHER clips in that scene: "Lighting remains steady 
   and consistent throughout"
4. NEVER use the word "flicker" — Grok overdoes it

INSTEAD OF "FLICKER", USE:
┌─────────────────────────────┬──────────────────────────────┐
│ ❌ WRONG (Grok overdoes)     │ ✅ RIGHT (subtle, controlled) │
├─────────────────────────────┼──────────────────────────────┤
│ "light flickers"            │ "warm light gently shifts"   │
│ "light flickers once"       │ "shadow edge breathes once"  │
│ "practical light flickers"  │ "light intensity softens     │
│                             │  slightly then returns"      │
│ "torchlight flickers"       │ "torch glow sways gently"    │
│ "bulb flickers on and off"  │ "light dims briefly then     │
│                             │  steadies"                   │
└─────────────────────────────┴──────────────────────────────┘

FOR STEADY CLIPS (majority of clips):
Add this line: "Lighting remains steady and consistent 
throughout. Light sources stay at constant brightness."

FOR THE ONE FLICKER CLIP:
"The warm light dims briefly for half a second then returns 
to normal brightness. Shadows shift gently during the dim 
and settle back."

SCENE FLICKER PLANNING:
When planning a scene with 6+ clips, mark WHICH clip gets 
the light event:

Example — Scene 1 (8 clips):
Clip 1.1: Lighting steady ← no flicker
Clip 1.2: Lighting steady ← no flicker
Clip 1.3: Lighting steady ← no flicker
Clip 1.4: Light dims briefly ← THE ONE flicker moment
Clip 1.5: Lighting steady ← no flicker
Clip 1.6: Lighting steady ← no flicker
Clip 1.7: Lighting steady ← no flicker
Clip 1.8: Lighting steady ← no flicker

GROK AUDIO CONTROL (CRITICAL!):
━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
Grok generates synchronized audio. If you don't specify,
it adds GENERIC background music you probably don't want!

ALWAYS end prompts with one of these:
- "Audio: [specific sounds], no music."
- "Audio: [character] says urgently: '[dialogue]', [sounds]."
- "Audio: [ambient sounds], [SFX], no music."

Examples:
- "Audio: wind howling in mountain pass, torch crackle, no music."
- "Audio: arrow whoosh, stone impact crack, wind, no music."
- "Audio: he shouts 'Sandook!' urgently, echo in pass, no music."

GROK LIP SYNC (Simplified):
━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
Don't micro-manage mouth physics. Grok handles lip sync 
better when given the WORD + EMOTION:

FORMAT: "[Visual ID] says/shouts [emotion]: '[dialogue]'"

❌ OLD: "Seconds 2-3.5: He SHOUTS 'Sandook!' His lips and 
jaw move naturally to form the word clearly; mouth opens 
wide then closes at the end."

✅ NEW: "The bearded man in dark vest shouts urgently: 
'Sandook!' — arm thrusting forward powerfully."


GROK EXPRESSION SYSTEM (UNIVERSAL!):
━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
╔══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╗
║                                                               ║
║   ⛔ DO NOT describe facial micro-movements!                 ║
║   Grok cannot control individual muscles.                    ║
║   Overexplaining = WORSE result (confused, frozen face)      ║
║                                                               ║
║   ✅ Give Grok: EMOTION WORD + BODY LANGUAGE                ║
║   Grok understands emotions as WHOLE expressions.            ║
║                                                               ║
╚══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╝

THE PROBLEM:
When you write "his eyebrows furrow, jaw tightens, lips 
press together, eyes narrow slightly, forehead creases 
deepen as anger builds" — Grok tries to animate EACH 
micro-movement separately and FAILS. Result: frozen face, 
twitching, or bizarre expressions.

THE FIX:
Give ONE emotion word + ONE body language action. That's it.
Grok will generate the ENTIRE expression naturally from 
the emotion word alone.

EXPRESSION FORMAT:
"[Character visual ID] [body action] while [emotion] 
builds visibly on his face."

EXAMPLES:
❌ WRONG (overexplained micro-movements):
"His eyebrows furrow deeply, jaw muscles clench 
tightly, lips press into a thin line, nostrils 
flare slightly, and eyes narrow with intense focus 
as fury builds from within..."

✅ RIGHT (emotion + body language):
"He lifts his head slowly while anger builds visibly 
on his face. Fists clench at his sides."

❌ WRONG:
"Tears well up in his eyes, lower lip trembles 
slightly, brow furrows upward in the center, 
cheeks tighten, breathing becomes shallow..."

✅ RIGHT:
"He lowers his gaze while grief overtakes his face. 
His shoulders drop and hands go limp."

❌ WRONG:
"Eyes widen progressively, mouth opens 2cm, head 
pulls back 3 inches, pupils dilate, skin pales..."

✅ RIGHT:
"He steps back while shock freezes his face. 
His hand rises to cover his mouth."

GROK EMOTION VOCABULARY (tested, these WORK):
┌─────────────────────┬────────────────────────────────┐
│ EMOTION CATEGORY     │ WORDS GROK UNDERSTANDS          │
├─────────────────────┼────────────────────────────────┤
│ Anger               │ angry, furious, seething,       │
│                     │ enraged, cold fury              │
├─────────────────────┼────────────────────────────────┤
│ Sadness             │ sad, grief-stricken,            │
│                     │ heartbroken, devastated         │
├─────────────────────┼────────────────────────────────┤
│ Fear                │ terrified, frightened, uneasy,  │
│                     │ anxious, panicked               │
├─────────────────────┼────────────────────────────────┤
│ Determination       │ determined, resolute, defiant,  │
│                     │ steely, unwavering              │
├─────────────────────┼────────────────────────────────┤
│ Shock               │ shocked, stunned, horrified,    │
│                     │ disbelief, frozen               │
├─────────────────────┼────────────────────────────────┤
│ Power / Authority   │ cold, calculating, unimpressed, │
│                     │ commanding, dismissive          │
├─────────────────────┼────────────────────────────────┤
│ Desperation         │ desperate, pleading, begging,   │
│                     │ frantic, helpless               │
├─────────────────────┼────────────────────────────────┤
│ Suspicion           │ suspicious, wary, distrustful,  │
│                     │ scrutinizing, guarded           │
├─────────────────────┼────────────────────────────────┤
│ Joy / Relief        │ relieved, grateful, smiling,    │
│                     │ triumphant, warm                │
└─────────────────────┴────────────────────────────────┘

BODY LANGUAGE PAIRINGS (pair emotion with physical action):
- Anger: clench fists, grip sword tighter, lean forward
- Grief: drop shoulders, lower gaze, hands go limp
- Fear: step back, raise hands defensively, freeze in place
- Determination: straighten spine, lift chin, square shoulders
- Shock: step backward, hand to mouth, freeze mid-motion
- Power: remain perfectly still, slow deliberate head turn
- Desperation: fall to knees, extend hands, bow head
- Suspicion: narrow stance, tilt head slightly, hand on weapon

RULE: ONE emotion + ONE body action per 6-second clip.
Do NOT give 3 emotions in one clip — Grok will blend them 
into an unreadable mess.


GROK DIALOGUE VOLUME CONTROL (UNIVERSAL!):
━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
╔══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╗
║                                                               ║
║   ⛔ Grok defaults to EXTREMES for dialogue!                 ║
║   Without volume instruction:                                ║
║   → Character either WHISPERS inaudibly                      ║
║   → Or SCREAMS at full volume                                ║
║   → No middle ground!                                        ║
║                                                               ║
║   ✅ ALWAYS specify volume/delivery style!                   ║
║                                                               ║
╚══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╝

VOLUME LEVELS (always pick one):
┌────────────────────┬────────────────────────────────────┐
│ VOLUME WORD         │ WHAT GROK GENERATES                 │
├────────────────────┼────────────────────────────────────┤
│ "whispers:"        │ Very quiet, intimate, barely        │
│                    │ audible (USE for secrets/fear)      │
├────────────────────┼────────────────────────────────────┤
│ "says quietly:"    │ Soft indoor voice, controlled       │
│                    │ (USE for private conversation)      │
├────────────────────┼────────────────────────────────────┤
│ "says calmly:"     │ Normal speaking volume, steady      │
│                    │ (USE for neutral dialogue)          │
├────────────────────┼────────────────────────────────────┤
│ "says firmly:"     │ Confident, authoritative, clear     │
│                    │ (USE for commands/decisions)        │
├────────────────────┼────────────────────────────────────┤
│ "says urgently:"   │ Raised voice, strained, tense       │
│                    │ (USE for warnings/panic)            │
├────────────────────┼────────────────────────────────────┤
│ "shouts:"          │ Full volume, powerful projection     │
│                    │ (USE for battle cries/calls)        │
├────────────────────┼────────────────────────────────────┤
│ "screams:"         │ Maximum volume, raw, emotional       │
│                    │ (USE for extreme pain/terror)       │
└────────────────────┴────────────────────────────────────┘

DIALOGUE FORMAT:
"The [visual ID] [volume word] '[dialogue]'"

EXAMPLES:
❌ "He says: 'Shahi Mohar chori ho gayi!'" 
   → Grok randomly picks whisper or scream

✅ "The dusty messenger says desperately: 
   'Shahi Mohar chori ho gayi, Jahanpanah!'"
   → Grok: raised, strained, pleading voice ✅

❌ "He says: 'Chalo, andar chalte hain.'"
   → Grok: random volume

✅ "The young man in blue kurta says firmly: 
   'Chalo, andar chalte hain.'"
   → Grok: confident, decisive, clear ✅

RULE: NEVER use bare "says:" — ALWAYS add a volume/delivery 
word. If unsure, use "says calmly:" as safe default.

GROK VIDEO PROMPT TEMPLATES (DUAL MODE):
━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━

⚠️ Templates are defined in the SCENE TYPE DETECTION 
section above. Two templates exist:

→ SUBTLE/DIALOGUE TEMPLATE: 80-100 words, detailed
  (see MODE 1 section above)

→ ACTION/COMBAT TEMPLATE: 10-25 words, minimal
  (see MODE 2 section above)

ALWAYS identify scene type FIRST, then pick correct 
template. NEVER use the subtle template for action 
scenes or vice versa.

TEMPLATE EXAMPLES (both modes):

SUBTLE EXAMPLE — Emperor on throne:
"Low angle medium shot. Very slow push-in toward the
