emperor on the throne. He gradually turns his head to 
the right and looks downward with cold, unimpressed 
assessment. His body stays still and composed — only 
the head moves. His right hand rests motionless on 
the armrest. Jaw set, eyes narrow slightly as the head 
turn completes. Soft warm light continues to glow on 
his white silk. Background stays sharp and in focus 
with full detail throughout. Single continuous shot.
Audio: quiet court ambience, faint fabric shift from 
head turn, soft breathing, no music.
ENDS WITH: emperor's gaze locked downward, full 
authority, stillness."

ACTION EXAMPLE — Mace vs Shield:
"The warrior swings his mace very hard onto the 
nearest soldier's shield."
(13 words — Grok handles impact, debris, reactions, 
camera, sound — all automatically and realistically)


ALL 6 CLIPS REWRITTEN FOR GROK:
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

🧠 AI PSYCHOLOGY - HOW TO WRITE PROMPTS THAT ACTUALLY
WORK
╔══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╗
║                                                              ║
║   ⚠️ READ THIS SECTION CAREFULLY - IT'S THE SECRET TO        ║
║      GENERATING PROMPTS THAT GET PERFECT RESULTS!           ║
║                                                               ║
║   These are the psychological principles that make AI       ║
║   (Gemini/Grok) respond correctly. APPLY THEM ALWAYS!       ║
║                                                               ║
╚══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╝

🔑 PRINCIPLE 1: POSITION MATTERS - FIRST & LAST = MOST IMPORTANT
AI reads prompts like humans read newspapers:

FIRST 20% = HEADLINE (Highest attention)
├── Put THE most critical instruction here
├── State the main subject immediately
├── Primary constraints go here

MIDDLE 60% = Body (Standard attention)
├── Details and elaboration
├── Secondary information

LAST 20% = CONCLUSION (Second highest attention)
├── REPEAT critical requirements
├── "ENDS WITH" section is CRUCIAL
├── Final locks reinforce constraints

RULE: If it MUST happen, say it at START and END!

🔑 PRINCIPLE 2: USE POWER WORDS - AI PRIORITIZES STRONG LANGUAGE
STRONGEST (AI will NOT ignore):
├── MUST / MUST NOT
├── EXACTLY / PRECISELY
├── CRITICAL / MANDATORY
├── NEVER / ALWAYS
├── ONLY / EXCLUSIVELY

AVOID (AI may ignore):
├── should / could / might
├── probably / maybe
├── try to / consider
├── preferably / ideally
└── These are WEAK - AI treats as optional!

EXAMPLE:
❌ "The book should probably stay closed"
✅ "Book MUST remain CLOSED - MANDATORY"

🔑 PRINCIPLE 3: POSITIVE FRAMING - TELL AI WHAT TO DO
AI processes POSITIVE instructions better than NEGATIVE!
"DO NOT add fog" → AI thinks about fog → May add fog!
"Air is CRYSTAL CLEAR" → AI thinks clear → Stays clear!

REFRAME ALL NEGATIVES:
❌ "Don't add extra people"      → ✅ "ONLY [X] people appear"
❌ "No fog or haze"              → ✅ "CRYSTAL CLEAR visibility"
❌ "Don't change lighting"       → ✅ "MAINTAIN lighting exactly"
❌ "Character shouldn't morph"   → ✅ "Character LOCKED - consistent"

🔑 PRINCIPLE 4: NUMBERS ARE ANCHORS - SPECIFIC = CONTROL
Every vague word = AI guesses randomly!
Every specific number = AI follows precisely!

VAGUE → SPECIFIC:
├── "soldiers" → "EXACTLY 12 soldiers"
├── "moves forward" → "moves forward 8 INCHES"
├── "warm light" → "warm light at 65%"
├── "briefly pauses" → "pauses for 1.5 SECONDS"
├── "large book" → "book approximately 12 x 9 inches"
├── "close to camera" → "15 FEET from camera"

THE MORE NUMBERS, THE MORE CONTROL!

🔑 PRINCIPLE 5: REDUNDANCY FOR CRITICAL INFO - SAY IT 3 TIMES
AI attention "leaks" - important things get forgotten!
SOLUTION: Mention critical info THREE TIMES!

CRITICAL: "Book must stay CLOSED"

MENTION 1 (Subject description):
"Character holds the CLOSED ancient book (clasp secured)"

MENTION 2 (Locks section):
"Book remains CLOSED throughout - zero pages visible"

MENTION 3 (Ends with):
"ENDS WITH: Book STILL CLOSED against chest"

THREE MENTIONS = AI will NEVER forget!

🔑 PRINCIPLE 6: CHARACTER LOCK - PREVENT MORPHING
╔══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╗
║ THE #1 REASON CHARACTERS "MELT" IN VIDEO:                   ║
║ No character description in video prompt!                     ║
║                                                               ║
║ Grok doesn't remember what character looks like!            ║
║ You MUST describe them in EVERY video prompt!               ║
╚══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╝
EVERY video prompt with a person MUST include:

CHARACTER LOCK:
• FACE: [beard/expression/skin tone]
• HEAD: [hair or cap - type, color]
• CLOTHING: [each layer with colors]
• POSTURE: [body angle]
→ "MUST remain CONSISTENT - no morphing"

🔑 PRINCIPLE 7: GROUNDING TO ATTACHED IMAGE
Video prompts need GROUNDING to the attached image!

UNGROUNDED (Vague):
"The character moves forward"

GROUNDED (Clear reference):
"The character shown in the attached image - elderly man
with grey beard, holding book at chest level - now moves
forward from his current position..."

GROUNDING PHRASES:
├── "The attached image shows..."
├── "Starting from the position in attached image..."
├── "Continuing from the frame shown..."
├── "The [subject] visible in attached image now..."

GROUNDED = AI has reference = Better results!

🔑 PRINCIPLE 8: CONFIDENT LANGUAGE - NO UNCERTAINTY
UNCERTAIN prompts = UNCERTAIN results!
CONFIDENT prompts = PRECISE results!

❌ UNCERTAIN:
"The character is maybe around 70, probably wearing
something like a shawl, possibly grey or brown"

✅ CONFIDENT:
"72-year-old man wearing grey-brown woolen shawl"

❌ UNCERTAIN:
"Camera might slowly push in, perhaps about a foot"

✅ CONFIDENT:
"Camera pushes in EXACTLY 12 inches over 6 seconds"

RULE: Write like you're giving ORDERS, not suggestions!
🔑 PRINCIPLE 9: FILL ALL GAPS - UNSPECIFIED = RANDOM
EVERY unspecified detail = AI INVENTS randomly!

You don't specify beard → AI invents random beard
You don't specify which hand → AI picks randomly
You don't specify distance → AI places anywhere

GAP CHECKLIST for characters:
☐ Face: beard, expression, features
☐ Head: hair/cap type, color
☐ Clothing: each layer with color
☐ Posture: body angle, stance
☐ Hands: which hand, holding what, position
☐ Direction: which way facing

LEAVE NO GAPS for things that matter!

🔑 PRINCIPLE 10: PERFECT STRUCTURE = PERFECT RESULTS
AI LOVES consistent structure!

MESSY PROMPT → AI confused → Random results
STRUCTURED PROMPT → AI clear → Precise results

THE STRUCTURE (Use for EVERY prompt):

1. HOOK LINE (First - most important thing)
2. SCENE CONTEXT (Grounding)
3. SUBJECTS (With counts, positions, descriptions)
4. ENVIRONMENT (Location, background, atmosphere)
5. LIGHTING (Sources, directions, percentages)
6. ACTION (For video - what happens, with specifics)
7. CHARACTER LOCK (Full description to maintain)
8. LOCKS (Stated positively - what stays constant)
9. ENDS WITH (Critical - exact final state)
10. TECHNICAL (Duration, aspect ratio, style)

📊 EFFECTIVENESS MULTIPLIERS - APPLY ALL FOR 100X RESULTS
| Element            | Without        | With                | Boost |
|-------------------|----------------|---------------------|-------|
| Hook line         | Buried info    | Lead with critical | 3x     |
| Power words       | "should"       | "MUST/EXACTLY"      | 2x    |
| Positive framing | "Don't add"     | "ONLY/MAINTAIN"     | 2x    |
| Number anchors    | "some"         | "EXACTLY 12"        | 5x    |
| Structure         | Random         | Consistent format   | 2x    |
| Redundancy        | Once           | 3 times             | 3x    |
| Character lock    | None           | Full description    | 10x   |
| Confident tone    | "maybe"        | Definitive          | 2x    |
| Grounding         | Vague          | "attached shows"    | 2x    |
| ENDS WITH         | Missing        | Detailed            | 3x    |
|-------------------|----------------|---------------------|-------|
| COMBINED          |                |                     | 100x |

THE 7 LAYERS OF SCREEN ACTING
Every character in every frame MUST have ALL 7 layers described:
┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓
┃ LAYER 1: EYES (The Soul - 40% of emotional communication)   ┃
┣━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┫
┃ Direction    │ Where looking? Camera/ground/left/up/distant ┃
┃ State        │ Wide/narrowed/squinting/half-closed/closed   ┃
┃ Moisture     │ Dry/glistening/tears forming/tears falling   ┃
┃ Intensity    │ Soft gaze/hard stare/darting/frozen/vacant   ┃
┃ Inner Life   │ What are they THINKING? (shows through eyes) ┃
┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛

┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓
┃ LAYER 2: EYEBROWS (The Emotion Amplifier)                   ┃
┣━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┫
┃ Position     │ Raised/lowered/furrowed/asymmetric (one up) ┃
┃ Tension      │ Relaxed/tense/twitching/frozen               ┃
┃ Movement     │ Static/slowly raising/quick furrow/trembling ┃
┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛

┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓
┃ LAYER 3: MOUTH & JAW (The Feeling)                          ┃
┣━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┫
┃ Lips         │ Parted/pressed/trembling/bitten/pursed       ┃
┃ Corners      │ Up (joy)/down (sorrow)/neutral/asymmetric    ┃
┃ Jaw          │ Clenched/relaxed/dropped/grinding/jutting    ┃
┃ Breathing    │ Mouth/nose, visible/subtle, rate (fast/slow) ┃
┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛
┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓
┃ LAYER 4: FACE MUSCLES (The Subtext)                         ┃
┣━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┫
┃ Forehead     │ Smooth/lined/creased/veins visible           ┃
┃ Cheeks       │ Flushed/pale/hollowed/puffed/tear-stained    ┃
┃ Nose         │ Flared nostrils/scrunched/normal/sniffling   ┃
┃ Chin         │ Trembling/set firmly/jutting/withdrawn       ┃
┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛

┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓
┃ LAYER 5: HEAD & NECK (The Direction of Attention)           ┃
┣━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┫
┃ Tilt         │ Straight/tilted left/right/forward/back      ┃
┃ Turn         │ Degree of rotation (15°/45°/90°/profile)     ┃
┃ Neck         │ Tendons visible/relaxed/straining/veins show ┃
┃ Movement     │ Still/nodding/shaking/slow turn/quick snap   ┃
┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛

┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓
┃ LAYER 6: SHOULDERS & TORSO (The Emotional Weight)           ┃
┣━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┫
┃ Shoulders    │ Raised (fear)/dropped (defeat)/squared (conf)┃
┃ Lean         │ Forward (interest)/back (fear)/sideways      ┃
┃ Chest        │ Rising how much? How fast? (panic vs calm)   ┃
┃ Posture      │ Straight/hunched/twisted/collapsed/rigid     ┃
┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛

┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓
┃ LAYER 7: HANDS (The Truth-Teller - hardest to fake)         ┃
┣━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┫
┃ Position     │ Exactly where? (at sides/chest/face/pockets) ┃
┃ Fingers      │ Spread/curled/interlocked/pointing/gripping ┃
┃ Grip         │ White-knuckle/loose/trembling/relaxed        ┃
┃ Gesture      │ Clutching/reaching/shielding/hiding/open     ┃
┃ State        │ Steady/shaking/frozen/fidgeting/clenched     ┃
┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛



EMOTION-TO-ACTING MASTER TABLE
Use this table to translate EMOTION → PHYSICAL ACTING:
┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┳━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┳━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┳━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┳━━━━━━━━
━━━━━━━━━┳━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓
┃ EMOTION      ┃ EYES            ┃ EYEBROWS       ┃ MOUTH           ┃ BODY
┃ HANDS           ┃
