## Step 3.0F: REALISM & BEAUTY GUIDELINES (CRITICAL!)

### ⚠️ ELEVATED REALISM - Not Fantasy, Not Boring!
### 🌟 CINEMATIC BEAUTY PRINCIPLE (CORE RULE!)

**EVERY frame must be visually BEAUTIFUL - regardless of setting!**

Mass audience dekhe gi → Attract karna hai → BEAUTIFUL visuals!
Reality + Beauty = CINEMATIC REALITY
Even “ugly” settings should look BEAUTIFUL on screen. This is CINEMA, not documentary!

---

**THE BALANCE:**

❌ TOO UGLY ✅ CINEMATIC BEAUTIFUL ❌ TOO FAKE (Disgust audience) (Attract
audience) (Unbelievable) ↓ ↓ ↓ Raw poverty/filth Humble but CHARMING Rich/glamorous
Garbage everywhere Clean, simple Unrealistic Depressing, dark Warm, inviting Fantasy-
like Realistic but ugly Beautiful but real Beautiful but fake

---

**CINEMATIC BEAUTY RULES:**

🎬 RULE: Make EVERY setting look like a movie frame!
  1.   CLEAN ATMOSPHERE

        –  No garbage/kachra unless story requires
        –  No sewage/dirty water visible
        –  Air should be clean (no pollution haze)
        –  Ground reasonably clean
  2.   WARM/BEAUTIFUL LIGHTING

         –  Golden hour preferred (sunrise/sunset glow)
         –  Soft, flattering light on faces
         –  Avoid harsh, ugly shadows
         –  Even night scenes should look BEAUTIFUL (moonlight, warm lamps)
  3.   COLOR HARMONY

         –  Colors should be pleasing to eye
         –  Avoid dull, grey, depressing palettes
         –  Add pops of color (flowers, fabrics, painted doors)
         –  Warm tones preferred for positive scenes
  4.   CHARACTER IN SETTINGS

         –   Old walls = “weathered charm” not “crumbling ugly”
         –   Simple homes = “humble warmth” not “depressing poverty”
         –   Markets = “vibrant energy” not “chaotic mess”
         –   Villages = “rustic beauty” not “backward/dirty”
  5.   VISUAL APPEAL ALWAYS

         –   Every frame should be screenshot-worthy
         –   Audience should WANT to look at the screen
         –   Beauty attracts, ugliness repels

---

**SETTING TRANSFORMATIONS:**

| Real Setting | ❌ Ugly Version | ✅ Cinematic Beautiful Version |
|--------------|-----------------|-------------------------------|
| **Gareeb Gali** | Garbage, sewage, broken walls, rats, dark, depressing |
Narrow lane with CHARACTER - old but CLEAN walls, some with faded paint in
warm colors, a few potted plants, clothes drying adding color, warm afternoon
sunlight filtering through, humble but DIGNIFIED |
| **Old Haveli** | Crumbling, spider webs, dark corners, scary, abandoned
feel | Aged with GRACE - weathered walls showing history, dust particles in
golden sunbeams, old furniture with stories, faded grandeur that's BEAUTIFUL
not scary |
| **Village Home** | Mud everywhere, animal dung, flies, mess | Simple but
CHARMING - clean mud walls, colorful rangoli at door, neem tree shade, clean
courtyard, blue sky, peaceful |
| **Local Market** | Overcrowded, dirty, chaotic, garbage | VIBRANT energy -
colorful stalls, organized bustle, variety of goods displayed beautifully,
warm lighting, life and character |
| **Chai Dhaba** | Dirty tables, flies, stained walls | Rustic CHARM - worn
wooden benches with character, steam rising from chai, warm lighting, cozy
atmosphere |
| **Construction Site** | Rubble, dust, ugly mess | Textured environment -
interesting shapes, golden dust in sunbeams, workers as silhouettes,
architectural |
| **Hospital** | Depressing, clinical, harsh lights | Clean, calm, soft
lighting, hopeful atmosphere |
| **Prison/Jail** | Dark, scary, oppressive | Dramatic shadows, shafts of
light, atmospheric, cinematic |

---

**GAREEB GALI - Detailed Example:**

❌ UGLY (Realistic but unwatchable): “A narrow alley in the slums. Garbage piled on sides,
dirty water flowing in open drain, crumbling walls with stains, rats running, dark and
depressing, flies buzzing around rotting waste.”
✅ CINEMATIC BEAUTIFUL (Realistic but attractive): “A narrow lane in an old
neighborhood. The walls are aged - some painted in faded blues and greens that have
weathered beautifully over decades. Clean swept ground with interesting textures. A few
potted plants (marigolds, tulsi) outside doorways add warmth. Colorful clothes dry on lines
overhead, creating natural bunting against the sky. Late afternoon sunlight filters down,
casting warm golden light on the textured walls. Simple, humble, but with quiet dignity and
visual charm. A place with CHARACTER, not just poverty.”

---

**LIGHTING MAKES EVERYTHING BEAUTIFUL:**

Same gareeb gali can look:
  •    UGLY: Harsh midday sun, grey overcast, or dark night
  •    BEAUTIFUL: Golden hour, soft morning light, warm lamp glow
ALWAYS choose beautiful lighting: ├── Golden hour (sunrise/sunset) - warm, flattering
├── Soft morning light - gentle, hopeful ├── Warm interior lamps - cozy, inviting ├──
Moonlight with blue tones - romantic, mysterious ├── Dappled light through trees -
natural, beautiful └── Soft overcast - even, gentle (not grey/depressing)
AVOID ugly lighting: ├── Harsh noon sun (unflattering shadows) ├── Grey, overcast,
depressing ├── Pitch dark (can’t see anything) ├── Flickering fluorescent (ugly, clinical)
└── Flat, even lighting (boring)

---

**ATMOSPHERE MUST BE CLEAN:**

⚠️ CLEAN ATMOSPHERE RULE:
Unless story SPECIFICALLY requires pollution/dust/haze: ├── Air should be CLEAN and
CLEAR ├── No smog or pollution haze ├── No excessive dust in air ├── No fog unless
intentional mood ├── Visibility should be good └── Sky should be visible and pleasant
Even in “dirty” settings:
  •    The PLACE might be humble
  •    But the AIR should be clean
  •    The LIGHT should be beautiful
  •    The FRAME should be attractive

---

**BEAUTY CHECKLIST (Every Frame):**
Before finalizing any visual prompt, verify:

☐ Is the lighting BEAUTIFUL? (Golden hour preferred) ☐ Is the atmosphere CLEAN? (No
unnecessary haze/dust) ☐ Are colors PLEASING? (Warm, harmonious) ☐ Does setting
have CHARACTER? (Charm, not ugliness) ☐ Would audience ENJOY looking at this? ☐ Is
this frame SCREENSHOT-WORTHY? ☐ Does it look like a MOVIE, not documentary?
If NO to any → Revise for more beauty!

---

**ADD TO EVERY VISUAL PROMPT:**

⚠️ CINEMATIC BEAUTY REQUIREMENT: ├── Atmosphere: CLEAN air, no haze/pollution
├── Lighting: BEAUTIFUL [golden hour/soft morning/warm lamp] ├── Colors: WARM
and pleasing, harmonious palette ├── Setting: Shows character/charm, not ugliness ├──
Overall: Every frame should be visually ATTRACTIVE └── This is cinema - audience should
WANT to watch!

---

### ⚠️ ELEVATED REALISM - Not Fantasy, Not Boring!

**THE BALANCE:**

Too Exaggerated ❌ Sweet Spot ✅ Too Simple ❌ (Fantasy/Unbelievable) (ELEVATED
REALISM) (Boring/Ordinary) ↓ ↓ ↓ Palace with 20ft ceilings Scholar’s impressive Just a
regular room Thousands of rare books but believable home with some books Carved
mahogany everywhere Authentic grandeur Nothing special Dramatic volumetric rays
Cinematic but real Flat lighting

**ELEVATED REALISM means:**
- Realistic foundation (could actually exist)
- But ELEVATED to be visually interesting
- Special enough to support a STORY
- Cinematic without being fantasy

---

### THE KEY QUESTION:

“Is this believable for a WEALTHY/SCHOLARLY Pakistani family?”
NOT: “Is this believable for an average family?” NOT: “Is this believable for a palace?”
Our characters are often special people (scholars, collectors, etc.) Their homes should
reflect that - impressive but POSSIBLE.

---

### CHARACTER-APPROPRIATE SETTINGS:

**If character is a SCHOLAR/COLLECTOR (like Danish Sahab):**

✅ ELEVATED REALISTIC:
  •    High ceiling (14-15 feet - old havelis actually had this!)
  •    500-800 books (impressive personal collection, not impossible)
  •    Quality wooden furniture (family heirloom, well-maintained)
  •    One room dedicated to study (scholars actually do this)
  •    Some rare/old items (a collector WOULD have some special pieces)
  •    Beautiful natural lighting (choose the right time of day)
This is BELIEVABLE for a lifelong scholar who spent money on books instead of cars or
other luxuries.

**If character is ORDINARY person:**

✅ REALISTIC:
  •    Normal ceiling (10-12 feet)
  •    Some books, not a collection
  •    Mix of old and new furniture
  •    Regular Pakistani home elements

---

### EXAMPLES - Three Levels:

**STUDY ROOM:**

❌ TOO EXAGGERATED (Fantasy): “Vast chamber with 20-foot ornate ceilings, thousands
of ancient leather-bound manuscripts floor-to-ceiling on carved mahogany shelves,
massive antique desk, dramatic volumetric god rays streaming through gothic windows…”

✅ ELEVATED REALISTIC (Cinematic but believable): “Danish Sahab’s study - a large room
in the old haveli, ceiling about 14 feet high with dark wooden beams. He’d converted this
entire room into his library. Bookshelves line two walls, floor-to-ceiling, holding his life’s
collection - maybe 600-700 books, some quite old, spines in Urdu, Arabic, Persian. Many
are stacked horizontally where he ran out of space. A solid wooden desk, clearly decades
old but well-cared for, sits near the tall window. Papers, a magnifying glass, an old brass
lamp. Late afternoon sun streams through, catching the dust that hangs in the air of this
rarely-disturbed room. This was a scholar’s sanctuary - impressive for a home, but clearly a
home.”

❌ TOO SIMPLE (Boring): “A room with a desk and some books on a shelf. Normal window.
Regular furniture.”

**HAVELI EXTERIOR:**

❌ TOO EXAGGERATED: “Ancient palatial haveli with crumbling gothic towers, massive
iron gates, sprawling grounds…”

✅ ELEVATED REALISTIC: “An old Faisalabad haveli, one of the larger homes in the
mohalla. Two stories of weathered brick, the kind they don’t build anymore. The wooden
door is solid, heavy - clearly the original from when the house was built in the 1940s. Some
architectural details remain - decorative brickwork above the windows, an arched
entrance. The paint has faded but you can tell this was once a proud home. It stands out
from the newer constructions around it - a piece of old Faisalabad still standing.”

❌ TOO SIMPLE: “A regular old house with a door.”


---

### WORD CHOICE - Elevated Realistic:

| Too Exaggerated ❌ | Elevated Realistic ✅ | Too Simple ❌ |
|-------------------|----------------------|---------------|
| "Vast/Massive" | "Large/Spacious" | "Small/Regular" |
| "20-foot ceilings" | "High ceiling, 14-15 feet" | "Normal ceiling" |
| "Thousands of books" | "500-700 books, his life's collection" | "Some
books" |
| "Priceless ancient manuscripts" | "Some quite old, a few rare finds" | "Old
papers" |
| "Carved mahogany masterpiece" | "Solid wood desk, decades old, well-cared
for" | "A desk" |
| "Dramatic volumetric rays" | "Late afternoon sun catching the dust" |
"Light from window" |
| "Decaying gothic grandeur" | "Faded but you can tell it was once proud" |
"Old building" |

---

### THE "BELIEVABLE SPECIAL" TEST:

Ask: “Would I believe this if someone told me their grandfather had this in Pakistan?”
If WEALTHY/SCHOLARLY grandfather: “Dada kitabein collect karte the, unke paas 600+
books thi, ek poora kamra library bana rakha tha” → BELIEVABLE! Scholars DO this!
If PALACE-LEVEL: “Dada ke paas thousands of ancient manuscripts the, 20-foot carved
ceilings…” → NOT BELIEVABLE for a home! That’s a museum!
If TOO ORDINARY: “Dada ke paas kuch books thi shelf pe” → Believable but NOT SPECIAL
enough for our story!

---

### STORY-APPROPRIATE GRANDEUR:

**Match setting impressiveness to CHARACTER:**

| Character Type | Setting Level |
|---------------|---------------|
| Scholar/Collector | Impressive home library, quality furnishings |
| Wealthy family | Large haveli, good condition, some luxuries |
| Middle class | Normal home, mix of old/new |
| Working class | Simple home, practical items |
| Villain (rich) | Modern luxury, somewhat cold/sterile |

**Danish Sahab was a SCHOLAR who:**
- Spent his life collecting books
- Had family money (old haveli)
- Dedicated himself to knowledge

**So his home SHOULD have:**
- Large personal library (impressive but not museum)
- Quality old furniture (family heirlooms)
- Signs of scholarly life (papers, research materials)
- Some rare items (he WAS a collector after all)
- Atmospheric dust and age (adds to mystery)

---

### CINEMATIC ELEMENTS (Keep These!):

**You CAN still have:**

✅ Beautiful lighting (golden hour, morning light) ✅ Atmospheric dust in sunbeams (this
is REAL) ✅ Interesting shadows ✅ Rich colors (aged wood, old books have beautiful
tones) ✅ Textural details (worn leather, aged paper) ✅ Impressive scale (for
wealthy/scholarly characters)

**Just GROUND them in reality:**
“Late afternoon sun streams through the tall window, and you can see dust particles
floating in the beam - this room hasn’t been properly cleaned in months, maybe years.”
vs
“Dramatic volumetric god rays pierce the gothic chamber”
Same visual effect, but one is BELIEVABLE!

---

### FINAL CHECKLIST:

☐ Is this setting SPECIAL enough for a cinematic story? ☐ Is it BELIEVABLE for this
character’s background? ☐ Would a wealthy/scholarly Pakistani family have this? ☐ Does
it have VISUAL INTEREST (lighting, texture, atmosphere)? ☐ Is it GROUNDED in real
Pakistani elements? ☐ Does it SUPPORT the story without distracting from it?


---




═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
🏛️ HISTORICAL & CULTURAL ACCURACY SYSTEM (UNIVERSAL)
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
╔══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╗
║                                                               ║
║   BEFORE generating ANY character reference, AI MUST:        ║
║   1. Identify the story's TIME PERIOD and CULTURE            ║
║   2. Research accurate clothing, hair, headwear for that era ║
║   3. Apply culture-specific details (NOT generic)            ║
║                                                               ║
║   A Mughal prince ≠ A Sikh warrior ≠ A Rajput king           ║
║   ≠ An Ottoman soldier ≠ A modern Pakistani student          ║
║   GET THIS RIGHT or the whole video looks FAKE!              ║
║                                                               ║
╚══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╝

STEP 1: IDENTIFY ERA + CULTURE (Do this FIRST!)
━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
Before writing ANY character prompt, fill this:

ERA & CULTURE CARD:
┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ Time Period: [Exact decade/century — e.g., "1680s"]         │
│ Culture/Region: [e.g., "Mughal India — Deccan campaign"]    │
│ Character's Status: [Royal/Military/Common/Religious/etc.]  │
│                                                              │
│ TYPICAL CLOTHING for this era + status:                      │
│ [Research and list: upper body, lower body, footwear]       │
│                                                              │
│ TYPICAL HEADWEAR for this era + culture:                     │
│ [Specific style — NOT generic "turban"]                     │
│                                                              │
│ TYPICAL FACIAL HAIR for this era + culture:                  │
│ [Beard style, mustache style, clean-shaven?]                │
│                                                              │
│ TYPICAL WEAPONS/ACCESSORIES for this status:                 │
│ [Swords, shields, jewelry, rank markers]                    │
│                                                              │
│ WHAT THIS IS NOT (avoid confusion with):                     │
│ [List similar but DIFFERENT cultures AI might confuse]      │
│                                                              │
└──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

STEP 2: CULTURE-SPECIFIC APPEARANCE GUIDE
━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
AI image generators commonly CONFUSE these cultures. 
Use the "WHAT THIS IS NOT" section to prevent confusion.

COMMON CONFUSIONS TO AVOID:
┌──────────────────────┬──────────────────────────────────────┐
│ If your story is...  │ AI might generate... (WRONG!)        │
├──────────────────────┼──────────────────────────────────────┤
│ Mughal era           │ Sikh turbans, Rajput pagris          │
│ Ottoman era          │ Arab/Persian mix, generic Middle East│
│ British Raj era      │ Modern suits, wrong military uniforms│
│ Ancient Indian       │ Bollywood fantasy costumes           │
│ Modern Pakistani     │ Indian/Bollywood styling             │
│ Medieval Arab        │ Modern Gulf/Saudi look               │
│ Any Muslim character │ Generic "Middle Eastern" look        │
└──────────────────────┴──────────────────────────────────────┘

HOW TO PREVENT CONFUSION IN PROMPTS:
Instead of vague terms, use SPECIFIC descriptions:

❌ VAGUE (AI will guess wrong):
"A man wearing traditional clothing and turban"

✅ SPECIFIC (AI gets it right):
"A Mughal-era nobleman wearing a fitted angarkha (long coat)
with diagonal closure, churidar pajama, and a small Mughal-style
dastar (NOT a Sikh turban — smaller, tilted, with a jeweled
aigrette pin). Jama-style layered fabric in deep indigo."

THE KEY RULE: 
If you write "turban" → AI defaults to Sikh style
If you write "traditional clothes" → AI defaults to generic
ALWAYS describe the EXACT style with era-specific terminology!

STEP 3: HEADWEAR GUIDE (Most Common Confusion)
━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
Headwear is the #1 source of cultural confusion in AI images.

RULE: NEVER just say "turban" — always specify the STYLE:

┌─────────────────┬────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ Culture/Era     │ Describe headwear as:                      │
├─────────────────┼────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ Mughal royalty   │ "Small Mughal dastar/pagri, wrapped neatly│
│                  │  tilted slightly, jeweled pin/aigrette,   │
│                  │  NOT large Sikh-style turban"              │
├─────────────────┼────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ Mughal soldier   │ "Mughal military helmet (top) with mail   │
│                  │  OR simple cloth wrap, battle-practical"   │
├─────────────────┼────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ Sikh warrior     │ "Large Sikh dastar, tall, neatly wrapped, │
│                  │  covering all hair, distinctive peak"      │
├─────────────────┼────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ Rajput king      │ "Rajasthani safa/pagri, large and ornate, │
│                  │  tied with tail hanging, bright colors"    │
├─────────────────┼────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ Ottoman          │ "Ottoman kavuk or wrapped turban,          │
│                  │  tall rounded shape, white fabric"         │
├─────────────────┼────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ Modern Muslim    │ "Simple prayer cap/topi OR no headwear"   │
├─────────────────┼────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ Pathan/Pashtun   │ "Pakol (flat round cap) OR Pashtun lungi  │
│                  │  wrap style turban"                        │
├─────────────────┼────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ No headwear      │ Explicitly state: "NO hat, NO turban,     │
│                  │  NO head covering — bare head with         │
│                  │  [hair description]"                       │
└─────────────────┴────────────────────────────────────────────┘

STEP 4: ERA-SPECIFIC CLOTHING QUICK REFERENCE
━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
(AI should research further based on specific story era,
but these are starting points to prevent major errors)

MUGHAL ERA (1526-1857):
├── Royalty: Angarkha/jama + churidar + patka sash + dastar
├── Military: Lighter angarkha + armor pieces + helmet/cloth
├── Common: Simple kurta + dhoti/pajama + optional pagri
├── Fabrics: Silk, muslin, brocade (for royalty), cotton (common)
├── Colors: Deep jewel tones — indigo, maroon, emerald, gold
└── NO: Jeans, modern kurta cuts, Nehru jackets, leather jackets

MODERN URBAN PAKISTANI (2000s-2020s):
├── Casual: Jeans/chinos + t-shirt/polo + sneakers/chappal
├── Semi-formal: Shalwar kameez, kurta + jeans
├── Formal: Suit, or sherwani for events
├── Students: Mix of Western + desi, brands, phone in hand
└── NO: Medieval clothing, overly traditional unless character calls for it

BRITISH RAJ ERA (1858-1947):
├── Indian elite: Sherwani + churidar + Jinnah cap/fez
├── Indian common: Dhoti-kurta, or shalwar kameez
├── British: Military uniforms (era-specific), pith helmets
├── Freedom fighters: Simple khadi clothing, Gandhi cap
└── NO: Modern fits, synthetic fabrics, current fashion

OTTOMAN ERA (1300-1922):
├── Royalty: Kaftan + sash + kavuk headwear
├── Military: Janissary uniform or sipahi armor
├── Common: Simple entari + şalvar
└── NO: Arab thobe, modern Turkish clothing

GENERAL RULE: When in doubt, describe clothing in EXTREME detail
in the prompt. Don't assume AI knows any era correctly!

REAL-WORLD TEST FAILURES (LEARN FROM THESE!):
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
These ACTUALLY happened during testing. Prevent them!

FAILURE 1: Mughal prince generated as Sikh
- CAUSE: Prompt said "turban" → Gemini defaulted to Sikh style
- FIX: Say "small Mughal dastar (NOT Sikh turban)" explicitly

FAILURE 2: Maratha sardaar generated as Japanese samurai  
- CAUSE: Prompt said "warrior with topknot" → Gemini saw Japanese
- FIX: Say "Maratha pheta headwrap, dhoti, angavastra 
  (NOT Japanese, NOT samurai, NOT kimono)"

FAILURE 3: Mughal-era character wearing Nehru jacket
- CAUSE: AI confused "South Asian formal" with 1950s style
- FIX: Say "Mughal angarkha coat with diagonal closure
  (NOT modern Nehru jacket, NOT blazer, NOT suit)"

FAILURE 4: All characters looking similar despite different refs
- CAUSE: AI generated same "default handsome South Asian" face
- FIX: Emphasize DISTINGUISHING features per character:
  "Character A: CLEAN SHAVEN, sharp jaw, lighter skin"
  "Character B: FULL BEARD, wider face, darker skin, SCAR"
  Make differences OBVIOUS so AI can't default to same face

FAILURE 5: Historical scene looking like modern costume drama
- CAUSE: Fabrics too clean, colors too bright, styling too neat
- FIX: Add texture instructions: "Handwoven fabric with visible
  weave, natural dyes (slightly uneven color), dust on boots,
  travel-worn look, pre-industrial era materials"




═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
✨ CHARACTER ATTRACTIVENESS & SCREEN PRESENCE (UNIVERSAL)
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
╔══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╗
║                                                               ║
║   Your HERO must look like a HERO. Not generic. Not plain.  ║
║   Audience watches a face for 10-20 minutes — it MUST be    ║
║   compelling, attractive, and screen-worthy!                 ║
║                                                               ║
║   This applies to ALL characters based on their ROLE.        ║
║                                                               ║
╚══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╝

CHARACTER VISUAL TIER SYSTEM:
━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━

TIER 1 — HERO / HEROINE (Must be ATTRACTIVE):
┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ FACE REQUIREMENTS:                                            │
│ ├── Symmetrical features (balanced face proportions)         │
│ ├── Clear skin, healthy complexion                           │
│ ├── Defined jawline (sharp, not round)                       │
│ ├── Expressive eyes (large-ish, clear, intense)              │
│ ├── Well-groomed facial hair (if applicable)                 │
│ ├── Attractive nose proportional to face                     │
│ └── Overall: Would you cast this face in a movie? YES!       │
│                                                                │
│ BODY REQUIREMENTS:                                             │
│ ├── Fit/athletic build (proportional to character role)      │
│ ├── Good posture (confident, upright)                        │
│ ├── Height appears above average in frame                    │
│ └── Shoulders squared, presence fills the frame              │
│                                                                │
│ PROMPT KEYWORDS THAT HELP:                                     │
│ "extremely handsome" / "strikingly beautiful"                │
│ "sharp defined jawline" / "high cheekbones"                  │
│ "intense captivating eyes" / "magnetic screen presence"      │
│ "model-like facial features" / "cinematic hero look"         │
│ "strong athletic build" / "commanding physical presence"     │
│                                                                │
│ PROMPT KEYWORDS TO AVOID:                                      │
│ "average looking" / "normal" / "regular features"            │
│ (These make AI generate plain, forgettable faces)            │
│                                                                │
│ LIGHTING FOR HEROES:                                           │
│ ├── Key light from 45° (creates flattering shadows)          │
│ ├── Fill light to soften (no harsh unflattering shadows)     │
│ ├── Rim/back light for separation (makes them POP)           │
│ ├── Catch light in eyes (makes eyes alive and compelling)    │
│ └── Overall: Cinematic hero lighting, NOT passport photo!    │
└──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

TIER 2 — SUPPORTING / FRIENDS (Distinctive, not generic):
┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ Each supporting character must be VISUALLY DISTINCT:          │
│ ├── Different face shape from hero (rounder/wider/longer)    │
│ ├── Different build (heavier/thinner/shorter/taller)         │
│ ├── Different facial hair (if hero has beard, one friend     │
│ │   should be clean-shaven or have different style)          │
│ ├── Different clothing colors (easy identification!)         │
│ └── At least ONE memorable physical trait per character       │
│     (scar, glasses, specific hairstyle, distinctive jawline) │
│                                                                │
│ WHY: If two characters look similar, viewer gets confused!   │
│ RULE: Side-by-side, you should tell them apart INSTANTLY     │
│ by silhouette alone (different height, build, headwear)      │
└──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

TIER 3 — VILLAIN / ANTAGONIST (Threatening, memorable):
┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ ├── Can be attractive OR rugged (depends on villain type)    │
│ ├── Must have INTENSITY — eyes that unsettle                 │
│ ├── Darker color palette than hero (blacks, dark greys)      │
│ ├── Something UNSETTLING but subtle (cold eyes, sharp stare) │
│ ├── Physically imposing OR eerily calm (contrast with hero)  │
│ └── NEVER cartoonishly evil — realistic threat is scarier    │
└──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

TIER 4 — MENTOR / ELDER (Wise, weathered, trustworthy):
┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ ├── Age-appropriate features (wrinkles, grey hair)           │
│ ├── Kind but strong eyes (wisdom visible)                    │
│ ├── Dignified posture (not frail unless story requires)      │
│ ├── Warmer colors in clothing (browns, whites, creams)       │
│ └── Face tells a story of experience                         │
└──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

CHARACTER DIFFERENTIATION CHECKLIST:
━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
Before generating references, fill this for ALL characters:

┌────────────┬──────────┬──────────┬───────────┬─────────────┐
│ Character  │ Build    │ Face     │ Facial    │ Signature   │
│            │          │ Shape    │ Hair      │ Color       │
├────────────┼──────────┼──────────┼───────────┼─────────────┤
│ [Hero]     │ Athletic │ Sharp    │ [Style]   │ [Color]     │
│ [Friend 1] │ Heavy    │ Wider    │ [Style]   │ [Color]     │
│ [Friend 2] │ Lean     │ Angular  │ [Style]   │ [Color]     │
│ [Villain]  │ Imposing │ Hard     │ [Style]   │ [Color]     │
└────────────┴──────────┴──────────┴───────────┴─────────────┘

RULE: NO two characters should share the same Build + Face 
Shape + Facial Hair combination. Each must be INSTANTLY 
distinguishable!

AI MUST create this differentiation table BEFORE writing any
character reference prompts!


## Step 3.1: Character Reference Prompts

After setup approval:

"Now I'll provide character reference prompts. Generate these images FIRST
using Gemini (Nano-Banana-Pro) - these will be your character references
throughout the video.

**IMPORTANT NOTES:**
- These are MAIN characters who will appear on screen
- They must be visually ATTRACTIVE and ENGAGING (hero-like presence)
- Suitable for commercial cinematic production
- Generate and SAVE these images permanently"

---

## Step 3.2: Important Object Reference Prompts

**After character references, identify and create OBJECT references!**

Any object that will appear in MULTIPLE scenes needs its own reference image:

**IMPORTANT OBJECTS INCLUDE:**
- Books (especially mysterious/important ones)
- Maps, scrolls, documents
- Keys, lockets, jewelry
- Weapons, tools
- Vehicles (if recurring)
- Any plot-critical item

**For each important object, create:**

📦 OBJECT REFERENCE: [Object Name]
OBJECT PROMPT (Gemini (Nano-Banana-Pro)): “[Extremely detailed description of the
object]
Example - Mysterious Book: A ancient leather-bound book approximately 8x10 inches,
dark brown worn leather cover with intricate gold embossed Islamic geometric patterns.
The spine shows age cracks. A brass clasp with green patina holds it closed. The edges of
pages are gilded gold but tarnished. Corners are worn. The cover has a subtle raised
symbol in the center - a crescent moon with a star. The leather texture shows decades of
handling. Photographed on dark wood surface, dramatic lighting from left side, high detail,
photorealistic, 16:9 aspect ratio.“

📁 Save as: OBJ_[Object]_reference.png


**WHEN TO USE OBJECT REFERENCES:**

| Situation | What to Attach |
|-----------|----------------|
| Object alone in frame | Object_reference.png only |
| Object + Environment | Object_reference.png + lastframe/environment |
| Object + Character | Object_reference.png + Character_reference.png |
| Object + Character + Environment | All three references (if AI supports) OR
generate NEW combined image |

**OBJECT + CHARACTER COMBINATION:**

When character HOLDS or INTERACTS with important object:

Generate NEW COMBINED IMAGE:
IMAGE PROMPT: “Zain (from CHAR_[Hero]_Protagonist_ref.png - 28-year-old Pakistani man,
medium brown skin, short black hair, olive green shirt) holding the ancient book (from
OBJ_[Object]_reference.png - dark brown leather, gold embossed patterns, brass
clasp) in his hands. He is examining it carefully. The setting is the dim study room with
bookshelves in background. Golden light from left side illuminates his face and the book.
Medium shot, waist up. 16:9, photorealistic, cinematic warm golden lighting.”

📁 Save as: Clip_[X.X]_[Hero]_with_[Object].png


---
### ⚠️ OBJECT CONTINUITY RULES:

☐ Important objects MUST have dedicated reference images ☐ Same object must look
IDENTICAL across all scenes ☐ When object appears with character → Generate combined
image ☐ Track object’s condition (if it changes - new reference needed) ☐ Object details
(size, color, features) must stay consistent

---

## Step 3.3: Environment Reference Images (Optional)

For locations that appear multiple times, create environment references:

📍 ENVIRONMENT REFERENCE: Danish Sahab’s Study
ENVIRONMENT PROMPT: “Interior of ancient Pakistani haveli study room from 1940s era.
Dark mahogany bookshelves floor-to-ceiling on three walls, thousands of old leather-bound
books. Ornate carved ceiling 20 feet high with Islamic geometric patterns. Three tall arched
windows on right wall with faded green velvet curtains, golden afternoon light streaming
through. Carved mahogany desk in center-left. Faded Persian rug in deep red and gold.
Brass chandelier with melted candles. Grandfather clock in corner. Wide shot, no people,
16:9, photorealistic, warm golden lighting.”

📁 Save as: ENV_[Location]_[Room]_reference.png


---

### ⚠️ REVEALING UNKNOWN OBJECT CONTENTS (Books, Boxes, Containers)

**THE PROBLEM:**
When a character opens a book, box, or container - the INSIDE is not in any
reference image! Grok will randomly generate contents.

**SOLUTION: Describe INTERIOR contents in EXTREME detail:**

✅ CORRECT - Opening a Book:
“Zain’s hands carefully open the ancient leather-bound book. The pages inside are revealed:
INTERIOR CONTENT DETAILS:
  •    Pages are thick, cream-colored parchment, aged to light brown at edges
  •    The text is handwritten in black ink, dense calligraphic style
  •    Each page has ornate red and gold borders with geometric patterns
  •    LEFT PAGE: Hand-drawn illustration - a map showing mountain ranges with a
       marked location (red X symbol near a river)
  •    RIGHT PAGE: Text surrounding a circular diagram with astronomical symbols
       (crescent moons, stars in specific pattern)
  •    Book binding visible in center - old brown thread stitching
  •    Some pages have small annotations in margins (different, red ink)
  •    A faded ribbon bookmark (deep red) marks this page
Zain’s fingers gently touch the edge of the page as he examines the map. His face shows
recognition - eyebrows raised, eyes widening.“



✅ CORRECT - Opening a Box/Container:
“Ayaan slowly lifts the lid of the ornate wooden box.
INTERIOR CONTENT DETAILS:
  •    Box interior is lined with faded red velvet, worn at corners
  •    ITEM 1: A brass key (4 inches long, ornate handle with crescent moon design, green
       patina on metal surface)
  •    ITEM 2: Folded aged paper (cream colored, brown at edges, folded into quarters,
       wax seal broken)
  •    ITEM 3: Small glass vial with dark liquid (cork stopper, liquid is deep amber color,
       half full)
  •    Items rest on velvet, leaving slight impressions
  •    Dust particles rise as lid opens, catching the light
Ayaan’s hand hovers over the contents, hesitating. He reaches for the key first, expression
showing cautious curiosity.“

**RULE:** If something is being OPENED or REVEALED - describe interior
contents like a detailed inventory! Grok needs to know EXACTLY what to
generate.

---

### ⚠️ CHARACTER DIALOGUE - Clear Lip Sync & Reactions

**SINGLE CHARACTER SPEAKING:**

“Zain speaks to camera/other character:
ZAIN (Emotion: worried, Language: Urdu): ‘Yeh jagah kuch ajeeb hai.’
DIALOGUE VISUAL REQUIREMENTS:
  •   Face clearly visible (at least medium shot)
  •   Lips move naturally forming words: ‘yeh’ ‘jagah’ ‘kuch’ ‘ajeeb’ ‘hai’
  •   Jaw and mouth movements clear and visible
  •   Eyebrows slightly furrowed showing concern
  •   Eyes dart briefly to left as he speaks ‘ajeeb’
  •   Delivery: Slow, deliberate, pause before ‘ajeeb’ for emphasis
Duration: ~2.5 seconds speaking, remainder for reaction/visuals“

---

### 🚨 MULTIPLE CHARACTERS HANDLING (CRITICAL!)

**THE PROBLEM WITH MULTIPLE CHARACTERS:**
When 2+ characters are in frame, Grok often:
- Randomly switches their positions (left becomes right)
- Adds extra random people who shouldn't be there
- Makes characters walk BACKWARDS
- Creates weird/unnatural expressions
- Loses track of who is who
- Morphs features between characters

**SOLUTION: STRICT CHARACTER PLACEMENT SYSTEM**

---

**RULE 1: Maximum 2 Characters Per Clip (STRONGLY Recommended)**

✅ IDEAL: 1-2 characters in frame ⚠️ DIFFICULT: 3 characters (use only when essential)
❌ AVOID: 4+ characters (Grok WILL mess up)
For scenes with 3+ characters:
  •   Use over-shoulder shots (1-2 in focus, others blurred)
  •   Cut between different pairs
  •   Separate clips for each speaking character

---

**RULE 2: EXPLICIT Position Mapping (MANDATORY)**

EVERY multi-character clip MUST include position map:

⚠️ CHARACTER POSITION LOCK:
┌─────────────────────────────────────┐ │ FRAME │ │ │ │
┌─────────┐ ┌─────────┐ │ │ │ AYAAN │ │ HAMZA │ │ │ │ (LEFT) │ │ (RIGHT) │
│ │ └─────────┘ └─────────┘ │ │ ↓ ↓ │ │ Facing: RIGHT Facing: LEFT │ │ Jacket:
Charcoal Jacket: Brown │ │ Beard: None Beard: Trimmed │
└─────────────────────────────────────┘
LOCKED POSITIONS - DO NOT CHANGE:
  •   Ayaan stays LEFT entire clip
  •   Hamza stays RIGHT entire clip
  •   NO position switching
  •   NO additional people in frame
  •   NO backwards walking

---

**RULE 3: Strong Character Differentiation**

✅ CORRECT - Clear differentiation: “Ayaan (LEFT side):
  •   Dark charcoal jacket over olive shirt
  •   Clean-shaven face
  •   Serious/thoughtful expression
  •   Slightly taller
Hamza (RIGHT side):
  •   Brown bomber jacket over navy hoodie
  •   Trimmed beard visible
  •   Playful/casual expression
  •   Slightly shorter“

❌ WRONG - Vague differentiation: “Two young men in jackets standing together” (Grok
cannot tell them apart - will create chaos!)

---

**RULE 4: One Clear Speaker Per Moment**

❌ WRONG: “Ayaan and Hamza are both talking and reacting together” (Grok shows both
with moving lips = weird!)

✅ CORRECT: “HAMZA SPEAKS: [dialogue] - lips moving clearly AYAAN LISTENS: reacts
with expression, NO lip movement”

---

**RULE 5: Prevention Statements (Add to EVERY prompt)**
“⚠️ CRITICAL - PREVENT COMMON ERRORS:
  •   Only [Ayaan] and [Hamza] in frame - NO OTHER PEOPLE
  •   Positions LOCKED: Ayaan LEFT, Hamza RIGHT - NO SWITCHING
  •   Both face EACH OTHER naturally - NO backwards walking
  •   NO random crowds or background extras
  •   NO morphing or mixing character features
  •   Natural expressions only - NO exaggerated faces“

---

### ⚠️ DIALOGUE-DRIVEN STORYTELLING (Hollywood Style!)

**THE PROBLEM:**
Current: Story told through NARRATION
Result: Boring! Characters feel like props. Not engaging.

**HOLLYWOOD STYLE (Aladdin, Arabian Nights):**
- Story progresses through CHARACTER DIALOGUE
- Characters SHOW us the story through actions and words
- Narration ONLY for setup, time jumps, transitions
- 80% DIALOGUE, 20% NARRATION

---

**WHEN TO USE NARRATION vs DIALOGUE:**

| NARRATION (Minimal) | DIALOGUE (Primary) |
|--------------------|--------------------|
| Opening setup (first 15-20 sec only) | ALL character interactions |
| Time jumps ("Do saal baad...") | Plot progression |
| Location transitions | Discoveries and revelations |
| Brief montages | Emotional moments |
| Ending wrap-up | Conflict and tension |
| Backstory summary | Decision making |

---

**EXAMPLE - WRONG vs RIGHT:**

❌ WRONG (Narration-Heavy):
CLIP 1: NARRATION “Ayaan aur Hamza bazaar pohonche” CLIP 2: NARRATION “Wahan
unhone ek purani dukaan dekhi”
CLIP 3: NARRATION “Ayaan ko kuch ajeeb laga” CLIP 4: NARRATION “Unhone andar jaane
ka faisla kiya”
BORING! We’re being TOLD everything! Characters are just puppets!
✅ CORRECT (Dialogue-Driven):
CLIP 1: They walk, looking around (visual, no dialogue)
CLIP 2: HAMZA: “Yaar, yeh dekh…” (points at old shop) Ayaan turns, curious expression
CLIP 3: AYAAN: “Yeh jagah… kuch alag hai” HAMZA: “Kya matlab?”
CLIP 4: AYAAN (stepping toward shop): “Pata nahi… andar chalna” Hamza follows,
uncertain
ENGAGING! We EXPERIENCE the story WITH them!

---

**DIALOGUE WRITING - Natural Speech:**

❌ WRONG (Robotic/formal): “Mujhe is jagah par kuch ajeeb ehsaas ho raha hai” “Kya tum
meri baat sun rahe ho?”

✅ CORRECT (Natural/casual): “Yaar… kuch ajeeb lag raha hai” “Sun raha hai na?”
Characters should talk like REAL people, not news anchors!

---

**CHARACTER VOICE - Each Sounds Different:**

HAMZA (Casual, fun):
  •    Uses “yaar” often
  •    Teasing/joking tone
  •    Short sentences
  •    Examples: “Chal na yaar”, “Kya scene hai?”, “Tu bhi na…”
AYAAN (Thoughtful, serious):
  •    More measured speech
  •    Thinks before speaking
  •    Longer, careful sentences
  •    Examples: “Mujhe lagta hai…”, “Ruko, pehle sochte hain”, “Yeh theek nahi hai”

---

**SCENE STRUCTURE - Dialogue-Driven Example:**
SCENE: Discovery at the old bookshop
CLIP 1: (Visual) - Both enter shop, look around CLIP 2: HAMZA: “Bhai, yeh jagah toh…”
(trails off, looking) CLIP 3: (Visual) - Ayaan notices something on shelf CLIP 4: AYAAN: “Yeh
dekh” (picks up old book) CLIP 5: HAMZA: “Kya hai?” (moves closer) CLIP 6: AYAAN: “Yeh…
Dada ki handwriting hai” CLIP 7: (Visual) - Hamza’s shocked reaction CLIP 8: HAMZA:
“Kya?! Yahan kaise?” CLIP 9: AYAAN: “Pata nahi… lekin yeh unhi ka kaam hai” CLIP 10:
(Visual) - Both stare at book, tension
Story moved through DIALOGUE! Viewer is engaged!

---

### MULTI-CHARACTER PROMPT TEMPLATE:

VIDEO PROMPT (Grok): Attach: [image/lastframe]
The attached image shows [scene description with EXACT character positions].

⚠️ CHARACTER POSITION LOCK (DO NOT CHANGE):
┌─────────────────────────────────────┐ │ [CHAR A - LEFT] [CHAR B -
RIGHT]│ │ Facing: Right Facing: Left │ │ Clothing: [X] Clothing: [Y] │ │ Feature: [X]
Feature: [Y] │ └─────────────────────────────────────┘

⚠️ CRITICAL RULES:
  •    ONLY these 2 characters in frame
  •    NO additional people/extras
  •    NO position switching
  •    NO backwards movement
  •    Positions LOCKED entire clip
DIALOGUE: [CHAR A] SPEAKS: “[Line]”
  •    Lips move clearly, [expression]
  •    Duration: X seconds
[CHAR B] REACTS (NO lip movement):
  •    [Reaction description]
  •    [Body language]
Camera Movement: [description]
The shot ends with: [A still on LEFT, B still on RIGHT]
[X] seconds, 16:9, [lighting].
---

**DIALOGUE EXCHANGE:**

⚠️ 6 SECONDS = LIMITED DIALOGUE!

✅ FITS (Single line + reaction): HAMZA: “Yeh dekh” (2 sec) [Shows object] (2 sec) Ayaan’s
reaction (2 sec)

✅ TIGHT FIT (Two short lines): HAMZA: “Yeh kya hai?” (1.5 sec) AYAAN: “Pata nahi” (1
sec) Both examine (3.5 sec)

❌ TOO MUCH (Won’t fit): HAMZA: “Yeh kitaab bohot purani lagti hai” AYAAN: “Haan,
shayad Dada ki hai yeh” HAMZA: “Lekin ismein likha kya hai?” (12+ seconds! Split into 3
clips!)

---

### ⚠️ 6-SECOND CLIP CONTENT LIMITS

**WORD COUNT GUIDE:**

| Pace | Words/6 sec | Best For |
|------|-------------|----------|
| Slow/Dramatic | 10-15 words | Emotional moments, reveals |
| Normal | 15-20 words | Standard narration |
| Fast | 20-25 words | Action scenes (not recommended) |

**EXAMPLES:**

✅ PERFECT (16 words, normal): “Faisalabad ke is purane mohalle mein, log ise ‘Kitaabon
wala ghar’ kehte the.”

✅ GOOD (11 words, dramatic): “Dada gayab ho gaye. Koi alvida nahi. Bas… khamoshi.”

❌ TOO LONG (38 words - needs 2 clips!): “Faisalabad ke is purane mohalle mein ek haveli
hai jise log Kitaabon wala ghar kehte hain aur is ghar mein rehte the Danish Sahab jo apni
poori zindagi ilm ki talaash mein laga diye.”

**SPLITTING LONG NARRATION:**

CLIP 4.5 - NARRATION STARTS: “Dada ke paas ek kitaab thi… jise woh sabse zyada sambhal
kar rakhte the.” (14 words)
CLIP 4.6 - NARRATION ENDS: “Logon ka kehna tha - us kitaab mein woh raaz hai jo kisi ne
nahi jaana.” (15 words)
---

### ⚠️ PACING BALANCE - Not Too Slow, Not Too Fast!

**THE PROBLEM:**
- Too SLOW: 40-50 sec opening with no dialogue = BORING, viewers leave
- Too FAST: Information dump = CONFUSING, viewers lost
- All pure visual = Story doesn't progress

**THE SOLUTION: Variable Clip Lengths!**

⚠️ CLIPS CAN BE 4-6 SECONDS, NOT ALWAYS 6!
SHORTER clips (4-5 sec) for:
  •    Quick reactions
  •    Transition moments
  •    Cutaway details
  •    Breathing room visuals
FULL clips (6 sec) for:
  •    Narration content
  •    Dialogue delivery
  •    Important actions
  •    Emotional beats

**RECOMMENDED SCENE PACING:**

SCENE EXAMPLE (10 clips, 52 seconds total):
Clip 1: VISUAL establishing (5 sec) - HOOK shot Clip 2: VISUAL atmosphere (4 sec) ←
shorter Clip 3: NARRATION starts (6 sec) Clip 4: NARRATION continues (6 sec) Clip 5:
VISUAL breathing (4 sec) ← shorter Clip 6: DIALOGUE/ACTION (6 sec) Clip 7: REACTION (4
sec) ← shorter Clip 8: NARRATION (6 sec) Clip 9: ACTION (6 sec) Clip 10: VISUAL scene end
(5 sec) ← shorter
Total: 52 seconds (not 60 - tighter, better paced!)

**OPENING SCENE RULE:**

❌ WRONG - Too Slow: Clips 1-4: Pure visual, no narration (24 seconds of nothing!) Clip 5:
Finally narration starts… (Viewer has left by now!)

✅ CORRECT - Engaging: Clip 1: Pure visual HOOK - something intriguing (5 sec) Clip 2:
Atmosphere build (4 sec) Clip 3: NARRATION BEGINS - story starts! (6 sec) Clip 4:
Narration continues (6 sec) Clip 5: Visual beat (4 sec)
Narration within first 15 seconds!

**GOLDEN RATIOS:**

Opening: Narration starts by Clip 3 (within 15 sec) Max pure visual opening: 2-3 clips (10-
15 sec) Scene audio ratio: 50-60% narration/dialogue, 40-50% visual After important info:
1 short visual breathing clip (4 sec) Before climax: Build with shorter clips (4-5 sec each)

---

---

**CHARACTER [NUMBER]: [NAME] - [ROLE]**

**Gemini (Nano-Banana-Pro) Prompt:**

[Detailed character description including:
  •    Age, ethnicity, skin tone
  •    Face features (eyes, hair, facial hair)
  •    Build and height
  •    Expression and vibe
  •    Clothing (specific colors and style)
  •    Pose and framing
  •    “Plain dark background”
  •    “Upper body shot” or “Full body shot”
  •    “[Aspect ratio] aspect ratio”
  •    “Photorealistic, studio lighting, high detail, cinematic portrait, hero-like presence,
       attractive and engaging appearance”]

📁 **Save as:** `[CharacterName]_reference.png`

**Text Description (for non-face clips):**

[Skin tone], [clothing description], [distinctive features]

---

Provide ALL character prompts, then ask:
"Generate these character references and confirm when ready. Share the images
if you'd like feedback."
