## Step 5.1: Generate One Scene at a Time

For each scene, provide:

---

# SCENE [NUMBER]: [SCENE NAME]

**Location:** [Where]
**Time:** [When - lighting condition]
**Duration:** [X seconds] ([X clips])
**Mood:** [Emotional tone]
**Characters:** [Who appears]

---

## CLIP [SCENE.CLIP] - [Clip Name]

### Reference:

[Clearly state what to attach and WHY]
Options:
  •    “Generate NEW IMAGE - First clip of scene”
  •    “Generate NEW IMAGE - Character [Name] face appearing first time”
  •    “Attach: Clip_X.X_lastframe.png - Continuing scene”
  •    “Attach: Clip_X.X_lastframe.png - Hand/body only, describe in text”

### IMAGE PROMPT (Gemini (Nano-Banana-Pro)):

╔══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
════════╗ ║ ║ ║ IMAGE PROMPT MUST BE HYPER-DETAILED! ║ ║ ║ ║ This image
becomes the STARTING FRAME of your video. ║ ║ Every detail you DON’T specify = Gemini
guesses randomly! ║ ║ ║
╚══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
════════╝

**IMAGE PROMPT MANDATORY ELEMENTS:**

  1.   SETTING/LOCATION: ├── Specific place (not just “cave” but “narrow rocky alcove”)
       ├── Time period (1840s, present day, etc.) ├── Time of day (night, golden hour,
       etc.) └── Weather if relevant (light snow, clear, etc.)

  2.   SUBJECTS (with EXACT COUNTS): ├── How many people? “Exactly 5 scholars” not
       “scholars” ├── Positions in frame (LEFT, CENTER, RIGHT) ├── Distance from
       camera (12 feet, 200 feet) ├── What they’re wearing (specific colors, materials)
       ├── What they’re holding (which hand?) └── Body posture (standing, kneeling,
       running)

  3.   KEY OBJECTS: ├── Exact state (book CLOSED, door 25% open) ├── Size (2 inches
       wide, 4 inches tall) ├── Position in frame (center, bottom-left) └──
       Material/texture (brass, leather, rough stone)

  4.   LIGHTING (with PERCENTAGES): ├── Primary light source + direction (warm
       lantern from LEFT 60%) ├── Secondary light + direction (cool moonlight from
       RIGHT 40%) ├── Contrast level (high contrast, soft) └── Color temperature (warm
       orange, cool blue)

  5.   WHAT TO EXCLUDE: ├── Don’t say “NO fog” - instead just don’t mention fog ├──
       State what IS there, not what ISN’T └── Exception: “No readable text” is okay for
       avoiding gibberish

  6.   TECHNICAL: └── “16:9, photorealistic, cinematic, ultra-detailed”


**❌ vs ✅ IMAGE PROMPT COMPARISON:**

═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
═════════ ❌ VAGUE IMAGE PROMPT (Gemini will guess everything):
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
═════════
Close-up on rock wall inside alcove. A scholar carves a simple symbol (NO text): a crescent
above three dots arranged in a triangle. Fresh scratch marks. Hands medium brown, rough.
Lighting: warm lantern from LEFT, cool moonlight from RIGHT.
PROBLEMS: ├── “Close-up” = HOW close? What fills frame? ├── “A scholar carves” =
Carving NOW or ALREADY CARVED? ├── “Hands” = Which hand holds chisel? Which
steadies? ├── “Simple symbol” = How big? 2 inches? 6 inches? ├── “Fresh scratch marks”
= How deep? What color? ├── “Warm lantern” = What percentage? 60%? 80%? └──
Gemini guesses all this = random image!
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
═════════ ✅ DETAILED IMAGE PROMPT (Gemini knows exactly what to create):
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
═════════
1840s Kashmir, interior of a hidden rocky alcove at night.
FRAMING: Close-up shot of a rough grey-brown rock wall surface, filling 80% of frame. The
wall texture shows natural striations and small cracks.
SUBJECT: A scholar’s RIGHT hand grips a small iron chisel (4 inches long, dark metal)
against the rock surface. The hand is medium-brown skin, aged with visible wrinkles,
wearing a simple copper ring on the index finger. The LEFT hand is partially visible at
frame edge, steadying against the rock.
OBJECT STATE: The rock surface is BLANK - no carving marks yet. The chisel tip is
positioned where the first mark will be made, about CENTER of frame.
SPATIAL: The hand is approximately 18 inches from camera. The rock wall fills the
background entirely.
LIGHTING:
  •    Primary: Warm orange lantern light from frame-LEFT (60%)
  •    Secondary: Cool blue moonlight spill from frame-RIGHT (40%)
  •    Creates dramatic shadow of hand on rock surface
  •    High contrast between lit and shadow areas
ATMOSPHERE: Clean air, no fog or haze. A few tiny dust particles visible in the lantern light
beam.
16:9, photorealistic, cinematic, ultra-detailed macro feel.
WHAT’S BETTER: ├── Exact framing: “Close-up, 80% of frame” ├── Exact hand: “RIGHT
hand grips chisel” ├── Exact object: “4 inches long, dark metal” ├── Exact state: “BLANK -
no carving marks yet” ├── Exact lighting: “60% warm LEFT, 40% cool RIGHT” ├── Clear
what IS there (not what isn’t) └── Gemini knows EXACTLY what to create!
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
═════════


📁 Save as: `Clip_[X.X]_image.png`




GROK VIDEO PROMPT SYSTEM (COMPLETE OVERHAUL!)
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
╔══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╗
║                                                               ║
║   THIS SECTION REPLACES ALL PREVIOUS VIDEO PROMPT GUIDANCE   ║
║   Based on: Grok Imagine official guide, community testing,  ║
║   Aurora engine documentation, and REAL user feedback.        ║
║                                                               ║
║   Grok Image-to-Video = Our PRIMARY video generation tool    ║
║                                                               ║
╚══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╝


HOW GROK IMAGE-TO-VIDEO WORKS:
━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
1. You UPLOAD a generated frame image
2. You write a MOTION PROMPT describing what happens
3. Grok ANIMATES the image for 6-15 seconds
4. The image IS the first frame — Grok brings it to life

CRITICAL UNDERSTANDING:
┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ Grok ALREADY HAS the scene from the image!                    │
│ It can SEE: environment, characters, objects, lighting       │
│ It DOES NOT NEED: re-description of static elements          │
│                                                                │
│ Your prompt should ONLY describe:                             │
│ → WHAT MOVES (subject + action)                               │
│ → HOW IT MOVES (intensity, speed, direction)                  │
│ → HOW CAMERA MOVES (pan, zoom, drift)                        │
│ → WHAT WE HEAR (audio, dialogue, SFX)                        │
│                                                                │
│ DO NOT re-describe what's already visible in the image!       │
│ Grok will expand your prompt based on what it SEES.           │
└──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘


GROK VIDEO PROMPT GOLDEN FORMULA:
━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
╔══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╗
║                                                               ║
║   [Subject Feature] + [Action with Intensity Adverb]         ║
║   + [Camera Movement] + [Audio/Atmosphere]                   ║
║                                                               ║
║   KEEP IT: 50-150 words MAX for video prompts!               ║
║   UNDER 200 words ALWAYS!                                    ║
║                                                               ║
║   First 20-30 words = MOST IMPORTANT (Grok prioritizes)     ║
║                                                               ║
╚══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╝

WHY SHORT PROMPTS WIN:
- Grok's Aurora engine EXPANDS short prompts intelligently
- Long prompts OVERLOAD the model → ignores details
- Grok reads the IMAGE for static info — you add MOTION only
- 50-150 word prompts = BEST results (confirmed by testing)

❌ YOUR CURRENT PROBLEM — OVER-DESCRIPTION:
Your current prompts are 300-500 words with:
- 10+ lines re-describing what's already in the image
- Inch-level measurements Grok can't process
- 7-layer acting system too complex for video AI
- Timestamp beats (0-2s, 2-4s) Grok can't follow precisely
- Extensive LOCK sections that don't affect video output

✅ THE FIX — MOTION-FOCUSED, SHORT, INTENSE:
50-150 words focusing ONLY on what CHANGES.


GROK VIDEO PROMPT STRUCTURE (NEW!):
━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━

Every video prompt follows this 5-LAYER structure:

LAYER 1: SUBJECT + MAIN ACTION (First 20-30 words!)
"The young man in indigo coat reaches powerfully toward 
the wooden chest, his expression intense and urgent."

LAYER 2: SECONDARY MOTION + ENVIRONMENT LIFE
"Wind whips through the narrow mountain pass, torch flames 
bend sharply to the right, loose dust lifts from the stone path."

LAYER 3: CAMERA MOVEMENT
"Slow cinematic push-in toward the subject, handheld 
tension feel, slight drift."

LAYER 4: PHYSICAL REALISM (body physics!)
"His coat fabric flutters in the wind, chest rises with 
heavy breathing, weight shifts to his front foot."

LAYER 5: AUDIO DIRECTION
"Audio: howling mountain wind, distant arrow whistles, 
crackling torch fire, no music."


THE 10 COMMANDMENTS OF GROK VIDEO PROMPTS:
━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━

1. DO NOT re-describe the image
   ❌ "The attached image shows a medium-wide night shot 
      on the Deccan Ghaat path with torchlight from the 
      right and cool moonlight from the left creating deep 
      navy shadows and warm amber highlights. Background 
      figures are only silhouettes..."
   ✅ "The young man reaches toward the chest decisively."
   (Grok can SEE the image — it knows the ghaat, the light, 
   the shadows. Just tell it WHAT MOVES.)

2. USE INTENSITY ADVERBS (Grok responds strongly!)
   ❌ "hand moves down" (vague — Grok guesses speed)
   ✅ "hand SLAMS down forcefully" or "hand lowers gently"
   
   POWER ADVERBS: quickly, violently, with large amplitude,
   powerfully, wildly, gently, slowly, gracefully, urgently,
   desperately, cautiously, aggressively, tenderly

3. KEEP UNDER 150 WORDS
   ❌ 400-word prompt with locks and measurements
   ✅ 80-120 word motion-focused prompt
   
   Grok's attention drops SHARPLY after 150-200 words.
   Everything beyond 200 words is likely IGNORED.

4. DESCRIBE BODY PHYSICS (stops "mannequin" look!)
   ALWAYS include at least 2 of these:
   - Fabric/clothing movement: "coat flutters in wind"
   - Breathing: "chest rises and falls with tension"
   - Weight transfer: "shifts weight to front foot"
   - Hair movement: "hair whips across face in wind"
   - Muscle tension: "jaw clenches, neck muscles tighten"
   - Sweat/skin: "torch glow glistens on sweaty forehead"
   
   WITHOUT body physics = robot puppet
   WITH body physics = living person

5. SIMPLIFY ACTING TO 1-2 KEY MOVEMENTS
   ❌ 7-layer system (eyes + brows + mouth + jaw + head 
      + shoulders + hands — TOO MUCH!)
   ✅ ONE primary action + ONE reaction
   
   Example:
   ❌ "Eyes: focused upper-left for 2 seconds, then shift 
      to sandook for 2 seconds, then lift forward. Eyebrows: 
      slight furrow, tightening at mid-clip. Mouth: closed, 
      jaw set. Head: 10-degree turn. Shoulders: squared..."
   ✅ "He stares intently at the chest, then snaps his head 
      up with alarm as he hears the arrow whistle."
   
   Grok will fill in the micro-expressions naturally!
   Trust the AI for subtle acting — direct the BIG moves.

6. NO INCH MEASUREMENTS — USE RELATIVE TERMS
   ❌ "moves 14 inches forward" "6 inches toward" 
      "2 inches above" "10-degree turn"
   ✅ "moves closer" "reaches toward" "hovers just above" 
      "turns his head slightly"
   
   AI does NOT process inches. It processes INTENT.

7. NO TIMESTAMP BEATS — USE SEQUENCE WORDS
   ❌ "Seconds 0-2: gathers breath. Seconds 2-3.5: shouts. 
      Seconds 3.5-6: breathing hard."
   ✅ "First, he gathers his breath with visible tension. 
      Then he SHOUTS the word powerfully. After the shout, 
      he holds position, breathing hard."
   
   SEQUENCE WORDS: First, Then, After that, Meanwhile, 
   In the final moment, As this happens, Simultaneously

8. ANTI-MORPH LOCK (add to EVERY prompt — 1 line!)
   "Single continuous shot, no morphing, no scene changes, 
   no new objects appearing. Everything stays grounded."
   
   This ONE line prevents 80% of morphing issues.

9. FACE CLARITY INSTRUCTION
   When face is visible, add:
   "Face remains sharp and in focus throughout. 
   No motion blur on facial features."

10. WALKING = EXPLICIT LEG INSTRUCTION
    When character walks/moves, ALWAYS say:
    "Walking with visible natural leg movement — feet 
    lift off ground, knees bend with each step, natural 
    human walking gait. NOT sliding."
    
    Without this: characters SLIDE like mannequins on rails.


GROK CAMERA MOVEMENT KEYWORDS:
━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
Grok supports these camera instructions (use natural language):

┌─────────────────────┬────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ Movement            │ How to write in prompt                  │
├─────────────────────┼────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ Push in / Dolly in  │ "Camera slowly pushes in toward..."    │
│ Pull back           │ "Camera gradually pulls back to reveal"│
│ Pan left/right      │ "Camera pans slowly to the right"      │
│ Tilt up/down        │ "Camera tilts upward to reveal sky"    │
│ Orbit / Surround    │ "Camera orbits around the subject"     │
│ Follow / Tracking   │ "Camera follows the running figure"    │
│ Aerial              │ "Aerial view, camera drifts forward"   │
│ Handheld            │ "Slight handheld shake, documentary"   │
│ Zoom                │ "Slow zoom into the subject's face"    │
│ Static              │ "Camera stays completely still"         │
│ Crane up/down       │ "Camera rises smoothly upward"         │
│ Dutch tilt          │ "Camera tilts 15 degrees for unease"   │
└─────────────────────┴────────────────────────────────────────┘

⚠️ When using camera movement, select "UNFIXED LENS" in Grok!
If camera should stay still, select "FIXED LENS."


LIP SYNC / DIALOGUE SYSTEM:
━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
When character SPEAKS in the clip:

STRUCTURE:
"[Character description] shouts the word '[WORD]' loudly 
and urgently. His mouth opens wide for the first syllable 
'[SYL-1]', then closes firmly on '[SYL-2]'. The shout 
is [emotion: desperate/angry/commanding]."

EXAMPLE — Dalawar shouts "Sandook!":
"The bearded man in dark vest SHOUTS 'Sandook!' with 
desperate urgency. Mouth opens wide, jaw drops for 'SAN',
then rounds and closes on 'DOOK'. His pointing arm jabs 
forward during the shout. Voice: loud, raw, commanding."

RULES:
- Break the word into syllables for better lip movement
- State the emotion of the delivery
- Include ONE body movement that accompanies the speech
- Keep dialogue to 1-3 words per 6-second clip MAX
- Longer dialogue = worse lip sync quality


NARRATION IN VIDEO PROMPTS:
━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
Narration is NOT lip-synced — it's voiceover ADDED LATER 
in editing software (CapCut/Premiere/DaVinci).

DO NOT include narration text in the Grok video prompt!
Grok cannot add voiceover narration.

Instead:
1. Write video prompt focusing ONLY on visual motion
2. Generate the video clip
3. Add narration audio in post-production editing

FORMAT IN DEEPFRAME OUTPUT:
┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ 📝 GROK VIDEO PROMPT: [motion-only prompt for Grok]         │
│                                                                │
│ 🎵 AUDIO (add in post-production):                           │
│ - Narration: "[text]" [voice direction]                      │
│ - SFX: [sound effects to layer]                               │
│ - Music: [track continues / changes]                          │
│                                                                │
│ Keep these SEPARATE — Grok prompt ≠ audio plan!              │
└──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘


ENVIRONMENT LIFE CHECKLIST:
━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
Add 2-3 of these to EVERY clip for "alive" feeling:

FIRE/TORCH: "Torch flames bend and flicker in the wind"
WIND: "Wind gusts through the pass, dust lifts, fabric flutters"
WATER: "Water drips slowly from rock surface"
DUST: "Fine dust particles drift through torch light"
SMOKE: "Thin smoke wisps curl upward from torch"
SHADOWS: "Flickering shadows dance on rock wall"
NATURE: "Leaves/branches sway in breeze"
ATMOSPHERIC: "Distant lightning flickers on horizon"

WITHOUT environment life = frozen painting that "moves"
WITH environment life = living, breathing scene


CLIP CHAINING SYSTEM (CONTINUITY):
━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
For multi-clip sequences, use LAST FRAME method:

1. Generate Clip 1.1 from Frame 1 image
2. EXPORT last frame of Clip 1.1 as PNG
3. Use that last frame as INPUT for Clip 1.2
4. Repeat for each subsequent clip

This ensures visual continuity between clips.

IMPORTANT: Each prompt is STANDALONE — Grok has NO memory!
Never reference "previous clip" or "earlier scene."
Always reference "the attached image."


ENDS WITH SECTION (IMPROVED!):
━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
Every clip prompt ends with VISUAL CHECKPOINT:

"ENDS WITH: [Character] is [pose/position], [expression], 
[key object state]. Camera at [position]. 
Energy level: [low/building/peak/settling]."

Example:
"ENDS WITH: The young man's hand hovers just above the 
chest lid, his jaw clenched with determination. Camera 
is slightly closer than start. Energy level: building."

This gives the next clip's input frame a clear target.


═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
BEFORE/AFTER EXAMPLES — REAL PROMPTS TRANSFORMED:
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

EXAMPLE 1: Clip 1.1 (Establishing wide, convoy moving)

❌ BEFORE (your current — 180 words):
"The attached image shows an extreme wide establishing 
view of a Deccan Ghaat mountain pass at night: a narrow 
stone path carved into dark jagged rock, a high rock wall 
on the left, a steep void drop on the right, and a tight 
bend ahead. A small convoy is visible only as distant 
silhouettes with a few torches; no faces are readable. 
The overall color tone is cold navy shadows with warm 
amber torch highlights.

Over 6 seconds, keep this as one continuous shot with no 
cuts or transitions. The camera performs a very slow, 
steady push forward along the path, moving approximately 
12 inches total over the full clip (gentle drift, not 
walking speed). The push-in subtly increases tension 
without changing the scene. The torch flames in the 
distance flicker naturally and bend slightly to the right, 
about 15–20 degrees, as if wind is passing through the 
narrow pass. The silhouettes of the convoy continue 
moving slowly toward the bend..."

✅ AFTER (Grok-optimized — 75 words):
"Convoy silhouettes move slowly toward the mountain bend, 
torches flickering and bending in the wind. Gentle slow 
camera push-in, building tension. Torch flames dance 
naturally, casting shifting warm light on nearby rock. 
Fine dust drifts across the stone path. Wind whistles 
softly through the narrow pass. The convoy inches forward 
cautiously. Single continuous shot, no morphing. 
Audio: low mountain wind, distant torch crackle."

WHY IT'S BETTER:
- 75 words vs 180 (Grok reads it ALL)
- No re-description of image (Grok sees it)
- ACTION first: "convoy moves, torches flicker"
- Environment life: dust, wind, flame dance
- Audio direction included
- Anti-morph lock included
- No inch measurements


EXAMPLE 2: Clip 1.3 (Aurangzab + sandook, face visible)

❌ BEFORE (your current — 290 words with 7-layer acting)

✅ AFTER (Grok-optimized — 95 words):
"The young man in indigo coat slowly lowers his right 
hand toward the wooden chest, fingers tensing as he 
reaches. His expression shifts from alert vigilance to 
focused determination — brow furrows, jaw tightens. His 
chest rises with a controlled deep breath. Wind catches 
his coat hem, torch flames flicker behind him. His left 
hand stays near his sash, ready. Face remains sharp and 
in focus throughout. Slow cinematic push-in, subtle 
handheld feel. Single continuous shot, no morphing.
Audio: torch fire crackle, distant wind, leather creak."

WHY IT'S BETTER:
- 95 words vs 290
- ONE clear action: "lowers hand toward chest"
- ONE emotional shift: "vigilance → determination"
- Body physics: breathing, coat flutter, fingers tensing
- Face sharpness instruction included
- No 7-layer breakdown (Grok handles micro naturally)
- No inch measurements
- Anti-morph included


EXAMPLE 3: Clip 1.6 (Dalawar shouts "Sandook!")

❌ BEFORE (your current — 260 words with timestamp beats)

✅ AFTER (Grok-optimized — 90 words):
"The bearded man in dark vest gathers his breath with 
visible tension, then SHOUTS 'Sandook!' loudly and 
desperately. His mouth opens wide and jaw drops for the 
shout, right arm jabs forward pointing urgently. After 
the shout, he holds position breathing hard, jaw 
clenched, eyes locked forward. His vest fabric shifts 
with the force of the shout. Face remains sharp 
throughout. Slight handheld camera feel, subtle push-in. 
Single continuous shot, no morphing.
Audio: the shout echoes off rock walls, wind, distant 
torch crackle."


═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
VIDEO PROMPT TEMPLATE (COPY THIS FOR EVERY CLIP!):
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

📝 GROK VIDEO PROMPT:
━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
[Subject feature] [MAIN ACTION with intensity adverb]. 
[Secondary motion or reaction]. [Body physics: breathing/
fabric/weight]. [Environment life: wind/fire/dust]. 
[Camera movement type]. Face remains sharp and in focus.
Single continuous shot, no morphing, no new objects.
Audio: [specific sounds].
━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━

ENDS WITH: [Character pose], [expression], [object state]. 
Camera at [position]. Energy: [level].

🎵 AUDIO (post-production):
- Narration: [text if any] [voice direction]
- SFX: [layered sounds]  
- Music: [track note]
━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━


QUICK REFERENCE — VIDEO PROMPT DOs AND DON'Ts:
━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━

┌─────────────────────────────┬───────────────────────────────┐
│ ❌ DON'T                    │ ✅ DO                          │
├─────────────────────────────┼───────────────────────────────┤
│ Re-describe the image       │ Describe only what MOVES      │
│ Write 300+ words            │ Write 50-150 words            │
│ Use inch measurements       │ Use relative terms            │
│ Use timestamp beats         │ Use sequence words            │
│ 7-layer acting breakdown    │ 1-2 key movements only        │
│ 10 lines of LOCKs           │ 1-2 lines of locks max       │
│ Include narration text      │ Separate narration for post   │
│ Say "previous clip"         │ Say "attached image"          │
│ Generic "hand moves"        │ "hand SLAMS down forcefully"  │
│ Forget body physics         │ Breathing, fabric, weight     │
│ Forget environment life     │ Wind, fire, dust, shadows     │
│ Forget anti-morph           │ "no morphing, no new objects" │
│ Forget face sharpness       │ "face remains sharp"          │
│ Forget walking legs         │ "visible leg movement, NOT    │
│                             │  sliding"                     │
│ Use negative prompts        │ Only positive descriptions    │
│ ("no blur", "no fog")       │ (describe what IS there)      │
└─────────────────────────────┴───────────────────────────────┘


GROK LIMITATIONS TO WORK AROUND:
━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━

1. FACES MORPH on close-ups with complex motion
   → Keep face-visible clips to SIMPLE movements
   → Avoid face + fast body action simultaneously
   → Best: face with subtle expression change ONLY

2. HANDS get distorted with complex gestures
   → Avoid detailed hand close-ups
   → Hands holding objects = safer than free gestures
   → "Hand grips sword hilt" > "fingers spread and curl"

3. WALKING looks unnatural / sliding
   → Always add "natural walking gait, feet lift off ground"
   → Or avoid full-body walking shots (use waist-up instead)

4. BUSY SCENES lose coherence
   → Max 2 characters moving simultaneously
   → Others should be still/silhouettes
   → Simple compositions = better motion quality

5. STYLE DRIFT (realistic → cartoon mid-clip)
   → Keep prompts short (less confusion)
   → "Photorealistic, live-action film look" in every prompt
   → Avoid mixing style references

6. NEGATIVE PROMPTS DON'T WORK
   ❌ "No fog, no blur, no cartoon"
   ✅ Just don't mention fog/blur/cartoon at all
   ✅ Describe what IS there positively

7. AUDIO IS AUTO-GENERATED (limited control)
   → Audio direction in prompt is SUGGESTIVE not exact
   → Best approach: add your own audio in post-production
   → Use Grok audio as placeholder/reference only


### VIDEO PROMPT (Grok):

**⚠️ CRITICAL: Video AI Has NO Context!**

The video generation AI (Grok) does NOT know:
- Your story or plot
- Previous scenes or clips
- Character backgrounds
- What happened before
- What will happen next
- What "previous clip" means

**IT ONLY SEES THE CURRENT PROMPT + ATTACHED IMAGE - NOTHING ELSE!**

Therefore, EVERY video prompt must be **EXTREMELY DETAILED** and
**STANDALONE**.

---

### ⚠️ CORRECT WORDING FOR VIDEO PROMPTS

**Grok only understands: "attached image" - NOT "previous clip"!**

❌ **WRONG PHRASES (Grok doesn't understand):**
“Starting from the wide view (from the previous clip)…” “Continuing from where we left
off…” “As seen in the last clip…” “The same room from before…” “Following the previous
shot…” “Building on the earlier scene…”


✅ **CORRECT PHRASES (Reference the attached image):**

“Starting from the wide view of the haveli entrance visible in the attached image, the
camera slowly pushes forward…”
“The scene shown in the attached image - the grand study room with bookshelves and
golden light - the camera now begins to…”
“From the exact frame shown in the attached image, where the desk is visible in the center,
the camera dollies forward…”
“Continuing the camera movement from the position shown in the attached image, slowly
panning right to reveal…”

---

### VIDEO PROMPT WORDING RULES:

| Wrong ❌ | Correct ✅ |
|----------|-----------|
| "from the previous clip" | "from the attached image" |
| "continuing from before" | "from the position shown in attached image" |
| "the same room we saw" | "the room visible in attached image" |
| "as established earlier" | "as shown in the attached image" |
| "following the last shot" | "continuing from the frame in attached image" |
| "where we left off" | "from the exact view in attached image" |

---

### CORRECT VIDEO PROMPT STRUCTURE:

VIDEO PROMPT (Grok): Attach: Clip_X.X_lastframe.png (1st attached image)
[Describe what IS in the attached image first - this grounds Grok] “The attached image
shows [description of what’s visible].”
[Then describe what happens] “From this view, the camera [movement description]…”
[Full scene details - remember Grok has NO context] [Environment, lighting, atmosphere -
ALL details]
[Narration/Dialogue if any - integrated with visuals]
[Camera Movement - specific and clear]
[Last frame description - DETAILED]
6 seconds, [aspect ratio], cinematic.

---

### EXAMPLE - CORRECT VIDEO PROMPT:

VIDEO PROMPT (Grok): Attach: Clip_1.4_lastframe.png (1st attached image)
The attached image shows a wide view of an ancient Pakistani haveli study room - dark
mahogany bookshelves reaching a 20-foot ceiling, golden afternoon light streaming
through three tall arched windows on the right, a carved wooden desk visible in the center-
left of the frame.
From this exact view, the camera begins a slow dolly movement forward toward the
mahogany desk. As the camera advances, more details of the cluttered desk surface become
visible - scattered papers, dark shapes of objects. The golden light intensifies as we move
deeper into the light beams. Tiny specks of dust drift lazily through the diagonal shafts of
sunlight.
NARRATION (Deep male voice, wise, Roman Urdu): “Is ghar mein rehte the Danish sahab…”
Camera Movement: Slow, steady dolly forward from the wide position shown in attached
image, gradually approaching the desk. Movement is smooth and reverent.
The shot ends with: Medium-wide shot with desk now occupying the lower third of frame,
surface details becoming more visible (shapes of papers, metallic objects catching light),
bookshelves framing both sides, golden light prominent.
6 seconds, 16:9 horizontal, cinematic warm golden lighting.

---

### ⚠️ NARRATION & DIALOGUE MUST BE INSIDE VIDEO PROMPT!

**❌ WRONG FORMAT (Narration separate):**

VIDEO PROMPT (Grok): Attach: Clip_1.6_lastframe.png NARRATION: “Dada ne kabhi un
kitaabon ko Zain ke haath nahi lagane dia.” A close-up shot of bookshelf with old dusty
books…


**✅ CORRECT FORMAT (Narration integrated):**

VIDEO PROMPT (Grok): Attach: Clip_1.6_lastframe.png (1st attached image)
A close-up shot of a dark mahogany bookshelf in the study, filled chaotically with
thousands of old, dusty, dark leather-bound books…
NARRATION (Deep male voice, nostalgic tone, Roman Urdu): “Lekin Dada ne kabhi un
kitaabon ko Zain ke haath nahi lagane dia. ‘Beta, pehle taleem mukammal karo’ – Dada
hamesha kehte.”
The narration plays over this visual of locked books - symbolizing knowledge kept away
from Zain. The camera slowly pans across the locked cabinet as the narrator speaks about
Dada’s protective nature.
Camera Movement: Slow horizontal pan across the locked books, timed with narration
pace.
The shot ends with: …
6 seconds, 16:9 horizontal, cinematic warm golden lighting.

### VIDEO PROMPT STRUCTURE (MANDATORY FORMAT):

---

## ⚠️ CRITICAL: ACTION-FOCUSED PROMPTS, NOT LOCK-HEAVY!

╔══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
════════╗ ║ ║ ║ PROBLEM: Prompts have 10 lines of LOCKS, 1 line of ACTION ║ ║
RESULT: Grok doesn’t know what to do, makes up actions! ║ ║ ║ ║ SOLUTION: ACTION
must be the MAIN FOCUS of prompt! ║ ║ Locks should be BRIEF (3-4 lines max) ║ ║ ║
╚══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
════════╝

---

### ❌ WRONG - Lock-Heavy, Action-Light:

The attached image shows a roadside Pakistani chai dhaaba…

⚠️ CONTINUITY/CONSISTENCY LOCKS:
  •    Warm daylight passport; NO color drift
  •    Steam: THIN, natural; NO heavy fog
  •    Objects: Only items visible in attached image; NO new extras spawn
  •    Single continuous shot; NO fades/morphs
CHARACTER POSITION LOCK (MANDATORY):
  •    LEFT: Zain (olive jacket, long hair) — stays LEFT entire clip
  •    RIGHT: Hamza (brown bomber, short beard) — stays RIGHT entire clip
  •    NO switching, NO extra people added
Posture lock:
  •    Both SEATED throughout (no standing/crouching)
No dialogue (establish the space). ← ONLY 1 vague line about action!
Camera: Subtle 6‑sec lateral drift left→right (~8–10 inches). Ends with: Same two‑shot;
cups on table centered. 6 sec, 16:9.
PROBLEM: 15 lines of locks, but “establish the space” tells Grok NOTHING! Grok will make
up random actions!

---

### ✅ CORRECT - Action-Focused, Natural Language:

Attached image: Two friends at chai dhaaba. Zain (LEFT, olive jacket) and Hamza (RIGHT,
brown bomber) seated at wooden table. Steel samovar behind, tin roof above, quiet lane
background.
ACTION (6 seconds): The scene opens with both men sitting in comfortable silence. Zain
has both hands wrapped around his warm chai cup, steam rising gently from it, while
Hamza looks down at his own cup on the table.
After about two seconds, Zain slowly lifts his cup toward his lips but pauses halfway, his
gaze drifting out toward the quiet lane - something on his mind. Hamza shifts slightly in his
seat and reaches for his own cup with his RIGHT hand.
In the final moments, Zain takes a small sip while his eyes stay on the distance, thoughtful
and preoccupied. Hamza brings his cup halfway up to chest level, waiting, watching his
friend. Both men sit in quiet contemplation - the silence says more than words.
CAMERA: Subtle lateral drift from left to right, moving about 8-10 inches over the full 6
seconds.
LOCKS:
  •    Positions fixed: Zain stays LEFT, Hamza stays RIGHT, no switching
  •    Both remain SEATED throughout, no standing
  •    Warm daylight color temperature, no shift
  •    No new objects appear
ENDS WITH: Two-shot of both men holding cups - Zain mid-sip with distant expression,
Hamza with cup at chest level. Setup for conversation.
6 sec, 16:9.
---

## ✍️ VIDEO PROMPT WRITING STYLE - NATURAL LANGUAGE (CRITICAL!)

╔══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
════════╗ ║ ║ ║ ⚠️ DO NOT USE ROBOTIC “BEAT” FORMAT! ║ ║ ║ ║ ❌ WRONG:
“Beat 1 (0-2s): Hand moves. Beat 2 (2-4s):…” ║ ║ ✅ CORRECT: Natural flowing paragraph
with specifics ║ ║ ║ ║ Write like describing a scene to a HUMAN cinematographer! ║ ║ ║
╚══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
════════╝


### ❌ WRONG: Robotic Beat Format

═══ ACTION (6s) ═══ BEAT 1 (0-2s): Hands pressed on stones. Stillness. BEAT 2 (2-4s):
RIGHT hand reaches down 8 inches. Grabs cloth. BEAT 3 (4-6s): Both hands pull back.
Torchlight flickers.
PROBLEMS: ├── Reads like code, not description ├── Fragmented, loses visual flow ├──
AI gets confused by ├── formatting ├── Timestamps feel robotic └── No sense of
continuous motion


### ✅ CORRECT: Natural Flowing Language with Specifics

ACTION (6 seconds): Both hands remain pressed flat on the stones, fingers spread wide,
holding completely still for the first second - a moment of finality. Then the RIGHT hand
releases and reaches DOWN about 8 inches to grab a dark brown woolen cloth from just
below frame. Gripping the cloth edge between thumb and two fingers, the hand pulls it UP
and drapes it over the stone area, covering roughly 80% of the hidden spot. The LEFT hand
stays pressed on the stones throughout. In the final two seconds, both hands slowly release
and pull BACK about 8 inches toward the body, withdrawing into the shadows until they’re
no longer visible. Near the end, the torchlight flickers once - dimming about 30% for half a
second before returning to normal.
WHAT’S BETTER: ├── Reads like a STORY, not code ├── Flows naturally, AI understands
sequence ├── Specifics are WOVEN IN (8 inches, 80%, 30%) ├── Feels like directions to a
cinematographer └── Continuous motion is clear

### THE RULE:

╔══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
════════╗ ║ ║ ║ Write prompts like you’re DESCRIBING a scene to a human ║ ║
cinematographer - NATURAL language but with SPECIFIC ║ ║ measurements, directions,
and timing woven in naturally. ║ ║ ║ ║ NOT: “0:02-0:02.5: Hand moves DOWN 8 inches” ║
║ YES: “Then the RIGHT hand reaches down about 8 inches” ║ ║ ║ ║ SPECIFICS REQUIRED
(woven into natural sentences): ║ ║ • Which hand (LEFT/RIGHT/BOTH) ║ ║ • Direction
(UP/DOWN/LEFT/RIGHT/BACK) ║ ║ • Distance (inches/feet) ║ ║ • How much
(percentages like “80% covered”) ║ ║ • What happens to OTHER body parts meanwhile ║
║║
╚══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
════════╝

---

## 🔢 SPECIFICITY RULES - USE NUMBERS EVERYWHERE!

╔══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
════════╗ ║ ║ ║ ⚠️ THE #1 REASON FOR BAD GEMINI OUTPUT: ║ ║ ║ ║ VAGUE
WORDS instead of SPECIFIC NUMBERS! ║ ║ ║ ║ “Torches flicker” = Gemini guesses
randomly ║ ║ “12 torches flicker 30° RIGHT” = Gemini does exactly that ║ ║ ║
╚══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
════════╝

### MANDATORY: Replace EVERY vague word with SPECIFIC NUMBER!

╔══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
═════════╗ ║ VAGUE WORD → SPECIFIC NUMBER ║
╠══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
═════════╣ ║ ║ ║ COUNTS: ║ ║ “torches” → “12 torches” ║ ║ “soldiers” → “12
soldiers in single-file” ║ ║ “some books” → “5 leather-bound books” ║ ║ “people” → “3 men
seated” ║ ║ ║ ║ DISTANCES: ║ ║ “in the distance” → “approximately 200 feet away” ║ ║
“push-in” → “push-in 12 inches” ║ ║ “moves forward” → “moves forward 6 inches” ║ ║ “far
away” → “50 feet from camera” ║ ║ ║ ║ PERCENTAGES: ║ ║ “grows larger” → “grows 20%
larger in frame” ║ ║ “more dominant” → “fills 60% of frame width” ║ ║ “slightly bigger” →
“10% larger than before” ║ ║ “most of frame” → “occupies 80% of frame” ║ ║ ║ ║
DIRECTIONS: ║ ║ “flicker” → “flames bend 30° RIGHT due to wind” ║ ║ “moving” →
“climbing LEFT to RIGHT” ║ ║ “approaches” → “moves toward camera from background” ║
║ “looks away” → “eyes shift DOWN-LEFT” ║ ║ ║ ║ TIMING: ║ ║ “slowly” → “over 2
seconds” ║ ║ “quickly” → “in 0.5 seconds” ║ ║ “pauses” → “holds still for 1 second” ║ ║
“gradually” → “increasing 10% per second” ║ ║ ║ ║ POSITIONS: ║ ║ “in corner” →
“bottom-right corner of frame” ║ ║ “to the side” → “LEFT side, 1/3 from edge” ║ ║ “near
table” → “2 feet from table edge” ║ ║ “background” → “15 feet behind main subject” ║ ║ ║
╚══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
═════════╝

---

### ❌ vs ✅ SPECIFICITY COMPARISON:
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
═════════ ❌ VAGUE (60% Quality - Gemini will GUESS):
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
═════════
Camera holds steady with distant torches flickering subtly as they move forward. Then
very slow push-in toward the cave mouth as torch dots grow slightly larger. The cave
entrance becomes more dominant in frame and tension builds.
PROBLEMS: ├── “Distant torches” - HOW MANY? 5? 12? 50? ├── “Flicker subtly” - HOW
MUCH? Which direction? ├── “Move forward” - HOW FAST? What distance? ├── “Grow
slightly larger” - HOW MUCH? 5%? 20%? ├── “More dominant” - WHAT PERCENTAGE of
frame? └── Gemini has to GUESS all these = RANDOM OUTPUT!
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
═════════ ✅ SPECIFIC + NATURAL LANGUAGE (95% Quality):
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
═════════
SCENE CONTEXT: Night, 1858 Kashmir mountains. The attached image shows the view
from inside a dark cave mouth looking OUT at a mountain path. Exactly 12 Dogra soldiers
with torches climb in the distance, approximately 200 feet away. Light snowfall, cold blue
moonlight contrasts with warm orange torch flames.
ACTION (6 seconds): The scene opens with the camera holding steady on the cave mouth
view. In the distance, 12 torch flames are visible in a single-file line, with the soldiers
climbing the path from LEFT to RIGHT. The flames bend about 30 degrees to the RIGHT due
to wind - the soldiers themselves are only silhouettes, too distant for faces to be visible.
Light snow streaks across the frame.
After about two seconds, the camera begins a very slow push-in toward the cave mouth,
moving forward approximately 6 inches. The 12 torches appear about 10% larger in the
frame now, though still distant. The cave mouth opening grows from about 40% to 50% of
the frame width.
In the final two seconds, the camera continues its push-in for another 6 inches (12 inches
total movement). The torches are now roughly 20% larger than they were at the start, and
the cave mouth fills about 60% of the frame width. The sense of approaching threat builds -
they’re coming.
LOCKS:
  •    Exactly 12 torches throughout, no more soldiers appear
  •    Soldier faces never visible (too distant, silhouettes only)
  •    No fog, haze, or mist added
  •    No people near camera or inside cave
  •    Single continuous shot
ENDS WITH: Cave mouth centered, filling 60% of frame width. 12 torch flames visible in
distance, 20% larger than at start. Same LEFT to RIGHT formation. Flames still bending 30°
RIGHT.
6 sec, 16:9, cinematic night.
WHAT’S BETTER: ├── EXACT count: “12 torches” not “torches” ├── EXACT distance: “200
feet away” not “distant” ├── EXACT direction: “30 degrees RIGHT” not “flicker” ├──
EXACT percentage: “10% larger” not “slightly larger” ├── EXACT frame fill: “60% of frame”
not “more dominant” ├── EXACT camera: “12 inches total” not “slow push-in” ├──
NATURAL LANGUAGE: Flows like story, not robot code └── Gemini has ZERO guessing =
EXACT OUTPUT!
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
═════════

---

### 🔢 SPECIFICITY CHECKLIST (Use for EVERY prompt!):

Before submitting ANY video prompt, check:

☐ COUNTS: Did I specify exact numbers? “12 soldiers” not “soldiers” “5 books” not “some
books” “2 cups” not “cups”

☐ DISTANCES: Did I specify exact distances? “200 feet away” not “in distance” “12 inches
push-in” not “slow push” “6 feet apart” not “spaced out”

☐ PERCENTAGES: Did I specify frame percentages? “fills 60% of frame” not “dominant”
“grows 20% larger” not “slightly bigger” “occupies bottom third” not “in lower area”

☐ DIRECTIONS: Did I specify exact directions? “LEFT to RIGHT” not “moving” “bend 30°
RIGHT” not “flicker” “eyes shift DOWN-LEFT” not “looks away”

☐ TIMING: Did I specify durations? “over 2 seconds” not “slowly” “holds for 1 second” not
“pauses” “in 0.5 seconds” not “quickly”

☐ POSITIONS: Did I specify exact positions? “LEFT side of frame” not “to the side” “bottom-
right corner” not “in corner” “2 feet from table” not “near table”
If ANY answer is NO → ADD THE SPECIFIC NUMBER!

## 🎬 GEMINI VIDEO PROMPT - COMPLETE GUIDE
╔══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
════════╗ ║ ║ ║ GEMINI REQUIRES MORE DETAIL THAN OTHER AI VIDEO TOOLS! ║
║ ║ ║ Short prompts = Random generations ║ ║ Vague actions = AI makes up movements
║ ║ Missing locks = Extra objects/people spawn ║ ║ ║ ║ SOLUTION: Detailed NATURAL
LANGUAGE prompts! ║ ║ (NOT robotic “Beat 1, Beat 2” format!) ║ ║ ║
╚══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
════════╝

---

### GEMINI VIDEO PROMPT PROBLEMS:

❌ COMMON GEMINI FAILURES:
  1.   SUDDEN APPEARANCES Problem: Objects/people appear from nowhere mid-clip
       Cause: Missing “DO NOT add any new elements” lock

  2.   CHARACTER MORPHING
       Problem: Face/clothes change during clip Cause: Missing detailed character
       description + lock

  3.   RANDOM MOVEMENTS Problem: Character does something not asked Cause: Vague
       action like “discussing” instead of specific movements

  4.   ENVIRONMENT CHANGES Problem: Background changes, lighting shifts Cause:
       Missing environment lock + color passport

  5.   TIMELINE CONFUSION Problem: Actions happen too fast or in wrong order Cause:
       Vague action description without clear sequence


---

### GEMINI VIDEO PROMPT TEMPLATE (MANDATORY):

╔══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
════════╗ ║ ║ ║ ⚠️ WRITE IN NATURAL FLOWING LANGUAGE! ║ ║ ║ ║ NOT robotic
“Beat 1, Beat 2, Beat 3” format! ║ ║ Write like describing a scene to a cinematographer. ║ ║
Specifics (inches, directions, percentages) WOVEN IN. ║ ║ ║
╚══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
════════╝


═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
═════════ 🎬 VIDEO PROMPT - Clip [X.X] (Gemini)
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
═════════
ATTACHED IMAGE: [filename] REFERENCES: [character/object reference files if needed]
SCENE CONTEXT: The attached image shows [brief description of what’s visible - location,
characters with positions (LEFT/RIGHT), objects, lighting direction]. This is [time of day],
[mood/atmosphere].
ACTION (6 seconds): [Write in natural flowing paragraphs. Describe the full sequence of
what happens from start to finish. WEAVE IN specific details like: which hand
(LEFT/RIGHT), directions (UP/DOWN/BACK), distances (8 inches, 12 inches), percentages
(80% covered, 30% dimmer), and what OTHER body parts do meanwhile. Make it read like
a story, not a robot instruction.]
Example of good natural language: “The scene begins with both hands pressed flat on the
stones, fingers spread wide, completely still for the first second. Then the RIGHT hand
releases and reaches DOWN about 8 inches to grab the dark brown cloth from below frame.
Gripping between thumb and two fingers, the hand pulls the cloth UP and drapes it over the
stones, covering roughly 80% of the spot. The LEFT hand stays pressed on the wall
throughout this motion. In the final moments, both hands slowly release and pull BACK
about 8 inches, withdrawing into shadow until no longer visible.”
[If there’s dialogue/narration]: DIALOGUE: [Speaker] says: “[exact line]” The speaker’s lips
move naturally as they speak, with [body language that matches the words - leaning in,
gesturing, etc.]. Meanwhile, the listener remains completely still, watching intently. Only
after the speaker finishes does the listener [describe reaction - nod, expression change,
etc.].
CAMERA: [Describe camera movement in natural language] Example: “The camera slowly
pushes forward about 12 inches over the full 6 seconds, starting from a medium shot and
ending slightly tighter on the hands.”
LOCKS:
  •    [Character] appearance unchanged from reference
  •    No additional people or objects appear
  •    Lighting color/direction stays consistent
  •    Single continuous shot, no cuts or transitions
ENDS WITH: [Describe exactly what’s visible in the final frame - character positions,
expressions, object states, camera framing. This becomes the seed for the next clip.]
TECHNICAL: 6 seconds, 16:9, [lighting style], cinematic.
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
═════════

### GEMINI PROMPT EXAMPLES:
**❌ WRONG (Robotic Beat Format):**

VIDEO PROMPT: Attach: Clip_1.5_lastframe.png
═══ ACTION (6s) ═══ BEAT 1 (0-2s): Hands pressed on stones. Stillness. BEAT 2 (2-4s):
RIGHT hand reaches down 8 inches. Grabs cloth. BEAT 3 (4-6s): Both hands pull back.
Torchlight flickers.
═══ LOCKS ═══
  •    Match colors
  •    No new items
  •    Single shot

**PROBLEMS:**
- Reads like code, not description
- Fragmented, no visual flow
- AI confused by "Beat 1, Beat 2" parsing
- Loses sense of continuous motion

---

**❌ WRONG (Vague - Gemini will fail):**

VIDEO PROMPT: Attach: Clip_1.2_image.png
Interior of a cave with Sufi scholars sitting in a circle around a central flame.
ACTION: The scholars are deep in discussion, heads bowed. One scholar is holding a large
book open. The flame flickers.
CAMERA: Slow push-in towards the circle.
6 seconds, 16:9.

**PROBLEMS:**
- "Deep in discussion" = WHAT exactly? No specifics!
- "Heads bowed" = All 6 seconds? No movement?
- "Slow push-in" = How slow? How far?
- NO locks = Random elements will appear!

---

**✅ CORRECT (Natural Language with Specifics):**

═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
═════════ 🎬 VIDEO PROMPT - Clip 1.2 (Gemini)
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
═════════
ATTACHED IMAGE: Clip_1.2_image.png
SCENE CONTEXT: The attached image shows the interior of a dark stone cave. Five Sufi
scholars in robes and turbans sit in a circle around a single oil lamp on the ground. The
CENTER scholar holds a large open book in his lap. Warm golden light illuminates their
robes from the center, with deep shadows on the cave walls behind them. The atmosphere
is ancient and secretive.
ACTION (6 seconds): The scene opens with all five scholars sitting completely motionless,
heads slightly bowed in contemplation, their hands resting on their knees. The small flame
flickers once during this opening moment, causing the shadows to shift gently on the walls
behind them.
After about two seconds, the CENTER scholar slowly raises his RIGHT hand and points
deliberately to a passage in the open book - the movement is careful and wise, not rushed.
The other four scholars remain still but their attention visibly shifts to watch his hand. The
flame steadies during this moment.
In the final two seconds, the scholar seated on the LEFT side of the circle leans his upper
body forward about 10 degrees, peering more closely at where the center scholar is
pointing. The other scholars show subtle head movements indicating their attention. The
flame gives one more gentle flicker as the camera settles into its final position.
CAMERA: The camera performs a very slow push-in toward the circle, moving forward
approximately 18 inches over the full 6 seconds. It starts as a wide shot with all five
scholars visible and dark edges around the frame, and ends tighter on the center three
scholars with the book clearly visible.
LOCKS:
  •    Exactly 5 scholars throughout, no additions
  •    Robe colors and turban styles unchanged
  •    Flame stays small, no bright fire effects
  •    No smoke, dust, or atmospheric particles added
  •    Faces remain shadowed/silhouetted, not clearly lit
  •    Single continuous shot, no cuts or transitions
ENDS WITH: The center scholar’s RIGHT hand rests on the book page, the LEFT scholar is
leaning forward with interest, and the others are attentive. The book is visible but any text
is too small to read. Warm golden light falls on their robes. The framing is now tighter and
more intimate than the start, creating a secretive atmosphere.
TECHNICAL: 6 seconds, 16:9, warm candlelight, cinematic.
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
═════════
---

### WHY NATURAL LANGUAGE WORKS BETTER:

╔══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
════════╗ ║ ║ ║ ROBOTIC FORMAT: ║ ║ “BEAT 2 (0:02-0:04): Hand raises. Points to
book.” ║ ║ → AI sees fragmented instructions, may miss flow ║ ║ ║ ║ NATURAL
LANGUAGE: ║ ║ “After about two seconds, the CENTER scholar slowly ║ ║ raises his
RIGHT hand and points deliberately to a ║ ║ passage in the open book - the movement is
careful ║ ║ and wise, not rushed.” ║ ║ → AI sees complete picture, understands intent +
feeling ║ ║ ║ ║ SAME INFORMATION, BETTER DELIVERY! ║ ║ ║
╚══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
════════╝

### ACTION DESCRIPTION RULES:

╔══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
═════════╗ ║ VAGUE vs SPECIFIC - Always choose SPECIFIC! ║
╠══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
═════════╣ ║ ║ ║ ❌ “Looking worried” ║ ║ ✅ “Eyebrows pinched together, lips
pressed thin, eyes ║ ║ darting left-right twice” ║ ║ ║ ║ ❌ “Picks up the book” ║ ║ ✅
“Right hand reaches down, fingers curl around book spine, ║ ║ lifts book 6 inches off table,
brings to chest level” ║ ║ ║ ║ ❌ “Walks to the door” ║ ║ ✅ “Takes 3 slow steps forward
(right foot, left, right), ║ ║ stops at door, right hand reaches for handle” ║ ║ ║ ║ ❌ “They
talk” ║ ║ ✅ “HAMZA’s lips move forming words (Beat 1-2), head tilts ║ ║ right slightly.
ZAIN watches, completely still, then ║ ║ nods once slowly in Beat 3” ║ ║ ║ ║ ❌ “Flame
flickers” ║ ║ ✅ “Flame bends left 15 degrees for 0.5 sec at 0:02, returns ║ ║ to center,
shadows on wall shift accordingly” ║ ║ ║
╚══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
═════════╝

---

### GEMINI LOCK CHECKLIST (Use for EVERY prompt):

Before submitting ANY video prompt to Gemini, verify:

☐ PEOPLE LOCK “DO NOT add any people not visible in attached image”

☐ OBJECT LOCK
“DO NOT add any objects not visible in attached image”

☐ APPEARANCE LOCK “DO NOT change character face, clothes, hair, or body type”
☐ ENVIRONMENT LOCK “DO NOT change background, walls, floor, or surroundings”

☐ LIGHTING LOCK “DO NOT change lighting color, direction, or intensity”

☐ EFFECTS LOCK “DO NOT add lens flares, particles, fog, or visual effects”

☐ CONTINUITY LOCK “SINGLE continuous 6-second shot, NO cuts, fades, or transitions”

☐ ACTION LOCK “ONLY perform the actions described, nothing additional”


---

### COMMON GEMINI FIXES:

PROBLEM: Extra people appear FIX: Add “The scene contains ONLY [X] people. DO NOT
add anyone else.”
PROBLEM: Character face changes FIX: Add “Character face MUST match attached image
exactly throughout.”
PROBLEM: Random objects spawn FIX: Add “ONLY objects visible in attached image. NO
new items appear.”
PROBLEM: Lighting shifts dramatically
FIX: Add “Lighting remains CONSTANT: [describe exact lighting]”
PROBLEM: Action happens too fast FIX: Break into 3 beats with exact timing for each
movement
PROBLEM: Wrong action performed FIX: Add “Character does ONLY: [specific action].
Nothing else.”
PROBLEM: Scene cuts or transitions FIX: Add “ONE continuous shot. NO cuts, fades,
dissolves, or transitions.”

---


### CHARACTER ACTING RULES (CRITICAL!):

╔══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
════════╗ ║ ║ ║ TWO MAJOR ACTING PROBLEMS TO AVOID: ║ ║ ║ ║ 1. OVER-
ACTING: Too many micro-instructions = exaggerated ║ ║ 2. EARLY REACTION: Listener
reacts before speaker done ║ ║ ║
╚══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
════════╝

---
**PROBLEM 1: Over-Acting (Too Much Detail)**

❌ OVER-DETAILED (Grok exaggerates everything!):
ZAIN - LISTENING: ├── Eyes: Fixed on Hamza’s face, attentive ├── Eyebrows: Slightly
raised (engaged, curious) ├── Mouth: CLOSED, lips together, relaxed ├── Head: Still,
slight forward lean └── Hands: Both wrapped around chai cup, motionless
Result: Robotic, exaggerated, unnatural!

✅ SIMPLE & NATURAL:
“Zain remains completely still, just watching Hamza speak.”
ONE natural sentence = natural acting!

---

**PROBLEM 2: Early Reaction (Listener Reacts Too Soon)**

❌ WRONG - Simultaneous/Early Reaction:
0:00-0:03: Hamza speaks, Zain nods and looks at pocket 0:03-0:06: Zain touches slip
Problem: Zain reacting WHILE Hamza still talking!

✅ CORRECT - Delayed Reaction with PAUSE:
0:00-0:03: Hamza speaks. Zain completely still, just watching. (NO reaction yet!) 0:03-0:04:
Hamza finishes. Brief PAUSE - both still. 0:04-0:06: NOW Zain reacts - nod, glance, touches
slip.
Key: Add PAUSE between speaking and reacting!

---

**DIALOGUE SCENE FORMULA:**

SPEAKER + LISTENER Scene Structure:
PHASE 1 - SPEAKING (0:00-0:03): ├── Speaker: Dialogue + matching body language │
(leaning in = curious, gesturing = explaining) ├── Listener: COMPLETELY STILL, just
watching └── Explicit: “No reaction yet from [Listener]”
PHASE 2 - PAUSE (0:03-0:04): ├── Speaker: Finishes, waits ├── Listener: Still processing
└── This pause feels NATURAL!
PHASE 3 - REACTION (0:04-0:06): ├── Speaker: Watches listener’s reaction ├── Listener:
NOW reacts (nod, expression, action) └── Explicit: “[Listener] does NOT react until 0:04”
---

**SPEAKER BODY-DIALOGUE SYNC:**

❌ WRONG (Body doesn’t match words): “Hamza speaks ‘emergency?’ - lips moving, casual
tone” (Body doing nothing while asking urgent question!)

✅ CORRECT (Body matches dialogue): “Hamza LEANS IN slightly and speaks ‘emergency?’
with curious expression”
SYNC EXAMPLES: ├── Asking question → Lean forward, raised eyebrows ├── Explaining
→ Hand gestures, eye contact ├── Warning → Serious face, firm posture ├── Joking →
Slight smile, relaxed body └── Whispering secret → Lean close, look around first

---

**ACTING INSTRUCTION STYLE:**

❌ TOO TECHNICAL (Over-acting): “Eyes: shift down-left 45 degrees over 1.2 seconds
Eyebrows: lower 3mm, thinking expression Mouth: lips press together for 0.5 seconds”

✅ NATURAL LANGUAGE (Good acting): “Zain gives a small nod, glances down at his
pocket, and touches the folded slip. Hamza watches his hand.”
Write like describing to a human actor, not a robot!

---

**COMPLETE DIALOGUE SCENE EXAMPLE:**

Attached image: Two friends at chai dhaaba table. Zain (LEFT, olive jacket) and Hamza
(RIGHT, brown bomber) seated.
═══ ACTION ═══
0:00-0:03: Hamza leans in slightly and speaks “Bhai, subah-subah emergency?” with
curious expression. Zain remains completely still, just watching Hamza speak. No reaction
yet from Zain.
0:03-0:04: Hamza finishes speaking, waits. Brief pause - both still.
0:04-0:06: NOW Zain reacts - small nod, then glances down at his jacket pocket and touches
the folded slip. Hamza’s eyes follow Zain’s hand.
═══ LOCKS ═══ Zain=LEFT, Hamza=RIGHT Both seated Only Hamza speaks (0:00-0:03)
Zain does NOT react until 0:04
═══ CAMERA ═══ Static two-shot.
═══ ENDS WITH ═══ Zain’s hand on pocket, Hamza watching the slip.
6 sec, 16:9.

---

### PROMPT LENGTH BALANCE:

IDEAL STRUCTURE:
Context/Image description: 2-3 lines (BRIEF!) ACTION BEATS: 6-8 lines (MAIN FOCUS!)
Locks: 3-4 lines (BRIEF!) Camera: 1-2 lines Ends with: 1-2 lines
TOTAL: 15-18 lines max
NOT: 30 lines of locks + 1 line of vague action!

---

### DETAILED FORMAT:

VIDEO PROMPT (Grok/Gemini): Attach: [image/lastframe]
SCENE CONTEXT: [2-3 lines describing what’s in the attached image - location, who’s
visible, lighting, mood]
ACTION (6 seconds): [Write in natural flowing paragraphs. Describe the full sequence from
start to finish. Include specific details woven naturally: which hand (LEFT/RIGHT),
directions (UP/DOWN/BACK), distances (8 inches, 12 inches), percentages (80% covered),
and what other body parts do meanwhile. Make it read like a story.]
[If DIALOGUE present: Describe speaker’s movement while talking, then note that listener
stays still, then describe listener’s reaction only after speaker finishes.]
CAMERA: [Movement type, direction, distance in natural sentence form. Example: “The
camera slowly pushes forward about 12 inches.”]
LOCKS: [3-4 critical locks - positions, appearance, lighting]
ENDS WITH: [Precise final frame description - positions, expressions, camera framing. This
becomes seed for next clip.]
6 sec, 16:9, cinematic.

---

**❌ WRONG - Too Vague (AI will generate garbage):**
A slow pan to reveal the study room. Walls covered with books. Dusty atmosphere. 6
seconds, 16:9, cinematic.


**✅ CORRECT - Fully Detailed (AI understands exactly what to create):**

Attach: Clip_1.3_lastframe.png (1st attached image)
A slow, low-angle cinematic pan outwards revealing a grand ancient Pakistani haveli study
room from the 1940s era. The room has 20-foot high ornate ceilings with intricate Islamic
geometric patterns carved into dark wood.
Walls are covered floor-to-ceiling with thousands of old leather-bound books - Urdu,
Arabic, Persian texts - stacked chaotically on dark mahogany shelves that reach to the
ceiling. Some books are falling over, others have yellowed papers sticking out.
Golden afternoon sunlight streams through three tall arched windows with faded dark
green velvet curtains, partially drawn. Dust particles float magically in the light beams,
undisturbed for years.
An antique brass chandelier with melted candles hangs from the ceiling center. Below it, a
massive carved wooden desk covered with scattered manuscripts, old maps, a brass
inkwell, and a magnifying glass.
A faded Persian rug in deep red and gold covers the wooden floor. Old leather armchair
near the window. A grandfather clock in the corner, stopped at 3:47.
NARRATION (Deep, wise male voice, slow nostalgic pace, Roman Urdu): “Faisalabad ke is
purane mohalle mein ek haveli thi - jise log ‘Kitaabon wala ghar’ kehte the.”
The narration introduces this magnificent space as camera reveals its grandeur. The voice
carries weight and history, matching the ancient atmosphere of the room.
Camera Movement: Very slow pan starting from low angle near floor, gradually moving
upward and widening to reveal the grand scale of this magnificent room. Movement timed
with narration pace.
The shot ends with: Wide view of entire study room visible - bookshelves reaching ceiling
on three walls, windows on right with golden light streaming in, desk in center-left,
chandelier above, Persian rug below, dust particles floating in light beams, no person
visible yet - the room awaits its visitor.
6 seconds, 16:9 horizontal, cinematic warm golden lighting.


📁 Save as: `Clip_[X.X]_video.mp4`
📁 Save last frame as: `Clip_[X.X]_lastframe.png`

---
**VIDEO PROMPT MUST INCLUDE:**

| Element | Details Required |
|---------|------------------|
| **Environment** | Style, era, architecture, size, materials, colors |
| **Objects** | Specific items, their condition, placement, materials |
| **Lighting** | Source, direction, color, quality (golden, harsh, soft) |
| **Atmosphere** | Dust, fog, particles, air quality |
| **Textures** | Wood type, fabric condition, metal finish |
| **Colors** | Specific color palette (faded red, dark mahogany, etc.) |
| **Scale** | Room size, ceiling height, object sizes |
| **Time Period** | Era, decade, historical context |
| **Narration** | INSIDE prompt - full text, voice style, timing with visuals
|
| **Dialogue** | INSIDE prompt - full text, character expression, lip sync |
| **Camera** | Movement type, speed, start position, end position |
| **Mood** | Emotional feeling (mysterious, peaceful, tense) |
| **Last Frame** | DETAILED description of final frame for next clip |

---

**DETAIL LEVEL EXAMPLES:**

| Vague ❌ | Detailed ✅ |
|----------|------------|
| "old books" | "leather-bound books from the 1800s, spines cracked with age,
faded gold patterns barely visible, pages yellowed and brittle" |
| "dusty room" | "thick layer of grey dust on every surface, tiny specks
drifting lazily through diagonal sunlight beams, cobwebs in upper corners" |
| "wooden desk" | "massive carved mahogany desk from British colonial era,
intricate floral patterns on legs, worn leather top surface, brass handles on
drawers" |
| "window light" | "golden afternoon sunlight streaming through tall arched
window, creating diagonal light shafts, dust particles catching the light" |
| "character enters" | "Zain steps slowly through the doorway, his olive
green jacket catching the light, his expression shifting from curiosity to
awe as he sees the room, his hand reaches out to touch the doorframe" |

**NOTE:** Avoid describing readable text on books/papers - Grok generates
gibberish. Instead describe textures, colors, conditions.

---

**RULE: If you can imagine multiple different versions, the prompt is NOT
detailed enough!**

A good prompt should create the SAME mental image for anyone reading it.

---
### ⚠️ GROK-SPECIFIC: How to Describe Visual Effects

**Grok has trouble with certain descriptions. Use these SPECIFIC phrasings:**

---

**TEXT, WRITING & SCRIPTS:**

**THE PROBLEM:**
When you write "Urdu text on books" or "Arabic script" → AI generates
GIBBERISH that looks like alien symbols! This ruins immersion and looks
unprofessional.

❌ WRONG (Creates unreadable gibberish):

“Gold-embossed Urdu titles on book spines” “Arabic script visible on the manuscript”
“Persian text on the old map” “Handwritten Urdu notes on papers”


✅ CORRECT APPROACHES:

**APPROACH A - Keep Text Unreadable/Blurred (RECOMMENDED):**

“Book spines with faded, illegible gold lettering - too worn and dusty to read clearly”
“Ancient manuscript pages with dense, small script - the text is too faded and the camera
too distant to make out individual words”
“Old map with geographical markings and annotations, the handwriting aged and blurred
beyond recognition”

**APPROACH B - Avoid Showing Text Directly:**

“Rows of leather-bound books, their spines facing inward, hiding any titles”
“Papers scattered face-down on the desk”
“The book is open but the camera angle shows only the texture of the page, not the content”

**APPROACH C - If Text MUST Be Readable (Close-up only):**

When text absolutely must be visible and readable, be EXTREMELY specific about what it
says - this gives AI the best chance:
“Extreme close-up on a book spine with embossed text reading ‘KITAB’ in stylized
lettering” (Keep it to 1-2 simple words)
“A single word ‘RAAZ’ is visible, hand-painted in gold on the leather cover”
**APPROACH D - Use Symbols Instead of Text:**

“The book cover features an ornate geometric Islamic pattern rather than text”
“A brass seal with a crescent moon symbol marks the document”
“The map shows illustrated mountains and rivers rather than written labels”

---

**TEXT VISIBILITY BY DISTANCE:**

| Distance | What's Visible | What's NOT |
|----------|----------------|------------|
| **Wide shot** | Books as colored shapes | ANY text |
| **Medium shot** | Book spines as rectangles | Readable text |
| **Medium close** | "Something is written" (blur) | Clear letters |
| **Close-up** | Individual letters (if specified) | N/A |
| **Extreme close-up** | Full words (if specified exactly) | N/A |

**RULE: If camera is not in EXTREME CLOSE-UP, describe text as "faded,"
"illegible," "too small to read," or "blurred by age."**

---

**SIGNS, SYMBOLS, AND DECORATIONS:**

✅ SAFE TO USE (AI handles well):

“Islamic geometric patterns carved into wood” “Floral motifs embossed on leather”
“Crescent moon and star symbol” “Simple border decorations” “Abstract patterns”


❌ AVOID (AI creates gibberish):

“Urdu calligraphy” “Arabic verses” “Quranic inscriptions” “Persian poetry text” “Specific
named text/quotes”

---

**DUST PARTICLES:**

❌ WRONG (Grok creates weird rounded blobs):

“Dust particles suspended magically in the light beam, perfectly still” “Dust motes floating
in the air” “Suspended dust particles”
✅ CORRECT (Natural, cinematic dust):

“Tiny specks of dust drift slowly through the diagonal shaft of golden sunlight, catching the
light as they move - some bright, some dim, creating a natural atmospheric haze. The dust
moves subtly, like particles in a sunbeam through a window.”


✅ ALTERNATIVE PHRASINGS:

“Fine atmospheric haze with tiny bright specks visible where sunlight cuts through the dim
room”
“Natural room dust visible in the light beam - countless tiny particles drifting lazily, some
catching light brilliantly”
“The golden light reveals the air itself - filled with fine particles that drift and swirl gently,
creating depth and atmosphere”

---

**FOG / MIST:**

❌ WRONG (Grok creates unnatural fog):

“Fog fills the room” “Misty atmosphere” “Foggy air”


✅ CORRECT:

“A soft, low-lying haze hugs the ground, wispy and translucent, allowing visibility but
adding mysterious depth to the scene”
“Thin atmospheric haze diffuses the light, creating soft glows around light sources and
reducing contrast in distant areas”
“Morning mist - delicate, wispy layers of moisture in the air that catch and scatter the light
softly”

---

**LIGHT BEAMS / GOD RAYS:**

❌ WRONG:

“Light beams shine through window” “God rays in the room”
✅ CORRECT:

“Distinct diagonal shafts of warm golden sunlight cut through the dim interior, made visible
by the fine dust in the air. The light creates sharp bright streaks against the darker
surroundings, with soft edges where dust diffuses the beams.”
“Volumetric light streams through the tall windows at a low angle, creating dramatic
diagonal columns of golden illumination that contrast with the shadowy areas of the room”

---

**SMOKE:**

❌ WRONG:

“Smoke in the air” “Smoky atmosphere”


✅ CORRECT:

“Thin wisps of pale smoke curl upward slowly, twisting and dispersing as they rise,
partially translucent against the darker background”
“Delicate trails of smoke drift lazily, catching the light and creating soft, ever-changing
patterns in the air”

---

**FIRE / FLAMES:**

❌ WRONG:

“Fire burning” “Flames flickering”


✅ CORRECT:

“Orange and yellow flames dance and lick upward from the fire, constantly shifting shape,
casting warm flickering light on nearby surfaces. The fire crackles, with occasional bright
sparks rising into the air.”

---

**REFLECTIONS:**

❌ WRONG:
“Reflection visible in mirror/water”


✅ CORRECT:

“The polished surface catches and mirrors the scene - showing a slightly darker, reversed
image of [specific thing reflected]. The reflection has subtle distortion at the edges.”

---

**SHADOWS:**

❌ WRONG:

“Shadows in the room”


✅ CORRECT:

“Deep shadows pool in the corners and beneath furniture, creating pockets of darkness that
contrast with the lit areas. The shadow edges are soft where indirect light reaches, sharp
where direct light is blocked.”

---

### GROK DESCRIPTION FORMULA:

For any atmospheric/visual effect, use this structure:

[WHAT it is] + [HOW it behaves/moves] + [HOW light interacts with it] + [WHERE in
frame]

**Example - Dust:**

WHAT: “Tiny specks of natural room dust” HOW IT BEHAVES: “drift slowly and lazily
through the air” LIGHT INTERACTION: “catching the golden sunlight, some bright, some
dim” WHERE: “visible in the diagonal light beam from the window”
FULL: “Tiny specks of natural room dust drift slowly and lazily through the air, catching the
golden sunlight - some appearing bright, others dim - visible throughout the diagonal light
beam streaming from the tall window.”

---

════════╗ ║ ║ ║ BG MUSIC: ONE track covers 3-6 clips (18-36 seconds) ║ ║
NARRATION: ONE recording covers 3-5 clips (18-30 sec) ║ ║ SOUND EFFECTS: Per clip
(only where needed) ║ ║ ║ ║ DO NOT generate audio separately for EVERY 6-sec clip! ║ ║

════════╝

---

### ❌ WRONG (Per-clip audio - DO NOT DO THIS!):

Clip 1.1: ├── BG Music: “Mysterious ambient…” ├── Narration: “Faisalabad ki galiyon
mein…” └── SFX: “Birds chirping”
Clip 1.2: ├── BG Music: “Continuing mysterious…” ├── Narration: “Kuch jagahen yaad hoti
hain…” └── SFX: “Footsteps”
Clip 1.3: ├── BG Music: “Same mysterious tone…” ├── Narration: “Is kahani ka raasta…”
└── SFX: “Door creak”
THIS IS WRONG! Creates choppy, unprofessional audio!

### ✅ CORRECT (BATCH audio approach):

═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════

═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
═════════

🎶 BG MUSIC BLOCK: ├── CLIPS COVERED: 1.1 - 1.6 (ALL scene clips) ├── DURATION:
36 seconds total ├── TRACK: “Mystery Unfolding” by Trending Music ├── SOURCE:
https://pixabay.com/music/ └── NOTES: Fade in 1.1, continuous, fade out 1.6

🎙️ NARRATION BLOCK: ├── CLIPS COVERED: 1.1 - 1.4 (First 4 clips only) ├──
DURATION: 24 seconds ├── ONE CONTINUOUS RECORDING: │ “Faisalabad ke Aminpur
Bazaar mein ek haveli hai, │ number 47. 35 saal pehle Professor Danish Mirza │ yahan se
ghayab hue. Unke pote Zain ne dada ki │ diary mein woh cipher dekh liya jo Kashmir ka │
raasta dikhata hai.” ├── STYLE: Deep male, 50s, mysterious └── Clips 1.5-1.6: SILENT (no
narration)
🔊 SOUND EFFECTS (Per clip where needed): ├── Clip 1.1: “Birds chirping morning” -
Mixkit (0:00-0:06) ├── Clip 1.2: “Footsteps on stone” - Mixkit (0:02-0:05) ├── Clip 1.3:
None ├── Clip 1.4: “Old door creak” - Mixkit (0:03-0:05) ├── Clip 1.5: “Room ambience” -
Pixabay (throughout) └── Clip 1.6: “Book pages turning” - Mixkit (0:02-0:04)

### AUDIO BATCH RULES:

╔══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
═════════╗ ║ AUDIO TYPE │ COVERAGE │ FORMAT ║
╠══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
═════════╣ ║ BG Music │ 3-6 clips minimum │ ONE track per block ║ ║ │ (entire
scene or │ Exact track name + ║ ║ │ scene section) │ source URL ║
╠══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
═════════╣ ║ Narration │ 3-5 clips │ ONE continuous recording ║ ║ │ (not every
clip!) │ Full text as single piece ║ ║ │ │ 60-70% clips = SILENT ║
╠══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
═════════╣ ║ Sound Effects │ Per clip │ Only where needed ║ ║ │ (individual) │
Exact name + source URL ║
╠══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
═════════╣ ║ Dialogue │ Per clip │ Only where character ║ ║ │ (individual) │
actually speaks ║
╚══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
═════════╝

---

### ⚠️ MANDATORY: DRAMATIC NARRATION CHECK

╔══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
════════╗ ║ ║ ║ BEFORE finalizing ANY narration text, verify it’s ║ ║ DRAMATIC, not
DRY! ║ ║ ║ ║ DRY narration = Viewer gets bored = Clicks away! ║ ║ DRAMATIC narration
= Viewer FEELS the story = Stays! ║ ║ ║
╚══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
════════╝

### NARRATION STYLE CHECKLIST (USE FOR EVERY NARRATION):

☐ SENSORY DETAILS? ❌ “Raat thi” ✅ “Raat ke 2 baje. Barf gir rahi thi. Haath kaanp rahe
the.”
☐ EMOTION CONVEYED? ❌ “Woh dar gaya” ✅ “Uska dil itni tez dhadak raha tha ke
saans lena mushkil tha”

☐ STAKES CLEAR? ❌ “Mushkil tha” ✅ “Agar 3 minute mein nahi bachaya, poora Kashmir
gulaam ban jayega”

☐ TIME PRESSURE? ❌ “Jaldi karna tha” ✅ “Sirf 3 minute the. 3 minute. Aur 200 sipahi
100 kadam door.”

☐ SHORT PUNCHY SENTENCES FOR TENSION? ❌ Long flowing paragraph ✅ “Woh ruka.
Saans roki. Andhera. Khamoshi. Phir… qadam ki awaaz.”

☐ SPECIFIC NUMBERS? ❌ “Bohat log”, “kuch waqt”, “door tak” ✅ “23 log”, “3 minute”,
“200 kadam”
If ANY checkbox is empty → REWRITE the narration!

### VOICE DIRECTION (Include with narration):

For ElevenLabs/voice generation, ALWAYS specify:
TENSE MOMENTS: “Voice: Lower pitch, slower pace. Pause after key words. Example
delivery: ‘Sirf… [pause] 3 minute the.’”
EMOTIONAL MOMENTS: “Voice: Warmer, intimate. Let emotion break through. Example:
‘Dada ki aakhri yaad… [voice slightly cracks] woh mushkurahat.’”
REVELATION MOMENTS: “Voice: Building energy, then pause before reveal. Example: ‘Aur
jab usne khola… [long pause] sab badal gaya.’”
ACTION MOMENTS: “Voice: Faster, urgent, breathless. Example: ‘Bhaago! Ab! ABHI!’”

---

### SCENE-LEVEL AUDIO PLANNING:

**BEFORE generating clip prompts, plan audio for ENTIRE scene:**

SCENE X AUDIO PLAN: ├── Total clips: [X] ├── Total duration: [X] seconds │ ├── 🎶 BG
MUSIC: │ ├── Clips covered: [X.1 - X.X] │ ├── Track name: “[Exact name]” │ └── Source:
[URL] │ ├── 🎙️ NARRATION: │ ├── Clips covered: [X.1 - X.X] (or NONE) │ ├── Silent
clips: [X.X - X.X] │ └── Full text: “[Complete narration]” │ ├── 🔊 SFX NEEDED: │ ├──
Clip X.1: [Effect] or None │ ├── Clip X.2: [Effect] or None │ └── … │ └── 🗣️ DIALOGUE:
├── Clip X.X: [Character]: “[Line]” └── Other clips: None

---
### WHY BATCH APPROACH:

PROFESSIONAL AUDIO WORKFLOW: ├── BG Music: Downloaded once, laid across
timeline ├── Narration: Recorded once, placed across clips ├── SFX: Added individually
where needed └── Result: Seamless, professional audio
PER-CLIP APPROACH (WRONG): ├── BG Music: Different description each clip =
inconsistent ├── Narration: Choppy sentences = unnatural ├── SFX: Same thing repeated
= redundant └── Result: Amateur, choppy audio

---

### ⚠️ AUDIO vs VIDEO - CLEAR DISTINCTION:

VIDEO PROMPT handles: ├── Character VISUALLY speaking (lip sync) ├── Character
expressions WHILE speaking ├── Character body language during dialogue ├── Visual
reactions to sounds ├── Timing of actions with narration └── ALL visual representation
of audio moments
AUDIO SECTION handles: ├── Actual voice generation prompts (for ElevenLabs, etc.) ├──
Background music generation prompts (for Suno AI, etc.) ├── Sound effect descriptions
(for Freesound, etc.) └── Complete audio production guide

**BOTH sections must be complete for EVERY clip!**

---

### ⚠️ CRITICAL: Audio Pacing Rules (VERY IMPORTANT!)

**PROBLEM - Every clip having narration/dialogue:**

❌ WRONG: Clip 1.1: Narration “Faisalabad ke is purane mohalle mein…” Clip 1.2:
Narration “…ek haveli thi jise log…” Clip 1.3: Narration “…Danish sahab rehte the…” Clip
1.4: Narration “…unka junoon tha kitabein…” Clip 1.5: Dialogue “Beta, pehle taleem
mukammal karo” Clip 1.6: Narration “Zain ne sun li…”
THIS IS EXHAUSTING! No breathing room. Feels artificial and non-engaging.


**✅ CORRECT - Natural pacing with visual breathing room:**

Clip 1.1: PURE VISUAL ────────────── (opening hook, atmosphere, NO narration)
Clip 1.2: PURE VISUAL ────────────── (building mood, NO narration) Clip 1.3:
NARRATION STARTS ───────── “Faisalabad ke is purane mohalle mein ek haveli thi…”
(begins) Clip 1.4: NARRATION CONTINUES ────── “…jise log ‘Kitaabon wala ghar’ kehte
the. Danish sahab…” (flows) Clip 1.5: NARRATION ENDS ─────────── “…yeh unka
junoon tha.” (completes) Clip 1.6: PURE VISUAL ────────────── (let the words sink
in, NO narration) Clip 1.7: PURE VISUAL ────────────── (emotional beat, visual
storytelling) Clip 1.8: NEW NARRATION STARTS ───── (fresh audio element begins)

---

### AUDIO PACING GUIDELINES:

| Clip Type | When to Use | Purpose |
|-----------|-------------|---------|
| **Pure Visual** | Opening shots, transitions, emotional beats, reactions |
Let visuals breathe, build atmosphere, process information |
| **Narration START** | Beginning a thought/story section | Introduce
information |
| **Narration CONTINUE** | Long narration spanning clips | Natural speech
flow |
| **Narration END** | Completing a thought | Allow processing time after |
| **Dialogue** | Character interaction | Story progression |
| **Reaction Shot** | After important dialogue | Show impact without words |
| **Silent Tension** | Before climax, dramatic moments | Build suspense |

---

### NARRATION DURATION RULES:

**How much narration fits in 6 seconds?**
- Slow, dramatic pace: 15-20 words
- Normal pace: 20-25 words
- Fast pace: 25-30 words (NOT recommended for cinematic feel)

**If narration is longer than 20-25 words:**
- SPLIT across multiple clips
- Mark clearly: "NARRATION STARTS", "NARRATION CONTINUES", "NARRATION ENDS"
- Visuals in each clip should match that portion of narration

**Example - Long Narration Split:**

Full narration (45 words - needs 2-3 clips): “Faisalabad ke is purane mohalle mein ek
haveli thi, jise log ‘Kitaabon wala ghar’ kehte the. Is haveli mein rehte the Danish sahab - ek
aisa insaan jisne apni poori zindagi ilm ki talaash mein guzaari.”
Clip 1.3 - NARRATION STARTS (16 words): “Faisalabad ke is purane mohalle mein ek haveli
thi, jise log ‘Kitaabon wala ghar’ kehte the.” Visual: Exterior of haveli, slow reveal
Clip 1.4 - NARRATION ENDS (18 words): “Is haveli mein rehte the Danish sahab - ek aisa
insaan jisne apni poori zindagi ilm ki talaash mein guzaari.” Visual: Interior, books, Danish
sahab’s study
---

### IDEAL AUDIO DISTRIBUTION (10-clip scene example):

Scene 1 (10 clips) - Opening: ├── Clip 1.1: PURE VISUAL ─────────── (atmosphere,
hook) ├── Clip 1.2: PURE VISUAL ─────────── (building mood) ├── Clip 1.3:
NARRATION STARTS ────── (intro begins) ├── Clip 1.4: NARRATION CONTINUES ───
(story flows) ├── Clip 1.5: NARRATION ENDS ──────── (thought completes) ├── Clip
1.6: PURE VISUAL ─────────── (breathing room) ├── Clip 1.7: NARRATION STARTS
────── (new information) ├── Clip 1.8: NARRATION ENDS ──────── (completes)
├── Clip 1.9: PURE VISUAL ─────────── (emotional beat) └── Clip 1.10: PURE
VISUAL ─────────── (scene transition prep)
Result: 4 clips with narration, 6 clips pure visual This feels NATURAL and CINEMATIC!

---

### DIALOGUE PACING RULES:

**Don't cram dialogue into every clip!**

❌ WRONG (machine-gun dialogue): Clip 5.1: Zain: “Yeh kya hai?” Clip 5.2: Hassan: “Pata
nahi, dikha” Clip 5.3: Zain: “Yeh to Dada ki cheez hai” Clip 5.4: Hassan: “Kya likha hai
ismein?”

✅ CORRECT (natural pacing with reactions): Clip 5.1: Zain: “Yeh kya hai?” (surprised
discovery) Clip 5.2: PURE VISUAL - Hassan moving closer, examining (REACTION, no
dialogue) Clip 5.3: Hassan: “Pata nahi… dikha zara” (natural pause before response) Clip
5.4: PURE VISUAL - Both examining together (visual storytelling) Clip 5.5: Zain: “Yeh to…
Dada ki cheez lagti hai” (realization dawning)

---

### KEY AUDIO PACING PRINCIPLES:

1. **Not every clip needs words** - Silent moments are powerful
2. **Reactions don't need dialogue** - Show, don't tell
3. **Long narrations MUST be split** - Natural speech across clips
4. **After important information → breathing room** - Let it sink in
5. **Before dramatic moment → silence** - Build tension
6. **Dialogue needs realistic pauses** - People don't respond instantly
7. **Pure visual clips = 40-60% of scene** - Essential for cinematic feel
8. **Mark narration status** - START/CONTINUE/END for clarity

---
### FOR AUDIO SECTION IN PURE VISUAL CLIPS:

Even when there's NO narration/dialogue, Audio Section must include:

AUDIO (For External Audio Generation):
Type            Content
BG Music        [Continuing/New] - [Mood description]
Sound Effects [Environmental sounds: wind, birds, footsteps, etc.]
Narration       None - Pure visual clip
Dialogue        None - Pure visual clip
Ambient         [Atmospheric sounds that enhance the mood]
