---

### EXAMPLE - Complete Video Prompt (Correct Format):

VIDEO PROMPT (Grok): Attach: Clip_1.1_lastframe.png (1st attached image)
The attached image shows an aerial view of the old Faisalabad mohalla with the haveli in
center. The haveli has a weathered peach/orange facade, dark brown wooden door
(closed), and is surrounded by trees and neighboring buildings.
From this aerial position, the camera smoothly descends toward the haveli, getting closer
to the entrance level while maintaining focus on the building.

⚠️ CONTINUITY REQUIREMENTS:
VISUAL MATCH: ├── Color temperature: Warm golden hour - NO SHIFT ├── Brightness:
Medium, natural daylight - MAINTAIN └── Contrast: Medium - NO CHANGE
SUBJECT LOCK: ├── Haveli facade: Peach/orange weathered brick - NO CHANGE ├──
Door: DARK BROWN wooden (almost black), CLOSED - LOCKED │ NO color change, NO
opening, NO red tint ├── Windows: 6 visible with white frames - same positions └──
Building: Colonial-era style - NO MORPHING
LIGHTING LOCK: ├── Source: Natural golden hour sun from upper right ├── NO magical
glow around door or windows ├── NO supernatural lighting effects ├── NO spotlight
effects appearing └── Lighting CONSISTENT from first to last frame
OBJECT LOCK: ├── Present: Trees on both sides, street, neighboring buildings ├── NO
scooters/motorcycles appearing ├── NO people appearing ├── NO new plants/pots
materializing └── Scene contents EXACTLY as in reference
TRANSITION LOCK: ├── SINGLE continuous shot - NO cuts ├── NO fades or dissolves
├── NO morphing to different building └── Same haveli start to end
Camera Movement: Smooth drone descent from high aerial (as shown in reference)
descending approximately 50 feet while pushing slightly forward. Movement is slow and
cinematic.
The shot ends with: Medium-wide shot of the haveli facade. Door (still dark brown, still
closed) now visible in detail. Upper floor and balconies visible. Trees framing both sides.
Same lighting, same colors as reference.
6 seconds, 16:9 horizontal, cinematic warm golden lighting.

---

### QUICK COPY-PASTE LOCK STATEMENTS:

**For outdoor/building shots:**

⚠️ LOCKS:
  •    Building: [color] facade - NO morphing
  •    Door: [color], [OPEN/CLOSED] - NO change, NO glow
  •    Lighting: Natural [time] sun - NO magical effects
  •    Objects: Only [list] - NO spawning
  •    Shot: SINGLE continuous - NO fades/morphs

**For indoor shots:**

⚠️ LOCKS:
  •    Room: [description] - NO changes to walls/furniture
  •    Lighting: [source] - NO magical glow appearing
  •    Objects: [list what’s visible] - NO spawning
  •    Window light: [description] - CONSISTENT
  •    Shot: SINGLE continuous - NO fades/morphs

**For character shots:**

⚠️ LOCKS:
  •    Character position: [LEFT/RIGHT] - NO switching
  •    Clothing: [description] - NO changes
  •    Background: [description] - NO morphing
  •    Lighting: [source] - CONSISTENT
  •    Other people: [NONE/list] - NO extras appearing

---

### EXAMPLE - Haveli Approach Shot:

❌ WRONG (Causes morphing):
“Drone descends toward the haveli, approaching the entrance. Golden hour lighting. The
camera gets closer to the door.”
(Too vague! Door will change color, magical light will appear!)

✅ CORRECT (Prevents morphing):
“Drone descends toward the haveli visible in attached image.

⚠️ SUBJECT CONSISTENCY LOCK:
BUILDING (Match reference exactly): ├── Facade: Weathered peach/orange colored brick
├── Style: Colonial-era haveli, 3 stories ├── Windows: 6 visible on front, white frames
├── Balconies: 2 on upper floor with iron railings └── MUST look identical throughout
clip
DOOR (LOCKED): ├── Color: DARK BROWN wooden door (almost black with age) ├──
Frame: Stone archway with decorative molding ├── NO color change - stays dark brown
entire clip └── NO golden glow or magical lighting on door
LIGHTING: ├── Natural golden hour sunlight from upper right ├── Warm but NATURAL -
no supernatural effects ├── NO magical glow appearing around door ├── NO spotlight
effects └── Consistent lighting start to end
OBJECTS (Only these): ├── Trees on both sides (as in reference) ├── Street in foreground
├── Neighboring buildings ├── NO scooters/motorcycles (not in reference) ├── NO
people (not in reference) └── NO new objects appearing
Camera Movement: Smooth drone descent from high aerial toward the building, ending in
medium shot of facade.

⚠️ The BUILDING, DOOR, LIGHTING, and OBJECTS stay EXACTLY the same - ONLY camera
position changes!“

---

### 🚨 PHYSICALLY POSSIBLE CAMERA MOVEMENTS (CRITICAL!)

**THE PROBLEM:**
AI writes camera movements that are PHYSICALLY IMPOSSIBLE, causing Grok to
create weird, broken clips.

**EXAMPLE OF IMPOSSIBLE MOVEMENT:**

Clip 1.1 ENDS: “Wide, HIGH-ANGLE view of haveli facade” (Camera is UP HIGH, looking
down)
Clip 1.2: “From this view, camera does dolly FORWARD toward door”

❌ IMPOSSIBLE!
  •   Camera is HIGH (looking down at building)
  •   Door is at GROUND level
  •   How can camera dolly FORWARD toward something BELOW it?
  •   This creates confusion and weird AI output!

**CAMERA MOVEMENT PHYSICS:**

UNDERSTAND CAMERA POSITIONS:
HIGH ANGLE (camera above, looking down): ├── Can: Crane DOWN, Tilt down more,
Zoom in ├── Cannot: Dolly forward to ground-level subject └── To reach ground: Must
DESCEND first
EYE LEVEL (camera at normal height): ├── Can: Dolly forward/back, Pan, Truck ├──
Natural for: Walking shots, character level └── Most versatile position
LOW ANGLE (camera below, looking up): ├── Can: Crane UP, Tilt up more, Dolly forward
├── Cannot: Easily rise while pushing forward └── Best for: Power shots, grandeur

**TRANSITION BETWEEN ANGLES:**

If Clip A ends HIGH, Clip B cannot start at GROUND level moving forward!

✅ CORRECT SEQUENCE: Clip 1.1: Crane UP, ends HIGH looking at facade Clip 1.2: From
HIGH, slowly DESCEND while approaching Clip 1.3: Now at EYE LEVEL, dolly toward door
❌ WRONG SEQUENCE: Clip 1.1: Crane UP, ends HIGH looking at facade
Clip 1.2: Suddenly at ground level, dolly toward door (Camera teleported!)

---

**CAMERA MOVEMENT COMPATIBILITY CHART:**

| If Clip Ends At | Next Clip Can Start With |
|-----------------|-------------------------|
| HIGH angle (above subject) | Descend, Zoom in, Hold, Tilt |
| EYE LEVEL | Dolly, Pan, Truck, Crane, Tilt |
| LOW angle (below subject) | Rise, Zoom in, Hold, Tilt |
| CLOSE-UP | Pull back, Hold, Pan across detail |
| WIDE shot | Push in, Pan, Crane, Hold |
| Moving FORWARD | Continue forward, Slow down, Stop |
| Moving UP | Continue up, Stop, Begin descent |

---

**BEFORE WRITING ANY CLIP, ASK:**

☐ Where is camera at END of previous clip?
  •    Height: High / Eye level / Low
  •    Distance: Close / Medium / Wide
  •    Direction: Moving or Static

☐ Is my planned movement PHYSICALLY POSSIBLE from that position?
  •    Can camera realistically do this movement?
  •    Would it require teleportation?
  •    Does gravity make sense?

☐ If position change needed, do I need TRANSITION clip?
  •    Add intermediate clip to reposition camera
  •    Or describe the repositioning in current clip

---

**FIXING THE EXAMPLE:**

❌ WRONG: Clip 1.1 ends: HIGH angle, looking down at facade Clip 1.2: “Dolly forward
toward the door” (impossible from high angle!)

✅ CORRECT Anchor Image Method (Continuous descent): Clip 1.1 ends: HIGH angle view of facade Clip
1.2: “Camera begins slow descent from high position, gradually lowering while pushing
forward toward the building. By end of clip, camera is at eye level approaching the
entrance.” Clip 1.3: Now at eye level, “Dolly forward toward door”

✅ CORRECT Anchor Image Method (Stay high, zoom): Clip 1.1 ends: HIGH angle view of facade Clip 1.2:
“Maintaining high angle, camera slowly zooms in toward the doorway below, door growing
larger in frame while keeping the aerial perspective.” (Movement matches the camera
position!)

✅ CORRECT Option C (New image for cut): Clip 1.1 ends: HIGH angle view Clip 1.2:
Generate NEW IMAGE at eye level near door (This is a CUT, acceptable for establishing
sequence)

---

### VIDEO PROMPT STRUCTURE (UPDATED):

VIDEO PROMPT (Grok): Attach: [lastframe.png] (1st attached image)
The attached image shows [EXACT description of what’s visible, including camera
height/angle].

⚠️ SINGLE CONTINUOUS SHOT - NO TRANSITIONS:
  •    NO fades, NO morphs, NO scene changes
  •    Same location throughout (start to end = same building)
  •    Camera movement only, subject stays constant

⚠️ VISUAL CONTINUITY - MATCH EXACTLY: ├── Color Temperature: [X] - DO NOT SHIFT
├── Brightness: [X] - MAINTAIN ├── Contrast: [X] - NO CHANGE └── Location: SAME as
attached - NO CHANGE
CAMERA POSITION IN ATTACHED IMAGE:
  •    Height: [High/Eye level/Low]
  •    Distance: [Close/Medium/Wide]
  •    Angle: [Looking up/straight/down]
CAMERA MOVEMENT (Physically possible from above position): [Describe movement that
makes sense from the starting position]
[Narration/Dialogue if any]
The shot ends with: [Description - same location, different framing]
6 seconds, 16:9, [lighting].

---
### ⚠️ PREVENTING SUBTLE JUMPS (Camera Pan Endings)

**THE SUBTLE PROBLEM:**

Even when camera movement is described correctly, the ENDING can cause jumps:

❌ EXAMPLE OF SUBTLE JUMP:
Clip 1.5 ENDS: “Medium close-up on desk surface - inkwell, papers” Camera was focused on
DESK
Clip 1.6: “Starting from desk, camera pans to bookshelves…” ENDS: “Close-up on SPECIFIC
SECTION of bookshelf”
PROBLEM:
  •   Start is fine (desk → pan to shelves)
  •   BUT ending jumps to “specific section” - WHICH section?!
  •   Grok randomly picks a section
  •   Result: Feels like a jump because it’s uncontrolled

---

### THE ISSUE: Vague End Points

When you write endings like:
- "Close-up on a specific section of bookshelf"
- "Focus on a particular area"
- "Camera settles on some books"

Grok will RANDOMLY choose where to end → Next clip won't match!

---

### SOLUTION: Precise Spatial End Points

**Every "shot ends with" must specify EXACTLY:**
1. **WHAT is in frame** (specific objects)
2. **WHERE in frame** (left, right, center, top, bottom)
3. **HOW MUCH of frame** (fills frame, occupies third, etc.)
4. **SPATIAL RELATION** to previous elements

---

### CORRECT WAY - Spatial Continuity:
Clip 1.5: “Camera dollies toward desk…” ENDS: “Medium close-up on desk - brass inkwell in
center-left of frame, scattered papers filling lower half, edge of bookshelf VISIBLE on the
right side of frame (this bookshelf will be featured in next clip).”
Clip 1.6: “Starting from the view in attached image where bookshelf edge is visible on the
right, camera pans RIGHT toward that bookshelf…” ENDS: “The dark mahogany bookshelf
section now fills the frame - specifically the MIDDLE SHELF at approximately eye level,
showing a row of dark leather books with gold detailing. The shelf is framed with partial
view of shelves above and below.”

**NOW Clip 1.7 knows EXACTLY which shelf section to continue from!**

---

### END FRAME PRECISION CHECKLIST:

☐ Is the ending SPECIFIC? (Not “a section” but “the middle shelf at eye level”) ☐ Is the
LOCATION defined? (center/left/right of frame) ☐ Is the RELATIONSHIP to next clip clear?
☐ Could another AI generate the SAME ending frame from this description? ☐ Is there
CONTINUITY HINT? (edge of next focus area visible)

---

### EXAMPLES - Vague vs Precise Endings:

| Vague ❌ | Precise ✅ |
|----------|-----------|
| "Focus on bookshelf" | "Middle shelf section at eye level, showing row of
6-7 dark books, fills center of frame" |
| "Close-up on books" | "Three leather-bound books with visible gold spine
patterns, stacked horizontally, filling lower two-thirds of frame" |
| "Camera settles on desk" | "Desk surface occupying lower half of frame,
brass inkwell in center, papers scattered around it, bookshelf visible in
background" |
| "Ends on a specific area" | "The third shelf from top, far-left section
near the window, showing books bathed in golden light" |
| "Window in view" | "Tall arched window occupying right third of frame,
curtain edge visible, light beam diagonal across center" |

---

### SPATIAL HINTS FOR NEXT CLIP:

**Always include the NEXT clip's subject at the EDGE of current clip's
ending:**

Clip 1.5 will focus on DESK, Clip 1.6 will focus on BOOKSHELF:
Clip 1.5 ending: “…desk surface with inkwell and papers. The EDGE of the bookshelf is
visible in the right portion of frame, hinting at the vast collection beyond.”
Now Clip 1.6 can naturally pan RIGHT to that bookshelf!


Clip 1.6 will focus on BOOKSHELF, Clip 1.7 will show LOCKED CAGE:
Clip 1.6 ending: “…row of books filling frame. In the lower-right corner, barely visible, the
edge of a BRASS STRUCTURE can be seen - something different from the regular shelves.”
Now Clip 1.7 can pan/tilt to reveal the cage!

---

### THE GOLDEN RULE FOR ENDINGS:

**The LAST FRAME of each clip should contain a HINT of what comes next.**

This creates:
- Visual continuity (element already in frame)
- Smooth camera movement (moving toward something visible)
- No "appearing from nowhere" objects
- Professional, cinematic flow

---

### ⚠️ 6-SECOND CLIP LIMITS (VERY IMPORTANT!)

**A single 6-second clip can only contain LIMITED action!**

**THE PROBLEM:**
AI often writes prompts that try to fit 30 seconds of action into 6 seconds:

❌ IMPOSSIBLE PROMPT (Too much for 6 seconds):
“Starting from close-up on book spines, the camera pulls back and down, settling into
medium-wide shot. Camera repositions behind the desk. Zain enters from doorway, pauses,
looks around with awe, his expression showing melancholy…”
In 6 seconds you CANNOT:

  •    Pull back from close-up ✗
  •    Move down ✗
  •    Settle into medium-wide ✗
  •    Reposition behind desk ✗
  •    Show character enter ✗
  •   Show character pause ✗
  •   Show character expression ✗
THIS NEEDS 4-5 CLIPS, NOT 1!

---

### WHAT CAN FIT IN 6 SECONDS:

**ONE of these:**
- A single camera movement (pan OR dolly OR zoom - not all)
- A character performing ONE simple action
- A transition from one shot size to the NEXT size (close→medium, not
close→wide)
- A character entering OR reacting (not both with detail)

---

### CAMERA MOVEMENT LIMITS (Per 6-Second Clip):

| Movement Type | What's Possible in 6 Seconds |
|---------------|------------------------------|
| **Dolly In/Out** | Move ONE shot size (wide→medium OR medium→close) |
| **Pan** | 30-45 degrees maximum, not full room sweep |
| **Tilt** | Up OR down, not both |
| **Static** | Can show more character action |
| **Complex** | Only if movement is the ONLY thing happening (no action, no
dialogue) |

---

### ACTION LIMITS (Per 6-Second Clip):

| Clip Type | What Can Happen |
|-----------|-----------------|
| **Pure Visual** | One camera movement OR one atmospheric moment |
| **With Narration** | Minimal camera movement (slow pan/static) |
| **Character Entrance** | Character enters and stops, OR enters and one
reaction |
| **Dialogue** | One short exchange (2-3 sentences max) |
| **Reveal** | ONE thing revealed, not multiple |

---

### BREAKING DOWN COMPLEX ACTIONS:

**Example - Character Entering Room:**
❌ WRONG (One clip trying to do everything): “Camera pulls back from books, repositions
behind desk, Zain enters, pauses, looks around, shows awe and melancholy…”

✅ CORRECT (Split into multiple clips):
Clip 2.5: Camera pulls back from book close-up, revealing more of room, doorway
becoming visible in background. ENDS: Medium shot, doorway visible in center-back.
Clip 2.6: Static medium shot. In the doorway, a shadow appears - someone approaching.
Anticipation builds. ENDS: Silhouette of person in doorway.
Clip 2.7: Zain (description) steps through doorway into the room. He takes two steps and
stops, overwhelmed. ENDS: Zain just inside doorway, pausing.
Clip 2.8: Close-up on Zain’s face. His expression shifts from curiosity to awe to melancholy
as he takes in the space. NARRATION plays over this reaction shot. ENDS: Zain’s emotional
face, room reflected in his eyes.

**4 clips instead of 1 = Smooth, cinematic, believable!**

---

### THE 6-SECOND RULE:

**Ask yourself: Can a REAL camera and REAL actor do this in 6 seconds?**

- Camera moves from books to behind desk in 6 seconds? **NO**
- Character enters, pauses, looks around, emotes in 6 seconds? **NO**
- Simple dolly forward while character enters? **YES**
- Static shot of character's reaction with narration? **YES**

---

### PROMPT COMPLEXITY CHECKLIST:

Before finalizing any video prompt, count:

☐ How many CAMERA MOVEMENTS? (Should be 0-1) ☐ How many CHARACTER
ACTIONS? (Should be 0-2 simple ones) ☐ How many LOCATION CHANGES? (Should be 0)
☐ How many NEW ELEMENTS appearing? (Should be 0-1) ☐ Is there DIALOGUE? (If yes,
minimize action) ☐ Is there NARRATION? (If yes, camera should be simple)
If any count is HIGH → Split into multiple clips!

---

### CAMERA POSITION REALITY:
**Camera CANNOT teleport between positions!**

❌ WRONG: Clip 1: Camera at BOOKSHELVES (front of room) Clip 2: Camera BEHIND
DESK (center of room)
How did camera get there?!

✅ CORRECT: Clip 1: Camera at bookshelves, begins pulling back Clip 2: Camera continues
back, now near center of room Clip 3: Camera settles into position with desk in foreground

**OR use a CUT with NEW IMAGE if camera position must change dramatically.**

---

**THE PROBLEM:**
AI writes "desk visible in last frame" but doesn't consider WHAT LEVEL OF
DETAIL is visible at that distance!

**REAL FAILURE EXAMPLE:**

Clip 1.3 ENDS: “Wide view of room, desk JUST VISIBLE in center” (Desk is tiny - maybe 5%
of frame)
Clip 1.4: “Desk covered with manuscripts, inkwell, magnifying glass, scattered papers,
chairs visible…”

❌ IMPOSSIBLE! In a wide room shot, you CANNOT see:
  •    Inkwell (tiny object)
  •    Magnifying glass (tiny object)
  •    Scattered papers (small details)
  •    Chairs (might be visible but not detailed)
These objects APPEAR OUT OF NOWHERE when camera gets close!

---

### VISIBILITY BY CAMERA DISTANCE:

| Shot Type | Frame Coverage | What's Visible | What's NOT Visible |
|-----------|---------------|----------------|-------------------|
| **Extreme Wide** | Entire room/location | Walls, large furniture shapes,
windows, doors | Any details, small objects, textures, text |
| **Wide Shot** | Most of room | Furniture (desk, shelves, chairs as shapes),
windows, major features | Objects ON furniture, small items, any text |
| **Medium Wide** | Half room / area | Furniture details, chair shapes, large
objects on surfaces (as vague shapes) | Small objects, papers, tools,
textures, text |
| **Medium Shot** | Furniture group | Chair details, objects on desk (general
shapes only - "cluttered surface"), books (as mass of color) | Individual
small items clearly, text, paper details |
| **Medium Close** | Single furniture | Objects on desk becoming
distinguishable (shapes of items), book spines as rectangles | Text readable,
very small items clear |
| **Close-up** | Object/detail | All details of that object, textures, small
items clear | N/A |
| **Extreme Close-up** | Tiny detail | Texture, specific simple text (if
described), micro details | Everything else |

---

### ⚠️ STRICT VISIBILITY ENFORCEMENT:

**THE REMAINING PROBLEM:**
AI still writes things like:

Clip 1.4: “Desk visible as large dark shape in wide shot” Clip 1.5: “Brass inkwell, magnifying
glass, scattered manuscripts visible”

❌ WRONG! Inkwell is 3 inches tall - CANNOT be visible when desk is just a “large dark
shape”!

**THE FIX - DESCRIBE WHAT'S ACTUALLY VISIBLE AT EACH STAGE:**

WIDE SHOT (desk is small in frame): ✅ “A large dark wooden desk dominates the center
of the room” ❌ “Desk with inkwell, papers, magnifying glass” (TOO DETAILED!)

MEDIUM WIDE (desk is larger but still not close): ✅ “The desk surface appears cluttered
with various shapes and objects” ❌ “Brass inkwell and magnifying glass visible” (STILL
TOO DETAILED!)

MEDIUM SHOT (approaching desk): ✅ “Objects on the desk are becoming distinguishable
- rectangular shapes that appear to be papers, some metallic items catching the light” ❌
“The brass inkwell has a tarnished finish” (TOO SPECIFIC!)

MEDIUM CLOSE (close to desk): ✅ “Now visible: a brass inkwell, scattered yellowed
papers, a magnifying glass with wooden handle” (NOW this level of detail is appropriate!)

CLOSE-UP: ✅ “The brass inkwell shows green tarnish and intricate engraving around its
rim” (Maximum detail now visible)

---

### OBJECT VISIBILITY PROGRESSION TABLE:
| Object | Wide | Med Wide | Medium | Med Close | Close-up |
|--------|------|----------|--------|-----------|----------|
| **Desk** | "dark shape" | "wooden desk" | "carved desk" | "mahogany
details" | "grain texture" |
| **Books** | "colored wall" | "rows of spines" | "individual books" |
"leather texture" | "embossed pattern" |
| **Inkwell** | NOT visible | NOT visible | "metallic shape" | "brass
inkwell" | "tarnished details" |
| **Papers** | NOT visible | NOT visible | "white/cream shapes" | "scattered
papers" | "handwriting visible" |
| **Magnifying glass** | NOT visible | NOT visible | "glint of glass" |
"magnifying glass" | "scratches on lens" |
| **Candle** | NOT visible | NOT visible | "tall shape" | "candle in holder"
| "wax drip details" |
| **Text on books** | NOT visible | NOT visible | NOT visible | "faded
lettering" | "illegible script" |

---

### PRACTICAL APPLICATION:

**When writing a sequence approaching an object:**

Clip X (Wide): “The study room with a large desk visible in the center, its surface appearing
dark and cluttered with undefined shapes.”
Clip X+1 (Medium Wide): “Moving closer, the desk reveals itself as ornate carved
mahogany. Various objects create a cluttered silhouette on its surface, catching occasional
glints of golden light.”
Clip X+2 (Medium): “The desk surface comes into focus. Rectangular shapes suggest papers
and documents. Something metallic reflects the light. The clutter hints at years of research.”
Clip X+3 (Medium Close): “Now clearly visible: a brass inkwell sits beside scattered
yellowed manuscripts. A magnifying glass with a dark wooden handle rests on an open
book. Papers curl at their edges.”
Clip X+4 (Close-up): “Extreme detail on the brass inkwell - green patina of age, intricate
floral engravings around the rim, dried ink staining its interior.”

**This is CORRECT progressive revelation!**

---

### THE GOLDEN RULE OF VISIBILITY:

**Objects should GRADUALLY REVEAL as camera approaches!**
❌ WRONG: Wide shot (desk tiny) → Medium shot (suddenly inkwell, papers, magnifying
glass visible) Objects APPEAR from nowhere!

✅ CORRECT: Wide shot → “desk visible as dark shape in center” Medium wide → “desk
shape clearer, something on surface visible” Medium → “objects on desk becoming
distinguishable - papers, dark shapes” Medium close → “inkwell now visible, papers
scattered, magnifying glass” Close-up → “brass inkwell detail, paper text partially readable”

---

### OBJECT INTRODUCTION BY DISTANCE:

When introducing NEW objects that will be important later:

**Step 1 - HINT (Wide/Medium Wide):**

“…a large desk in the center, its surface cluttered with undefined dark shapes and papers…”

**Step 2 - SUGGESTION (Medium):**

“…the desk surface reveals scattered items - what appears to be papers, some metallic
objects catching the light…”

**Step 3 - REVEAL (Medium Close):**

“…now visible: a brass inkwell, scattered manuscripts, an old magnifying glass, papers with
handwritten text…”

**Step 4 - DETAIL (Close-up):**

“…the brass inkwell is tarnished with age, the magnifying glass has a carved wooden
handle, the papers show faded Urdu script…”

---

# 🧠 STORY CONTINUITY & LOGIC TRACKING SYSTEM

╔══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
════════╗ ║ ║ ║ 🚨 MOST IMPORTANT SECTION - READ CAREFULLY! 🚨 ║ ║ ║ ║ AI
generates individual prompts but FORGETS story logic! ║ ║ You MUST track and maintain
continuity YOURSELF! ║ ║ ║ ║ Every prompt must reflect CUMULATIVE story state! ║ ║ ║
╚══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
════════╝
---

## 🏠 TIME-BASED ENVIRONMENT AGING

╔══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
════════╗ ║ ║ ║ RULE: Same location at different times = MUST show aging! ║ ║ ║ ║
1858 haveli → 2024 haveli = 166 years of wear! ║ ║ New house → 50 years later =
Deterioration visible! ║ ║ ║
╚══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
════════╝

### AGING PROGRESSION TABLE:

TIME GAP │ VISIBLE CHANGES REQUIRED
──────────────────┼────────────────────────────────────────
────── 10-20 years │ Paint fading, minor cracks, dust accumulation, │ furniture slightly
worn, colors muted 10-15% │ 50 years │ Major paint peeling, visible cracks in walls, │
broken furniture, cobwebs, dust layers, │ colors faded 30-40%, wood warped │ 100+ years
│ Walls crumbling, ceiling damaged, plants │ growing through cracks, complete color fade,
│ structural damage, rubble, decay smell implied │ 200+ years │ Ruins, partial collapse,
nature reclaiming, │ moss/vines covering surfaces, barely │ recognizable as original
structure

### EXAMPLE - SAME HAVELI:

═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
════════ SCENE 1 (1858) - Haveli in its PRIME:
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
════════
IMAGE PROMPT: “Grand Kashmiri haveli exterior, 1858. PRISTINE condition: walls are
smooth white-washed, intricate wooden jharokhas (balconies) freshly painted in deep red
and gold, all shutters intact and properly aligned, carved wooden door with brass fittings
POLISHED and gleaming, courtyard tiles perfectly laid with no cracks, manicured garden
visible, oil lamps burning in every window, wealthy prosperous feel. Colors VIBRANT and
SATURATED.”
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
════════ SCENE 5 (2024) - SAME Haveli, 166 years later:
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
════════
IMAGE PROMPT: “The SAME Kashmiri haveli exterior, NOW in 2024 - showing 166 YEARS
of aging and neglect. White-wash is 80% PEELED revealing grey stone beneath. Wooden
jharokhas are FADED to dull brown, paint cracked and flaking, several wooden pieces
MISSING or broken. Shutters CROOKED, some hanging by single hinge. The carved door’s
brass fittings are GREEN with oxidation, wood CRACKED and swollen. Courtyard tiles
CRACKED with weeds growing through gaps. Garden is OVERGROWN wilderness. No lights
in windows - dark and abandoned feel. Colors DESATURATED and MUTED (40% of original
vibrancy). Bird nests in corners, water damage stains visible.”
KEY DIFFERENCES EXPLICITLY STATED: ├── Whitewash: PRISTINE → 80% PEELED ├──
Wood: FRESH PAINT → FADED, CRACKED, MISSING pieces ├── Brass: POLISHED →
GREEN oxidation ├── Tiles: PERFECT → CRACKED with weeds ├── Garden: MANICURED
→ OVERGROWN wilderness ├── Lights: EVERY window → NONE, abandoned ├── Colors:
VIBRANT → DESATURATED 40% └── Feel: WEALTHY → ABANDONED, decaying

---

## 🏚️ ABANDONED LOCATION LOGIC

╔══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
════════╗ ║ ║ ║ If story says “No one has been here for X years” ║ ║ The location
MUST show X years of abandonment! ║ ║ ║
╚══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
════════╝

### ABANDONMENT SIGNS BY DURATION:

1-5 YEARS ABANDONED: ├── Thick dust on all surfaces (describe layer depth) ├──
Cobwebs in corners and on furniture ├── Stale, musty atmosphere ├── Faded curtains,
sun-bleached areas ├── Small animal evidence (droppings, nests) ├── Dead insects on
windowsills └── Furniture untouched but dusty
10-20 YEARS ABANDONED: ├── All above PLUS: ├── Ceiling water stains and peeling
├── Mold patches on walls ├── Broken windows (weather damage) ├── Collapsed
smaller furniture ├── Plants growing inside through cracks ├── Significant structural
cracks └── Smell of rot and decay implied
50+ YEARS ABANDONED: ├── All above PLUS: ├── Partial roof collapse ├── Walls
crumbling ├── Floor unsafe, holes visible ├── Nature fully reclaiming (vines, trees) ├──
Barely recognizable original features ├── Wildlife living inside └── Dangerous to enter

### EXAMPLE - LIBRARY ABANDONED 30 YEARS:

❌ WRONG (Ignores abandonment): “Beautiful old library with wooden bookshelves,
leather chairs, and warm lighting…”

✅ CORRECT (Shows 30 years abandonment): “Library abandoned for 30 YEARS shows
severe neglect: bookshelves WARPED and tilted, several COLLAPSED entirely with books
scattered on floor. Leather chairs CRACKED and TORN, stuffing exposed, covered in 30
years of DUST (thick grey layer visible on every surface). Cobwebs DENSE across corners
and between shelves - undisturbed for decades. Ceiling has WATER DAMAGE, plaster
falling, brown stains spreading. Windows BROKEN, letting in weather - rain damage visible
on nearby books (pages SWOLLEN, covers MOLDY). Light comes through dirty, cracked
glass - creating dusty beams. Floor has DEBRIS and fallen plaster pieces. Smell of MOLD and
ROT implied by green-black patches on walls. A bird’s nest visible on top shelf. Nature
beginning to reclaim - small plant growing through floor crack.”

---

## 🤕 INJURY & DAMAGE CONTINUITY

╔══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
════════╗ ║ ║ ║ RULE: Injuries/damage PERSIST until explicitly healed! ║ ║ ║ ║
Character gets cut → EVERY subsequent clip shows wound ║ ║ Object breaks → Stays
broken until shown being fixed ║ ║ Clothes torn → Remain torn until changed ║ ║ ║
╚══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
════════╝

### INJURY TRACKING SYSTEM:

WHEN INJURY OCCURS, CREATE MENTAL LOG:
INJURY LOG: ├── Clip [X.X]: [Character] received [injury type] ├── Location: [Where on
body] ├── Severity: [Minor/Moderate/Severe] ├── Visual signs: [Blood, bruise, swelling,
etc.] ├── Healing timeline: [How many clips until healed] └── Status:
[Active/Healing/Healed]
EVERY SUBSEQUENT CLIP MUST INCLUDE: “[Character] still shows [injury description]
from earlier”
HEALING PROGRESSION: ├── Fresh wound (0-2 clips): Bright red blood, raw ├── Recent
(3-5 clips): Dried blood, starting to scab ├── Healing (6-10 clips): Scabbed over, bruising
yellow ├── Nearly healed (11-15 clips): Scar forming, faded └── Healed (16+ clips): Scar
only, or fully healed

### INJURY EXAMPLES:

═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
════════ CLIP 3.4: Character gets cut on forehead
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
════════
“…the branch CUTS across his forehead, leaving a 2-inch GASH above his right eyebrow.
FRESH BLOOD immediately wells up and begins trickling down toward his eye…”
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
════════ CLIP 3.5 (Immediately after):
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
════════
CHARACTER LOCK MUST INCLUDE: “INJURY: Fresh 2-inch cut above RIGHT eyebrow,
blood actively trickling down face, has reached cheek level”
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
════════ CLIP 3.8 (4 clips later - maybe 2 minutes story time):
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
════════
CHARACTER LOCK MUST INCLUDE: “INJURY: 2-inch cut above RIGHT eyebrow, blood now
DRIED and crusted, brown streak down face from earlier bleeding”
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
════════ CLIP 4.3 (Next scene - maybe 1 hour story time):
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
════════
CHARACTER LOCK MUST INCLUDE: “INJURY: Cut above RIGHT eyebrow now SCABBED
over, dark red-brown, slight bruising around wound turning yellow”
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
════════ CLIP 6.1 (2 scenes later - maybe 1 day story time):
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
════════
CHARACTER LOCK MUST INCLUDE: “INJURY: Former cut above RIGHT eyebrow now a thin
PINK SCAR, scab has fallen off, healing well”

## 📦 OBJECT STATE TRACKING

╔══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
════════╗ ║ ║ ║ Every object has a STATE that must be tracked! ║ ║ ║ ║ Book: Closed
→ Opened → Closed (must show each change!) ║ ║ Vase: Intact → Cracked → Shattered
(progressive!) ║ ║ Torch: Lit → Flickering → Dying → Extinguished ║ ║ ║
╚══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
════════╝

### OBJECT STATE LOG:

MAINTAIN THIS LOG THROUGHOUT PRODUCTION:
OBJECT: Ancient Book ├── Clip 1.1-1.3: CLOSED (clasp secured) ├── Clip 1.4: Being
OPENED (show clasp being released) ├── Clip 1.5-2.3: OPEN (pages visible) ├── Clip 2.4:
Being CLOSED (show pages coming together) ├── Clip 2.5+: CLOSED again └── CURRENT
STATE: [Update after each clip]
OBJECT: Character’s Lantern ├── Clip 1.1-3.5: LIT (bright orange flame) ├── Clip 3.6-3.7:
FLICKERING (low oil, flame unstable) ├── Clip 3.8: DYING (flame very small, dim) ├──
Clip 3.9: EXTINGUISHED (smoke wisping, dark) ├── Clip 4.1: Being RELIT (striking match)
├── Clip 4.2+: LIT again (fresh flame) └── CURRENT STATE: [Update after each clip]

### DAMAGE PROGRESSION:

OBJECT DAMAGE MUST BE PROGRESSIVE, NOT INSTANT:
VASE BREAKING: ├── State 1: INTACT (perfect condition) ├── State 2: CRACKED (visible
crack, still whole) ├── State 3: SHATTERED (pieces falling, mid-break) ├── State 4:
BROKEN (pieces on ground) └── Cannot skip from State 1 to State 4!
SHOW EACH TRANSITION: ├── Clip shows vase CRACKING (impact moment) ├── Next
clip shows vase SHATTERING (falling apart) ├── Next clip shows BROKEN pieces on
ground └── Every future clip: Broken pieces remain visible!

---

## 👔 CLOTHING & APPEARANCE CONTINUITY

╔══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
════════╗ ║ ║ ║ Clothes don’t magically change! Track wear and tear! ║ ║ ║ ║
Running through thorns → Clothes get torn ║ ║ Falling in mud → Clothes stay muddy until
changed ║ ║ Sweating → Sweat stains visible until dried ║ ║ ║
╚══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
════════╝

### CLOTHING STATE TRACKING:

INITIAL STATE (Scene 1): “Clean white shirt, pressed dark trousers, polished shoes”
AFTER RUNNING (Scene 2): “White shirt now has SWEAT STAINS under arms and on back,
trousers dusty at hem, shoes scuffed”
AFTER FALL IN MUD (Scene 3): “White shirt has MUD SPLATTER on left side and sleeve,
sweat stains still visible, trousers have MUD on knees and thighs, shoes caked in mud”
AFTER STRUGGLE (Scene 4): “White shirt now TORN at right shoulder (2-inch tear), MUD
still present (slightly dried, brown now), sweat stains dried into salt marks, trousers torn at
knee revealing skin beneath, one shoe missing shoelace”
───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
── THIS MUST BE IN CHARACTER LOCK FOR EVERY SUBSEQUENT CLIP!
───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
──
CHARACTER LOCK (Scene 4+): ├── CLOTHING DAMAGE: │ ├── Shirt: Torn at RIGHT
shoulder (2-inch), mud on │ │ left side (dried brown), sweat salt marks │ ├── Trousers:
Torn at LEFT knee, mud stains on thighs │ └── Shoes: Right shoe missing lace, both caked
in │ dried mud └── “All damage from previous scenes MUST be visible”

---

## 🌦️ WEATHER & TIME CONTINUITY

╔══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
════════╗ ║ ║ ║ Weather and time must be CONSISTENT within scenes! ║ ║ ║ ║
Night scene → Cannot suddenly be daylight ║ ║ Raining outside → Characters entering
should be WET ║ ║ Winter scene → Breath should be visible (cold) ║ ║ ║
╚══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
════════╝

### WEATHER LOGIC RULES:

IF RAINING OUTSIDE: ├── Characters coming from outside = WET (dripping hair, │
soaked clothes, water on face) ├── Windows show rain streaks ├── Sound of rain implied
├── Puddles near doors └── Umbrella/rainwear if prepared
IF SNOWING: ├── Characters from outside = Snow on shoulders, hair ├── Cheeks and
nose RED from cold ├── Breath VISIBLE (cold vapor) ├── Shivering body language └──
Snow melting = wet patches forming
IF HOT DAY: ├── Characters = Sweating visibly ├── Squinting in bright light ├── Seeking
shade ├── Fanning themselves └── Heat shimmer in distance (exterior)
TIME OF DAY CONSISTENCY: ├── Morning light = Warm, golden, from East ├── Midday =
Harsh overhead, short shadows ├── Evening = Warm orange, long shadows from West
├── Night = Moon/artificial light only └── CANNOT change within a scene!

---

╔══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
════════╗ ║ ║ ║ BEFORE WRITING EACH CLIP, REVIEW THIS TRACKER! ║ ║ ║ ║
Maintain this information throughout entire production: ║ ║ ║
╚══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
════════╝
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
════════ MASTER CONTINUITY TRACKER - UPDATE AFTER EACH CLIP
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
════════

📅 TIMELINE: ├── Current story date/year: [1858 / 2024 / etc.] ├── Current time of day:
[Morning / Afternoon / Night] ├── Time since scene start: [0 min / 5 min / 1 hour] └──
Weather: [Clear / Rain / Snow / etc.]

📍 LOCATION: ├── Current location: [Haveli / Cave / Street] ├── Location age/state: [New
/ 50 years old / Abandoned] ├── Location damage: [None / Partial / Severe] └── Changes
since start: [List any changes]

├── Physical state: [Fresh / Tired / Exhausted / Injured] ├── Current position: [Where in
location] └── Holding: [Objects in hands]

👤 CHARACTER 2 - [Name]: ├── [Same as above]

📦 TRACKED OBJECTS: ├── Book: [Closed / Open / Damaged] ├── Lantern: [Lit / Dim /
Extinguished] ├── [Object 3]: [State] └── [Object 4]: [State]

🏠 ENVIRONMENT STATES: ├── Door: [Open % / Closed / Broken] ├── Windows: [Intact
/ Cracked / Broken] ├── Furniture: [Intact / Displaced / Damaged] └── Lighting sources:
[List what’s on/off]

⚠️ PENDING CHANGES: ├── [Injury that will heal in X clips] ├── [Object that will break
in Scene Y] └── [Weather change coming in Scene Z]

## 🎬 APPLYING CONTINUITY TO PROMPTS

### EVERY PROMPT MUST CHECK:

BEFORE WRITING ANY PROMPT, ASK:

☐ What is the current story timeline? → Does environment show correct aging?

☐ What injuries exist from previous clips? → Are they ALL mentioned in character lock?

☐ What is the current state of ALL tracked objects? → Are they described correctly?

☐ What clothing damage exists? → Is it ALL visible in description?
☐ What is the weather/time of day? → Is it consistent with previous clip?

☐ What happened in the last clip? → Does this clip follow LOGICALLY?

☐ Has anything changed since scene start? → Is the change reflected?


### PROMPT STRUCTURE WITH CONTINUITY:

═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
═════════ 🎬 VIDEO PROMPT - Clip [X.X]
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
═════════
[HOOK LINE]
───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
───── CONTINUITY STATE (From tracker):
───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
───── Timeline: [Story date, time of day] Location state: [Current condition with aging
noted] Weather: [Current weather, consistent with scene]
───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
───── CHARACTER LOCK (Including ALL accumulated states):
───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
───── • FACE: [Base description] • HEAD: [Base description] • CLOTHING: [Base + ALL
DAMAGE from previous clips] ├── Shirt: [Original + tears/stains/wet] ├── Pants:
[Original + damage] └── Shoes: [Original + damage] • INJURIES: [ALL active injuries with
current healing stage] ├── [Injury 1]: [Location, current appearance] └── [Injury 2]:
[Location, current appearance] • PHYSICAL STATE: [Tired level, sweat, dirt, etc.]
───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
───── OBJECT STATES (From tracker):
───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
───── • Book: [CLOSED/OPEN] - [any damage] • Lantern: [LIT/DIM/OFF] - [oil level
implied] • [Other objects]: [States]
───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
───── ACTION (6 seconds):
───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
───── [Action description that respects all continuity states]
───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
───── ENDS WITH (Update tracker after this):
───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
───── • Character position: [New position] • Any NEW damage: [If applicable] • Object
state changes: [If any] • [What to add to tracker for next clip]
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
═════════
